p. 1)

śrī

lakṣmītantram

prathamo'dhyāyaḥ

namo nityānavadyāya jagataḥ sarvahetave |
jñānāya nistaraṅgāya lakṣmīnārāyaṇātmane [B, c: 
tuṣārādrisamacchāyaṃ tulasīdāmabhūṣaṇam | turaṃgamamukhaṃ vande 
turaṅgasārasvatapradam || jñānānandamayaṃ devaṃ nirmalasphaṭikākṛtim | 
ādhāraṃ sarvavidyānāṃ hayagrīvamupāsmahe || || 1 ||

tippaṇī 

namasyāmaḥ śriyaṃ devīṃ devo dīvyati yatsakhaḥ |
traiguṇyaphalake citre kṣiptairbrahmādipāśakaiḥ ||

lakṣmītantramahāmbhodhau tattvaratnāni cinvatām |
tattvaprakāśanī ṭīkā sāhāyyaṃ kartumīhate ||

1. nama iti viśiṣṭopāyasya lakṣmīnārāyaṇātmana iti viśiṣṭopeyasya ca nirdeśaḥ | 
nityānavadyāyeti nārāyaṇasya jagadupādānatvaśaṅkitasavikāratva-nirāsaḥ 
baddhamuktajīvavailakṣaṇyaṃ coktam | jagataḥ sarvahetave 
ityaupaniṣadamabhinnanimittopādānatvaṃ nityajīvavailakṣaṇyaṃ coktam | 
jñānāyeti jagatkāraṇatvaupayikaguṇagaṇapūrtiḥ svarūpanirūpakadharmaścoktaḥ 
| nistaraṅgāyeti ṣaḍūrmirāhityaṃ satatapariṇāmyacidvailakṣaṇyaṃ coktam |

khagāsanaṃ [āll ṃss: ṛṇādhāraṃ] ghṛṇādhāramīdṛśaṃ 
somabhūṣitam |
akalaṅkendusūryāgniṃ lakṣmīrūpamupāsmahe || 2 ||

2. īdṛśamityādi | īkārarūpamityarthaḥ | akalaṅketyādi | 
svarasparśavyāpakākṣararūpamiti devyā vāksvarūpatvamuktaṃ bhavati | yathoktaṃ 
prapañcasāre - svarākhyāḥ ṣoḍaśa proktāḥ sparśākhyāḥ pañcaviṃśatiḥ 
| vyāpakāśca daśaite syuḥ somenāgnyātmakāḥ kramāt || iti |

vedavedānta [ḍ, E, ī: vedāṅga] tattvajñaṃ 
sarvaśāstraviśāradam |
sarvasiddhāntatattvajñaṃ dharmāṇāmāgatāgamam || 3 ||

jitendriyaṃ jitādhāraṃ rāgadveṣāvaśīkṛtam |
caturdaśāṅgayogasthaṃ prasaṃkhyānaparāyaṇam || 4 ||

4. caturdaśeti | bhaktinyāsayogetyarthaḥ |

p. 2)

viddhe svarbhānunā bhānau purā tapanatāṃ gatam |
nidānaṃ tapasāmādyaṃ tejorāśimanāmayam || 5 ||

5. viddha iti | atra mahābhāratānuśāsanike 260 adhyāyoktamanusaṃdheyam |

atrimatriguṇopetamatrivargasthamavyayam |
prātaḥ saṃdhyāmupāsīnamṛṣiṃ hutahutāśanam || 6 ||

6. atriguṇetyādinā maharṣeratrināmno'nvarthatvaṃ vyajyate |

pativratānāṃ paramā dharmapatnī yaśasvinī |
brahmaviṣṇumaheśānāṃ jananī kāraṇāntare || 7 ||

7. brahmetyādi | purā trimūrtayo'nasūyāyāḥ pātivratyaṃ parīkṣitumayatanta | tena 
kruddhayā tayā te dvihāyanāḥ śiśavo'kriyanta | tatastanmahiṣībhiḥ prasāditā sā 
tān yathāpuramakaroditi paurāṇikī kathātrānusaṃdheyā |

devairabhiṣṭatā śaśvacchāntinityā tapasvinī |
viduṣī sarvadharmajñā nityaṃ patimanuvratā || 8 ||

patyuḥ śrutavatī tāstā vividhā dharmasaṃhitāḥ |
praṇipātapuraskāramanasūyā vaco'bravīt || 9 ||

anasūyā -

bhagavan sarvadharmajña mama nātha jagatpate |
tvatta eva śrutā dharmāste te bahuvidhātmakāḥ || 10 ||

jñānāni ca vicitrāṇi phalasvarūpādibhedataḥ |
etebhyo bhagavaddharmo viśiṣṭo vidhṛto mayā || 11 ||

11. bhagavaddharmaḥ | bhagavatprāptiphalo nivṛttidharmaḥ |

tvayā kathayatā tāstā bhagavaddharmasaṃhitāḥ  [B, c: bhagavan##-
sūcitaṃ tatra tatraiva lakṣmīmāhātmyamuttamam || 12 ||

rahasyatvādapṛṣṭatvānna tvayā prakaṭīkṛtam |
tadahaṃ śrotumicchāmi lakṣmīmāhātmyamuttamam || 13 ||

13. apṛṣṭatvāditi | nāpṛṣṭaḥ kasyacid brūyāt iti vidhiratrābhipretaḥ |

yatsvabhāvā hi sā devī yatsvarūpā yadudbhavā |
yatpramāṇā yadādhārā yadupāyātha [c, E: ca] yatphalā || 14 ||

tadahaṃ śrotumicchāmi tvatto brahmavidāṃ vara |
bhaveyaṃ kṛtakṛtyāhaṃ yasya vijñānayogataḥ || 15 ||

taṃ me [B, ṅ: tanme] darśaya panthānamupasannāsmyadhīhi 
[c, E: smyahaṃ vibho] bho |
[B, c, E: omit this line] iti tasyā vacaḥ śrutvā 
bhagavānatrirabravīta || 16 ||

16. upasanneti | tasmai sa vidvānupasannāya samyak provāca tāṃ tattvato 
brahmavidyām iti śrūtyartho'bhipretaḥ | panthānamiti | sadupāyamityarthaḥ | 
mahājano yena gataḥ sa panthāḥ itivat |

atri - 

sādhu [c, ḍ, E, ṅ: samyak] saṃbodhito'smyadya dharmajñe 
dharmacāriṇi |
mayā pṛṣṭena vaktavyamiti noddhāṭitaṃ purā || 17 ||

arhā tvamasi kalyāṇi lakṣmīmāhātmyamuttamam |
śrotuṃ śrutiśiraḥśreṇihṛdayasthaṃ sanātanam || 18 ||

purā malayaśailasthā munayo dharmatatparāḥ |
śrutasāttvatavijñānā nāradāddevadarśanāt || 19 ||

apṛcchannetamevārthaṃ bhagavantaṃ sanātanam |
nāradaṃ brahmasaṃkāśaṃ bhagavaddharmavedinam || 20 ||

ṛṣayaḥ -

bhagavaṃstvacchruto'smābhiḥ [ā, B, c: bhagavattaḥ-] 
sāttvataḥ sattvasaṃśrayaḥ |
śuddho [ā, B, c, ṅ: bhagavato] bhāgavato dharmo 
mokṣaikaphalalakṣaṇaḥ || 21 ||

21. sāttvataḥ sattvasaṃśrayaḥ | sattvagaṇaikapradhāno dharma ityarthaḥ | anena 
sāttvataśabdanirvacanamapi sūcitam | yathā- sattvaṃ sattvaguṇaḥ asyāstīti 
sattvataḥ | parvatādivat matvarthe tappratyayaḥ | sattvata eva sāttvata iti | tasyaiva 
vivaraṇaṃ śuddho bhāgavato dharma iti |

p. 3)

tatra tattvārthakathane lakṣmīmāhātmyamuttamam |
sūcitaṃ tatra tatraiva nāpṛṣṭatvātprakāśitam || 22 ||

icchāmastamidaṃ śrotuṃ bhavasāgaratārakam |
padminīvaibhavaṃ sarvaṃ prajñāpayatu no bhavān || 23 ||

natāḥ sma śirasā pādau tava saṃsāratārakau |
adhīhi bho mune divyaṃ prapannāstvāṃ ciraṃ vayam || 24 ||

24. ciramiti | nāsaṃvatsaravāsine brūyāt iti vidhirabhipretaḥ | 

nāradaḥ -

sādhu saṃbodhito'smyadya munayaḥ saṃśitavratāḥ |
prasannaḥ kathayāmyadya lakṣmītantraṃ sanātanam || 25 ||

25. lakṣmītantramiti granthanāma | idaṃ ca tantraṃ śatakoṭigranthaparimitāt 
mūlabhūtalakṣmītantrāt sāramuddhṛtya kathitamiti vakṣyate'traiva (44##-

yatra sā dṛśyate devī svarūpaguṇavaibhavaiḥ |
padminī padmanābhasya mahiṣī padmasaṃbhavā || 26 ||

purā durvāsasaḥ śāpādabhibhūte puraṃdare |
niḥsvādhyāyavaṣaṭkāre bhraṣṭaśrīke jagattraye || 27 ||

27. puretyādi | iyamākhyāyikā viṣṇupurāṇe prathamāṃśe draṣṭavyā | 
niḥsvādhyāyavaṣaṭkāratva bhraṣṭaśrīkatve hetuḥ |

daridre devavarge ca kṛśe dharme nisaṃtate |
pitāmahe suraiḥ sārdhaṃ kṣīrodārṇavameyuṣi [ṅ: mīyuṣi; ī: 
kṣīrārṇavamupeyuṣi] || 28 ||

28. nisaṃtate | vicchinna ityarthaḥ |

bahūn varṣagaṇān [E, ī: bahuvarṣa-] divyāṃstaptvā tīvraṃ 
mahattapaḥ |
saṃbodhite jagannāthe devadeve janārdane || 29 ||

pitāmahena devāya kārye ca vinivedite |
kṣīrode mathite devaistadādiṣṭena vartmanā || 30 ||

pārijāte hayaśreṣṭhe gajendre'psarasāṃ gaṇe |
kālakūṭe samudbhūte vāruṇyāmamṛte tathā || 31 ||

saha candramasā devyāmutthitāyāṃ [ḍ, E, F, ī: sudhā] 
mahārṇavāt |
padminyāṃ padmanābhasya vakṣaḥsthāyāmanantaram || 32 ||

tayāvalokite devavarge śriyamupeyuṣi |
tayānavekṣite daityavarge caiva [ṅ: purā] parājite || 33 ||

33. anenānvayavyatirekābhyāṃ lakṣmīkaṭākṣapātasya sarvasaṃpannidānatvaṃ 
nirūpyate | yatsattve yatsattvamityanvaye yathā deveṣu iti dṛṣṭāntaḥ | yadabhāve 
yadabhāva iti vyatireke yathā daityeṣu iti dṛṣṭāntaḥ | imameva 
viṣayamanantarameva 35 tamaśloke vakṣyati |

svārājyamakhilaṃ prāpya modamāne puraṃdare |
bṛhaspatirupāgamya rahasīdaṃ vaco'bravīt || 34 ||

bṛhaspatiḥ -

kāle saṃbodhayāmyetacchṛṇu vākyaṃ puraṃdara |
anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ lakṣmyāste kathitā [ḍ, E, ī: viditā; ṅ: 
gaditā] purā || 35 ||

mahattā mahatāṃ nātha tasyāmāyatate [ā, B, c: māyāti te] 
sthitiḥ |
[ā, B, c, ṅ: omit this line] na bhraśyeta yathaivaiṣā tava 
rājyasthitiḥ parā || 36 ||

tathā yatasva [ā, B, c, ṅ: parāṃ yajasva] deveśa śaraṇaṃ 
gaccha padminīm |
eṣā hi śreyaso mūlameṣā hi paramā gatiḥ || 37 || 

p. 4)

śrutīnāmabhisaṃdhiśca saiva devī sanātanī |
eṣaiva jagatāṃ prāṇā eṣaiva jagatāṃ kriyā [c: priyā] || 38 ||

38. śrutīnāmiti | vedaiśca sarvairahameva vedyaḥ ityuktarītyā 
sarvavedatātparyaparyavasānabhūmirityarthaṃ | ubhayorapṛthagbhāvānna 
vacanavirodhaḥ |

eṣaiva [ā, B, c, ṅ: eṣā hi] jagatāmicchā jñānameṣā 
parāvarā |
eṣaiva sṛjate kāle saiṣā pāti jagattrayam || 39 ||

39. jagatkāraṇatvasya brahmāsādhāraṇatvasiddhānte'pi tacchaktirūpatvena 
tadapṛthaksiddhatvāt śaktikṛtasyāpi śaktimatkṛtatvavyapadeśo yujyata eva | 
vakṣyate caitat suspaṣṭaṃ devyaiva (11-6, 7).

jagatsaṃharate cānte tattatkāraṇasaṃsthitā |
mātaraṃ jagatāmenāmanārādhya mahat kutaḥ || 40 ||

etattu [E, ī: etattadvai] vaiṣṇavaṃ dhāma yato nāvartate yatiḥ [E: 
punaḥ] |
eṣā sā paramā niṣṭhā sāṃkhyānāṃ viditātmanām || 41 ||

eṣā sā yogināṃ niṣṭhā yatra gattvā na śocati |
eṣā pāśupatī niṣṭhā saiṣā vedavidāṃ gatiḥ || 42 ||

pañcarātrasya [E, ṅ: pāñca-] kṛtsnasya saiṣā niṣṭhā 
sanātani |
saiṣā nārāyaṇī devī sthitā nārāyaṇātmanā || 43 ||

pṛthagbhūtāpṛthagbhūtā jyotsneva himadīdhiteḥ |
taistairjñānaiḥ pṛthagbhūtairāgamaiśca pṛthagvidhaiḥ || 44 ||

44. nārāyaṇyā nārāyaṇātmanāvasthānaṃ pūrvaślokoktamevopapādayati - 
pṛthagbhūteti apṛthagbhūteti ca | dharmadharmiṇorapṛthaksiddhayoḥ 
niṣkarṣa-vivakṣāyāṃ dharmasya pṛthagvyapadeśaḥ | aniṣkarṣe tu 
dharmitayā | yathā śuklaṃ rūpam śuklaḥ paṭaḥ iti |

ekaivaiṣā parā devī bahudhā [ḍ, F: tadu-] samupāsyate |
tāmupehi mahābhāgāṃ śaraṇaṃ padmasaṃbhavām || 45 ||

tapoviśeṣai [E, F, ī: niyamaistaistaiśca vividhaiḥ] 
rvividhaistaistaiśca niyamaiḥ śubhaiḥ |
ārādhya mahiṣīṃ viṣṇoḥ sthirīkuru nijaśriyam || 46 ||

eṣā prasādasumukhī svaṃ padaṃ prāpayiṣyati |
abhipsitārthadā devī kāmināmapi kāmadā || 47 ||

47. devīprasāde paramapadarūpamokṣaprāptiḥ ānuṣaṅgikatrivargaphala##-

nāradaḥ -

iti saṃbodhitaḥ śakro guruṇā guruṇā svayam |
ārādhayitukāmastāṃ kṣīrodasyottaraṃ yayau || 48 ||

tatra divyaṃ tapastepe bilvamūlaniketanaḥ |
[E: omits 8 lines from here] ekapādasthito [F: bhūmau] maunī 
kāṣṭhabhūto'nilāśanaḥ || 49 ||

ūrdhvadṛgbāhuvaktraśca niyato niyatātmavān |
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ vai tapastepe suduścaram || 50 ||

tapaso'vabhṛthe tasya sā devī padmasaṃbhavā |
prasannavadanā viṣṇormahiṣī darśanaṃ yayau || 51 ||

agrataḥ saṃsthitāṃ devīṃ jagatāṃ mātaraṃ parām |
tāṃ śakraścakṣuṣāṃ vīkṣya vismayaṃ paramaṃ yayau || 52 ||

vihvalaḥ praṇipatyātha prāñjalirbalasūdanaḥ |
śriyaṃ [ī: śriyaḥ] sūktena tuṣṭāva padminīṃ pākaśāsanaḥ || 53 ||

p. 5)

ekāntabhāvamāpannamavyājāṃ bhaktimāsthitam |
taṃ vīkṣya jagatāṃ mātā vākyametaduvāca ha || 54 ||

śrīḥ -

vatsa śakra prasannāsmi tapasā tava suvrata |
varaṃ vṛṇu mahābhāga kimiṣṭaṃ karavāṇi te || 55 ||

śakraḥ -

adya me tapaso devi yamasya niyamasya ca |
sadyaḥ phalamavāptaṃ yad dṛṣṭā bhagavatī mayā || 56 ||

yadi vāpi varo deyastvayā me parameśvari |
tattvaṃ kathaya deveśi [E, ī: me devi] yāsi tvaṃ yatprakārikā || 57 ||

yatpramāṇā yadādhārā yadupāyā sanātanī |
yasya tvaṃ tena [E, ī: kena] vā devi saṃbandhastava yadvidhaḥ || 58 ||

yaccānyadveditavyaṃ te nānāśāstropabṛṃhitam |
kathayeśvari tatsarvamupasanno'smyadhīhi bho || 59 ||

iti [E: saṃbodhitā] prasāditā tena vatseneva payasvinī |
snihyatā manasā padmā pākaśāsanamabravīt || 60 ||

śrīḥ -

śṛṇu śakra mahābhāga yā [ḍ, F, ṅ, ī: smyahaṃ] hyahaṃ 
yatprakārikā |
yasyāhaṃ tena vā yādṛk saṃbandho mama vṛtrahan || 61 ||

iti [E: śrīpañcarātre; B: śrīpāñcarātrasāre; F: śrīpāñcarātre] 
śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre [ā, B, c, ḍ, F: 
sarvādhiṣṭhānaprakāśo; ā, ī: omit the title] śāstrāvatāro nāma 
prathamo'dhyāyaḥ || 1 ||
p. 6)

dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ 

śīruvāca -

asti nirduḥkhaniḥśīmasukhānubhavalakṣaṇaḥ |
paramātmā paraṃ yasya padaṃ paśyanti sūrayaḥ || 1 ||

1. nirduḥkhetyādinā nikhilaheyapratyanīkatvānantakalyāṇaguṇākaratvarūpo##-

kaścitkeśāṃcidātmā syāttasyānyeṣāṃ ca kaścana |
tasvāpyanya [ā: evam] itītthaṃ tu yatraiṣā [E, ī: eṣaḥ] 
vyavatiṣṭhate || 2 ||

2. ātmā | niyantetyarthaḥ | eṣeti | vyavastheti śeṣaḥ | vyavatiṣṭhate samāpnoti sa 
paramātmeti pūrveṇānvayaḥ |

[ā, B, c: adhvānam] adhvanāmadhvanaḥ pāraṃ 
paramātmānamūcire |
ahaṃ nāma smṛto yo'rthaḥ sa ātmā samudīryate || 3 ||

3. adhvanāmiti | traivargikādhvāpekṣayā paramo yo'dhvā mokṣādhvā arcirādiḥ 
tasya pāraṃ prāpyamityarthaḥ so'dhvanaḥ pāramāpnoti tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam 
iti vacanāt | adhvanāmadhvapate iti śrutyuddharaṇamatra | ahamiti | yaḥ 
ahaṃpratyayagocaraḥ sa ātmetyarthaḥ |

anavacchinnarūpo'haṃ paramātmeti śabdyate [B, c: śamyate] |
kroḍīkṛtamidaṃ sarvaṃ cetanācetanātmakam || 4 ||

4. anavacchinneti | vastvādyaparicchedena yo'haṃśabdāparyavasānavṛttyā 
pratīyate sa paramātmetyarthaḥ |

yena so'haṃsmṛto bhāvaḥ paramātmā sanātanaḥ |
sa vāsudevo bhagavān kṣetrajñaḥ paramo mataḥ || 5 ||

viṣṇurnārāyaṇo viśvo viśvarūpa itīryate |
ahaṃtayā samākrāntaṃ tasya [B, E: sarvaṃ] viśvamidaṃ jagat || 6 ||

6. āha ca śakre'nyatra - tvayaitadviṣṇunā cāmba jagadvyāptaṃ carācaram iti |

vastvavastu [ā, B, c: vastutastu] ca tannāsti 
yannākrāntamahaṃtayā |
[ā, B, c, ḍ: omit this line] idaṃtayā yadālīḍhamākrāntaṃ 
tadahaṃtayā || 7 ||

7. idaṃtayeti | idaṃśabdārthatayā pratītaṃ sarvaṃ jagadityarthaḥ |

sarvataḥ śānta [B: sarvaḥ śāntaḥ sa] evāsau nirvikāraḥ sanātanaḥ |
ananto deśakālādiparicchedavivarjitaḥ || 8 ||

mahāvibhūtirityukto vyāptiḥ sā mahatī yataḥ |
tad brahma paramaṃ dhāma nirālambanabhāvanam [ī: 
nirālambamabhāvanam] || 9 ||

nistaraṅgāmṛtāmbhodhikalpaṃ ṣāḍguṇyamujjvalam |
ekaṃ tacciddhanaṃ śāntamudayāstamayojjhaitam || 10 ||

10. nirastaraṅgetyādinā bhagavataḥ śāntoditāvasthocyate | tadevāha- 
śāntamiti |

apṛthagbhūtaśaktitvād brahmādvaitaṃ taducyate |
tasya yā paramā śaktirjyotsneva himadīdhiteḥ || 11 ||

11. brahmādvaitamiti | svāpṛthaksiddhaśaktyahaṃtāviśiṣṭatvāt tadviśiṣṭaṃ 
brahmaikameva tattvamityarthaḥ | svasvarūpasya tadapṛthaksiddhatvameva 
dṛṣṭāntamukhenāha - jyosneti |

p. 7)

sarvāvasthāgatā devī svātmabhūtānapāyinī |
ahaṃtā brahmaṇastasya sāhamasmi sanātanī || 12 ||

12. sarvāvasthāgatānapāyinītyanena rāghavatve'bhavatsītā rukmiṇī kṛṣṇajanmani 
| anyeṣu cāvatāreṣu viṣṇoreṣānapāyinī iti maharṣivacanaṃ smāryate |

ātmā sa sarvabhūtānāmahaṃbhūto hariḥ smṛtaḥ |
ahaṃtā sarvabhūtānāmahamasmi sanātanī || 13 ||

yena bhāvena bhavati vāsudevaḥ sanātanaḥ |
bhavatastasya devasya [B, c: sahāyo] sa bhāvo'hamitīritā || 14 ||

bhavadbhāvātmakaṃ brahma tatastacchāśvataṃ padam |
bhavannārāyaṇo devo bhāvo lakṣmīrahaṃ parā || 15 ||

lakṣmīnārāyaṇākhyātamato [E, ī: ṇākhyaṃ tat] brahma sanātanam |
ahaṃtayā samākrānto hyahamarthaḥ prasiddhyati || 16 ||

16. śrīviśiṣṭameva paraṃ brahma | na tu niḥśrīkamityarthaḥ | 
lakṣmīnārāyāṇayorubhayorātmahaviḥśeṣitve'pi āgnāvaiṣṇavādiṣviva 
viśiṣṭasyaivoddeśyatvāt tasya caikatvāt na śeṣidvitvaprasaktiriti bhāvaḥ |

ahamarthasamutthā ca sāhaṃtā parikīrtitā |
anyonyenāvinābhāvādanyonyena samanvayāt || 17 ||

tādātmyaṃ viddhi saṃbandhaṃ mama nāthasya cobhayoḥ |
ahaṃtayā vināhaṃ hi nirupākhyo na sidhyati || 18 ||

18. nirupākhyo nirviśeṣaḥ | loke sarvamapi vastu idamitthamiti 
prakāraviśeṣapuraskāreṇaiva sidhyati na tu nirviśeṣamiti bhāvaḥ |

ahamarthaṃ vināhaṃtā nirādhārā na siddhyati |
bhavadbhāvātmakaṃ rūpaṃ samastavyastagocaram || 19 ||

parokṣamaparokṣaṃ ca jagati pravicintyate [E: vibhāvyate; ī: vibhajyate] |
nirunmeṣe nirunmeṣā sāhaṃtā parameśvarīṃ || 20 ||

kroḍīkṛtyākhilaṃ sarvaṃ [B: viśvam] brahmaṇi vyavatiṣṭhate |
unmeṣastasya yo nāma yathā candrodaye'mbudheḥ || 21 ||

ahaṃ nārāyaṇī śaktiḥ sisṛkṣālakṣaṇā tadā |
nimeṣastasya yo nāma saṃhṛtau paramātmanaḥ || 22 ||

22. devyāḥ śaktirūpatvaṃ pūrvamuktam | adhunā tadbheda ucyate - 
sisṛkṣāśaktiḥ suṣupsāśaktiśceti | prathamayā sṛṣṭiḥ dvitīyayā saṃhāraśca 
jāyate | tatra nityavibhūtiviṣaye prathamāyā eva pravṛttiḥ | dvitīyāyāstu 
līlāvibhūtāvevopayogaḥ | 

ahaṃ nārāyaṇī śaktiḥ suṣupsālakṣaṇā hi sā |
[E: sisṛkṣāyāṃ mamodyantyāṃ] sisṛkṣāyā mamodyantyā 
devāllakṣmīpateḥ svayam || 23 ||

avyāhatamasaṃkocamaiśvaryaṃ pravijṛmbhate |
jñānaṃ tatparamaṃ brahma [B: sarvādeśi] sarvadarśi nirāmayam || 24 ||

24. jñānādiṣu ṣaṭsu guṇeṣu jñānaṃ tatsvarūpanirūpakadharmaḥ | anye guṇāstu 
nirūpitasvarūpaviśeṣaṇabhūtā iti vivekaḥ | tadeva vakṣyatyuttaratra 
śeṣamaiśvaryavīryādi jñānadharmaḥ iti | jñānākhyasya svarūpasya dharma 
ityarthaḥ |

jñānātmikā tathāhaṃtā sarvajñā sarvadarśinī |
jñānātmakaṃ paraṃ rūpaṃ brahmaṇo mama cobhayoḥ || 25 ||

śeṣamaiśvaryavīryādi jñānadharmaḥ sanātanaḥ |
ahamityāntaraṃ rūpaṃ jñānarūpamudīryate [E: mitīryate] || 26 ||

prakāśakādikaṃ rūpaṃ sphaṭikādisalakṣaṇaṃ |
[ī: atastat] atastu jñānarūpatvaṃ mama nārāyaṇasya ca || 27 ||

p. 8)

avyāhatiryadudyatyāstadaiśvaryaṃ paraṃ mama |
iccheti socyate tattattattvaśāstreṣu paṇḍitaiḥ || 28 ||

28. iccheti | sisṛkṣāśakteraiśvaryarūpatvaṃ pūrvaṃ caturviṃśe śloke 
varṇitamatra smartavyam | sisṛkṣā hi sraṣṭumicchā |

jagatprakṛtibhāvo me yaḥ sā śaktiritīryate |
sṛjantyā yacchramābhāvo mama tadbalamiṣyate || 29 ||

29. devyā jatatprakṛtibhāvo na svarūpataḥ tathātve vikāritvaprasaṅgāt | kiṃtu 
svaprakārabhūtacidacidātmaneti draṣṭavyam | eṣaiva hi brahmaṇo jagatprakṛtitve 
gatiḥ |

bharaṇaṃ yacca kāryasya balaṃ tacca pracakṣate |
śaktyaṃśakena [ā, c, ṅ: śakyaṃ śaktena ca] 
tatprāhurbharaṇaṃ tattvakovidāḥ || 30 ||

vikāraviraho vīryaṃ prakṛtitve'pi me sadā |
svabhāvaṃ hi jahātyāśu payo dadhisamudbhave || 31 ||

31. vikāravirahitvaṃ pūrvoktarītyā prakārāṃśe pariṇāmaḥ na tu prakāryaṃśa iti 
svīkārāt | prakāribhūte svarūpa eva pariṇāmamaṅgīkṛtya 
aghaṭitaghaṭanāsāmarthyāt vikārābhāvanirvāhastu na yuktisahaḥ | ata eva māyāṃ 
tu prakṛtiṃ vidyāt ityatreśvaropādhibhūtamāyāyāṃ prakṛtitvamartha iti 
keṣāṃcidabhyupagamaḥ |

jagadbhāve'pi sā nāsti [ī: prakṛtiḥ] vikṛtirmama nityadā |
vikāraviraho vīryamatastattvavidāṃ matam || 32 ||

vikramaḥ kathito vīryamaiśvaryāṃśaḥ sa tu smṛtaḥ |
sahakāryanapekṣā me sarvakāryavidhau hi yā || 33 ||

tejaḥ ṣaṣṭhaṃ guṇaṃ prāhustamimaṃ tattvavedinaḥ |
parābhibhavasāmarthyaṃ tejaḥ kecitpracakṣate || 34 ||

aiśvarye yojayantyeke tattejastattvakovidāḥ |
iti pañca guṇā ete jñānasya [E, ī: sphūrtayaḥ] srutayo'malāḥ || 35 ||

jñānādyāḥ ṣaḍguṇā ete ṣāḍguṇyaṃ mama tadvapuḥ |
udyatītthaṃ sisṛkṣāyā mamāyutatamī kalā || 36 ||

36. udyatīti | udayaṃ prāptetyarthaḥ |

śuddhāśuddhātmako vargastayā kroḍīkṛto'khilaḥ |
tatra śuddhamayaṃ mārgaṃ vyākhyāsyāmi sureśvara || 37 ||

37. śuddhasṛṣṭiḥ aśuddhasṛṣṭiśceti dvividhā sṛṣṭiḥ | tatra śuddhā 
traiguṇyarahitā śuddhasattvamayī | tasyāḥ svayaṃprakāśatvādajaḍatvamiti kecit | 
jaḍatvameveti pare | asyā eva nityavibhūtiriti vyavahāraḥ | aśuddhasṛṣṭistu 
traiguṇyamayī jaḍarūpā līlāvibhūtiriti vyavahṛtā |

abhivyaktānabhivyaktaṣāḍguṇyakramamujjvalam |
ālambitacatūrūpaṃ rūpaṃ tatpārameśvaram || 38 ||

38. na kevalaṃ nityavibhūtireva śuddhasṛṣṭiḥ | kiṃtu tatra vidyotamānāḥ 
vāsudevasaṃkarṣaṇapradyumnāniruddhākhyā vyūhā api tatheti mantavyam | tatra 
prathame vāsudeve ṣāḍguṇyakramasya na sphuṭāvabhāsaḥ | tadanantaravyūheṣu 
kiṃcitkiṃcit krameṇeti jñeyam | 

guṇakalpanayādhyasto [ī: guṇānāṃ kalpanāvastho] 
guṇonmeṣakṛtakramaḥ |
mūrtīkṛtaguṇaśceti tridhā mārgo'yamadbhutaḥ || 39 ||

39. teṣu kvacit vyūhe guṇānāṃ samyaganunmeṣāt na guṇātmatayā jñānam | 
kvacittu krameṇonmeṣāt kramādabhivyaktiḥ | kvacittu guṇānāmatiprakāśāt 
guṇātmanā parijñānamiti kramaḥ | 

yugāni trīṇi ṣaṇṇāṃ yānyāhujñānādikāni vai [E: vedinaḥ] |
samāsavyāsatasteṣāṃ cāturātmyaṃ vivicyate [ā, B, c, ḍ: 
amūrtimat] || 40 ||

[ā, B, c, ḍ: omit verses 41 and 42] samastavyastabhedena 
guṇānāṃ tadyugatrayam |
vivakṣyate yadā sā me śāntāyāścāturātmyatā [ī: caturātmatā] || 41 ||

p. 9)

ākṛtīranavekṣyāpi guṇānāṃ kalpanākṛtam [ī: guṇakalpanayā-] |
cāturātmyamidaṃ prāhuḥ śāntāyāstattvacintakāḥ || 42 ||

[ā, B, c, ḍ, F: omit this verse] śāntātiśāntādunmeṣo 
mama rūpādyugatraye |
kramavyaktaṃ tadādyaṃ me cāturātmyamamūrtimat || 43 ||

ataraṅgamanirdeśyaṃ niḥsattaṃ sattvamavyayam |
saccinmātrākhya unmeṣaḥ sādyā me śāntatācyutiḥ || 44 ||

vyaktajñānabalākhyāyāṃ pūrvaṃ saṃkarṣaṇātmani |
tilakālakavatsarvo [E, ī: tilakālika-] vikāro mayi tiṣṭhati || 45 ||

45. tilakālakavaditi | yathā prāṇināṃ dehe tilavat sūkṣmaḥ kālavarṇaśca 
binduviśeṣo'tyalpaparimite kvacitkoṇe dṛśyate tadvat sarvo'pi prapañcaḥ 
mamaikadeśe tiṣṭhatītyarthaḥ | saṃkarṣatīti saṃkarṣaṇa ityanvarthaṃ nāma | atra 
jñānabalayoḥ samunmeṣaḥ |

tanmāṃ saṃkarṣaṇātmānaṃ vidurjñānabale budhāḥ |
svayaṃ gṛhṇāmi kartṛtvamunmiṣantī tataḥ param [ā, B, c, 
ṅ, ī: parā] || 46 ||

pradyumna iti māmāhuḥ sarvārthadyotanīṃ tadā |
yugaṃ prasphuritaṃ rūpaṃ [B, c, ḍ, ī : rūpe] 
tasminnaiśvaryavīryayoḥ || 47 ||

47. prakṛṣṭaṃ dyumnaṃ balam tejo vā yasyetyanvarthaṃ nāma | 
sṛṣṭikartṛtvāt atra vīryaiśvaryayoḥ samunmeṣaḥ |

tatastayā kriyāśaktyā labdhāveśā [ā, B, c, ḍ: 
labdhāvasthā] cikīrṣayā |
yujyamānāniruddhākhyāṃ lambhitā tattvakovidaiḥ || 48 ||

48. aniruddheti | pālanakartaiṣaḥ | atra śaktitejasoḥ samunmeṣaḥ | tathā ca 
saṃjagṛhuḥ - ṣāḍguṇyādvāsudevaḥ para iti sa bhavānmuktabhogyo 
balāḍhyādbodhātsaṃkarṣaṇastvaṃ harasi vitanuṣe śāstramaiśvaryavīryāt | 
pradyumnaḥ sargadharmau nayasi ca bhagavan śaktitejo'niruddho bibhrāṇaḥ pāsi 
sattvaṃ gamayasi ca tathā vyūhya raṅgādhirāja | iti |

avasthāḥ kramaśo me tāḥ suṣuptisvapnajāgarāḥ |
tisro mama svabhāvākhyā vijñānaiśvaryaśaktayaḥ || 49 ||

49. suṣuptītyādi | saṃkarṣaṇapradyumnāniruddhānāṃ 
suṣuptyādyadhiṣṭhātṛtvam | vāsudevastu turīyādhiṣṭhāteti vivekaḥ | ayameva 
vibhāgaḥ māṇḍukyopaniṣadi viśvataijasaprājñaturyanāmabhirnirdiṣṭaḥ |

unmiṣantyaḥ pṛthaktattvatrayeṇa parikīrtitāḥ |
balaṃ vīryaṃ tathā teja ityetattu [E, ī: iti tattu] guṇatrayam || 50 ||

śramādyavadyābhāvākhyaṃ [ā, B, c, ṅ : śramādyavidyā##-
itthaṃ śāntoditāvasthādvayabhedajuṣo mama || 51 ||

51. śāntoditeti | śāntāvasthā paravāsudevāvasthā | uditāvasthā vyūhāvasthā | 
tatrāpi śāntoditāvasthaḥ prathamavyūhaḥ | nityoditāvasthā anye traya iti viśeṣaḥ |

svadharmormisamullāso na bhedāyāmbudheriva |
prāyo yadguṇakartavye varte kṛtyā yayā hyaham || 52 ||

tatra tadguṇayugmaṃ tu mama rūpatayocyate |
ato jñānabale devaḥ saṃkarṣaṇa udīryate [B: itīryate] || 53 ||

aiśvaryavīrye pradyumno'niruddhaḥ śaktitejasī |
ādyastvabhinnaṣāḍguṇyo [ḍ: āyaṃ-] brahmatattvāpṛthaksthitau || 
54 ||

eko'pyanunayaudāryakrauryaśauryādibhirguṇaiḥ |
naṭaḥ pravartate yadvadveṣaceṣṭādibhedavān || 55 ||

p. 10)

tadvaddekāpi tvaivāhaṃ jñānaśaktyādibhirguṇaiḥ [E, ī: 
śanaiśvaryā] |
saṃkarṣaṇādisadbhāvaṃ bhaje lokahitepsayā || 56 ||

kramaśaḥ [ī: krameṇa] pralayotpattisthitibhiḥ prāṇyanugrahaḥ |
prayojanamathānyacca śāstraśāstrārthatatphalaiḥ || 57 ||

57. saṃkarṣaṇaḥ pralayakartā śāstropadeṣṭā ca | pradyumnaḥ sṛṣṭikartā 
śāstrārthapravartayitā ca | aniruddhaḥ pālanakartā śāstrārthaphalanirvāhakaśceti 
pratyekaṃ vyāpāravibhāgaḥ |

daśāsturyasuṣuptyādyāścaturvyūhe'pi lakṣayet |
vibhavo'nantarūpastu padmanābhamukho vibhoḥ || 58 ||

58. paravyūhāvupavarṇya vibhavaḥ kathyate'tra | vibhavāvatārāḥ padmanābhādayo 
bahavaḥ | matsyakūrmādayo'pyatraiva parigaṇitā avatārāḥ |

aniruddhasya vistāro darśitastasya sāttvate |
arcāpi laukikī yā sā bhagavadbhāvitātmanām || 59 ||

59. arcā nāma devālayeṣu pratiṣṭhitā bimbaviśeṣāḥ |

mantramantreśvaranyāsātsāpi ṣāḍguṇyavigrahā |
parādyarcāvasāne'sminmama [ā, B, c, ḍ: parādyarcāvatāre##-

turyādyavasthā vijñeyā itīyaṃ śuddhapaddhatiḥ |
īṣadbhedena vijñeyaṃ tadvyūhavibhavāntaram ||
śuddhetaraṃ tvatho mārgaṃ mama śakra niśāmaya || 61 ||

iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre [ī: pāñcarātre] lakṣmītantre [ā, B, 
c, ḍ, F: omit the title; ṅ: śuddhasṛṣṭi] śuddhamārgaprakāśo 
nāma dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ ||
p. 11)

tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ 

śrīruvāca -

nityanirdoṣaniḥsīmakalyāṇaguṇaśālinī |
ahaṃ nārāyaṇī nāma sā sattā vaiṣṇavī parā || 1 ||

deśātkālāttathā rūpātparicchedo na me smṛtaḥ |
saṃvittireva me rūpaṃ sarvaiśvaryādiko [ḍ: saivai-] guṇaḥ || 2 ||

2. svarūpanirūpako dharmaḥ jñānam | anye pañcāpi guṇāḥ 
nirūpitasvarūpagṇabhūtā ityarthaḥ |

svasvātantryavaśenaiva vibhāgastatra vartate |
vijñānaiśvaryaśaktyātmā [E, ī: śaktyātma] vibhāgo yaḥ sa īritaḥ 
|| 3 ||

3. itthaṃ vibhāgo'pi madicchākṛta evetyāha - svasvātantryeti | 

vijñānaiśvaryaśaktīnāmunmeṣastvaparo'dhunā |
atarkyāyā mamodyatyā niyogānarhayā [E, ī: nuyogā-] sadā || 4 ||

4. apara iti | pūrvaṃ śuddhasṛṣṭau eka unmeṣa uktaḥ | adhunā 
aśuddhātmakatraiguṇyasṛṣṭāvanya unmeṣa ityarthaḥ |

icchayānyatkṛtaṃ rūpamāsījjñānādike trike |
yathaivekṣurasaḥ svaccho guḍatvaṃ pratipadyate || 5 ||

5. jñānāditrikarūpaṃ sattvāditrikātmanā anyathā kṛtamāsīdityarthaḥ |

tadvatsvacchamayaṃ jñānaṃ sattvatāṃ pratipadyate |
rajastvaṃ ca mamaiśvaryaṃ tamastvaṃ śaktirapyuta || 6 ||

6. tadevāha - tadvaditi | jñānaṃ sattvatayā aiśvaryaṃ rajastayā śaktiśca 
tamastayā jātamiti bhāvaḥ |

ete trayo guṇāḥ śakra traiguṇyamiti śabdyate |
rajaḥpradhānaṃ tatsṛṣṭau traiguṇyaṃ parivartate || 7 ||

7. traiguṇyamiti cāturvarṇyamitivat svārthe ṣyañ |

sthitau sattvapradhānaṃ tat saṃhṛtau tu tamomukham |
ahaṃ saṃvinmayī pūrvā vyāpinyapi puraṃdara || 8 ||

adhiṣṭhāya guṇān sṛṣṭisthitisaṃhṛtikāriṇī |
nirguṇāpi guṇānetānadhiṣṭhāyātmavāñchayā || 9 ||

9. nirguṇāpīti | pūrvaṃ ṣāḍguṇyasyoktatvāt atra nirguṇapadasya 
sattvarajastamorūpamiśraguṇarahitetyarthaḥ | ātmavāñchayetyanena 
jagatsṛṣṭyādau līlaiva prayojanamityuktaṃ bhavati | yathāha bhagavān 
bādarāyaṇaḥ - lokavattu līlākaivalyam iti |

cakraṃ pravartayāmyekā sṛṣṭisthityantarūpakam |

śakraḥ - 

vidhādvayaṃ samāsthāya jñānādye tu yugatraye [ā, ṅ, ī: 
guṇatraye] || 10 ||

śuddhetaravibhāgena kimarthaṃ tvaṃ pravartase |
vidhayoranayoḥ padme saṃbandhaḥ kaḥ parasparam || 11 ||

etatpṛṣṭā mayā brūhi namaste padmasaṃbhave |

śrīḥ -

aniyojyaṃ mamaiśvaryamicchaiva mama kāraṇam || 12 ||

p. 12)

muhyantyatra mahānto'pi tattvaṃ śṛṇu tathāpi me |
īśeśitavyabhāvena parivarte sadā hyaham [ā, B, c, ī: 
sadāpyaham] || 13 ||

13. śuddhetarasṛṣṭyoḥ saṃbandha ucyate - īśeśitavyeti | 
rakṣyarakṣakabhāva ityarthaḥ |

īśo nārāyaṇo jñeya īśatā tasya cāpyaham |
īśitavyaṃ tu vijñeyaṃ cidacicca puraṃdara || 14 ||

cicchaktistu parā tatra bhoktṛtāṃ pratipadyate |
bhogyopakaraṇasthānarūpaṃ tasyā acitpadam || 15 ||

anādyayā samāviddhā sā cicchaktiravidyayā |
matpravartitayā nityaṃ cicchaktirbhoktṛtāṃ gatā || 16 ||

ahaṃmamatvasaṃbandhāddhyacitsvenābhimanyate |
avidyā sā tirobhāvaṃ vidyayā yāti vai yadā || 17 ||

cicchaktirnirabhīmānā tadā madbhāvameṣyati |
tāṃ vidyāṃ śuddhamārgasthāṃ paravyūhādirūpiṇī || 18 ||

pravartayāmi kāruṇyājjñānasadbhāvadarśinī |
rakṣyarakṣakabhāvo'yaṃ saṃbandho vidhayordvayoḥ || 19 ||

vidhā rakṣati śuddhādyā rakṣyate ca vidhāparā [F: tathā-] |
etatte kathitaṃ śakra kiṃ bhūyaḥ śrotumicchasi || 20 ||

śakraḥ -

īśeśitavyabhāvena kimarthaṃ tvaṃ [F: ca] pravartase |
īśitavyaṃ kiyadbhedaṃ kiṃrūpaṃ tatra me vada || 21 ||

śrīḥ -

svabhāvo nānuyojyo'yaṃ [ā, B, c: ananu-] mama 
nārāyaṇasya ca |
īśo'hamīśitavyo [ā, B, c, ṅ: īśo hi ī] na sa ca devaḥ 
sanātanaḥ || 22 ||

īśitavyaṃ dvidhā proktaṃ cidacidvyatirekataḥ |
cicchaktirbhoktṛrūpātra sā ca cidrūpadhāriṇī [E: madrūpa-] || 23 ||

bhogyopakaraṇasthānairacicchaktistridhā sthitā [ḍ: matā; ī: smṛtā] |
prasarantyāstṛtīyaṃ me sā ca parva smṛtaṃ budhaiḥ || 24 ||

vibhakte api te ete śaktī cidacidātmike |
matsvācchandyavaśenaiva mama rūpe sanātane || 25 ||

cicchaktirvimalā śuddhā cinmayyānandarūpiṇī |
anādyavidyāviddheyamitthaṃ saṃsarati dhruvam || 26 ||

acicchaktirjaḍāpyevamaśuddhā pariṇāminī |
triguṇāpi mamaivedaṃ svācchandyāt pravijṛmbhitam || 27 ||

dhūmaketuryathā dhūmaṃ [ā, B, c, ḍ, ṅ: dhūmāt] 
dīpyamāno bhajet svayam |
śuddhasaṃvitsvarūpāpi bhaje sāhamacidgatim || 28 ||

28. jvalanasvabhāvo'pi dhūmaketuryathā malinadhūmarūpatāṃ pratipadyate tathā 
jñānasvarūpāpyahamacidbhāvamāpadya ityarthaḥ |

anākrāntā vikalpena [E, ī: śabdenāpya] śabdairapyakadarthitā |
ādhyānopadhināpyevaṃ varte'hamacidātmanā || 29 ||

29. ādhyānopadhineti | madicchārūpopādhinetyarthaḥ | dhyānālambanārthamiti 
vārthaḥ | yathā vakṣyati - dhyānaviśrāmabhūmayaḥ (4-24) iti |

bahirantaḥpadārthe hi citsvarūpamakhaṇḍitam |
viśinaṣṭi tathāpyetaccitrayopādhisaṃpadā || 30 ||

svātantryameva me heturnānuyojyāsmi kiṃcana |
itthaṃprabhāvāmevaṃ [ā, B, c, ṅ: idaṃprabhāvām] māṃ 
vidan buddho bhaviṣyasi || 31 ||

p. 13)

śakraḥ -

kathaṃ sṛjasi vai lokān sukhaduḥkhasamanvitān |
asṛṣṭirhi varaṃ yadvā sṛṣṭirastu sukhātmikā || 32 ||

32. asṛṣṭiriti | atra sṛjecca sukhamevaikamanukampāpracoditaḥ iti 
ślokavārttikavacanaṃ smartavyam |

śrīḥ - 

anādyavidyāviddhānāṃ jīvānāṃ sadasanmayam |
saṃcitaṃ karma saṃprekṣya miśrāṃ sṛṣṭiṃ karomyaham || 33 ||

śakraḥ -

kṣīrodasaṃbhave devi svācchandyaṃ kathaṃ bhavet |
karma cetsamavekṣya tvaṃ vidadhāsi sukhāsukhe || 34 ||

śrīḥ -

kurvatyā mama kāryāṇi karma tatkaraṇaṃ smṛtam |
kartuśca karaṇāpekṣā na svātantryavighātinī || 35 ||

niravadyā svatantrāhaṃ nānuyogapade sthitā |
vibhaje bahudhātmānaṃ kartṛkarmakriyādinā || 36 ||

līlāyai kāraṇaṃ nātra mṛgyamevaṃ sthiro bhava |

śakraḥ - 

yadvā tadvāstu taddevi svātantryaṃ te yadīdṛśam |
sṛṣṭiprakāramākhyāhi namaste padmasaṃbhave || 37 ||

iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre [ā: śrīpañcarātrasāre; ī: śrīpāñcarātre] 
lakṣmītantre traiguṇyaprakāśo [ṅ: miśrasṛṣṭi; ā, B, c, 
ḍ, F: omit the title] nāma tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ ||
p. 14)

caturtho'dhyāyaḥ 

śrīḥ [B: śrīruvāca] -

nirmalākāśakalpāhaṃ niḥsamānandacinmayī |
ahaṃ nārāyaṇī nāma bhāvo'haṃ tādṛśo [B: tādṛśī] hareḥ || 1 ||

na śāntā noditā nāpi madhyamāhaṃ cidātmikā |
tādṛśasya harerviṣṇoḥ svarūpamakhilātmanaḥ || 2 ||

tasyācitraikarūpasya [E, ī: tasya citraika-] vikalpapadavījuṣaḥ |
acitrāhaṃ tadākārā sarvataḥ samatāṃ gatā || 3 ||

tayornau saṃvidātmaiva kvacidunmeṣa utthitaḥ |
koṭikoṭisahasraughakoṭikoṭitamī kalā || 4 ||

sisṛkṣā nāma tadrūpā sṛṣṭimiṣṭāṃ karomyaham |
ekāṃśena viśuddhādhvarūpā varte'hamañjasā || 5 ||

vajraratnaprabhā yadvatparisphurati sarvataḥ |
evaṃ śuddhamayo mārgo mama sphurati sarvataḥ [ā, B, c, 
ṅ, ī: rūpataḥ] || 6 ||

ameghākāśasaṃkāśānniṣpandodadhirūpataḥ [ā, B: ameyā-] |
mama jñānaghanādrūpācchuddhā [ā, ṅ: -śuddha] sṛṣṭiḥ 
pravartate || 7 ||

nirvyāpāraṃ [E, ī: nirvāsanaṃ] sadānandaṃ śuddhaṃ sarvātmakaṃ 
param |
vyajyate prathamaṃ jñānaṃ sa saṃkarṣaṇa ucyate || 8 ||

hetvantarānapekṣaṃ yat svātantryaṃ viśvanirmitau |
tadaiśvaryaṃ tadāsīnme pradyumnaḥ puruṣottamaḥ || 9 ||

nilīnacitrarūpā yā sarvatra samavasthitā |
avyāhatāsīcchaktirme so'niruddhaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 10 ||

sṛṣṭisthityantakartāro vijñānaiśvaryaśaktayaḥ |
mama rūpamamī devāḥ puruṣāḥ puṣkarekṣaṇāḥ || 11 ||

ataraṅgārṇavābhāsamastāmbhodāmbaropamam [E, ī: 
anambhodāmbaropamam] |
rūpaṃ sisṛkṣamāṇāyā vāsudevo mamādimam [ā, ṅ: 
mamātmakaḥ] || 12 ||

jñānaśaktibalaiśvaryavīryatejāṃsyaśeṣataḥ |
unmiṣanti yadā tulyaṃ vāsudevastadocyate || 13 ||

teṣāṃ jñānabalonmeṣe [E: balonmeṣaḥ] saṃkarṣaṇa udīryate |
bibharti sakalaṃ viśvaṃ tilakālakavatsvataḥ || 14 ||

balamityeva tannāma tato vedāntaśabditam |
vīryaiśvaryasamunmeṣe [E, ī: samunmeṣaḥ] pradyumnaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 
15 ||

vikāraviraho vīryamavikārī tataśca saḥ |
śaktitejaḥsamunmeṣe hyaniruddhaḥ sa īritaḥ [E, ī: samīritaḥ] || 16 ||

tejastvanyānapekṣatvamaniruddhatvamapyuta [E, ī: ataḥ] |
śāstraṃ [E, ī: saṃkarṣaṇāddevāt] saṃkarṣaṇādeva bhāti 
nirghātaśabdavat || 17 ||

tatkriyā sakalā devātpradyumnāt saṃbhavedyataḥ [E, ī: 
saṃbhavatyuta] |
kriyāpalamaśeṣaṃ tadaniruddhātpracakṣate || 18 ||

p. 15)

sṛjate hyaniruddho'tra pradyumnaḥ pāti tatkṛtam |
sṛṣṭaṃ tadrakṣitaṃ cātti sa ca saṃkarṣaṇaḥ prabhuḥ || 19 ||

19. atrāniruddhasya sṛṣṭikartṛtvaṃ pradyumnasya pālanakartṛtvaṃ cocyate | 
pūrvaṃ tu dvitīyādhyāye aniruddhasya pālanakartṛtvaṃ pradyumnasya 
sṛṣṭikartṛtvaṃ coktam | tattu kalpāntareṇeti dhyeyam |

sṛṣṭisthityantakāryeṇa śāstradharmaphalena ca |
anugrahamime devāḥ sadā vidadhate svayam || 20 ||

yadyapyekaguṇonmeṣastathāpyete [E: -tadāpyete] hi ṣaḍguṇāḥ |
anyūnānadhikāḥ sarve vāsudevātsanātanāt [ā, B, ḍ: 
sanātanāḥ] || 21 ||

aṅgapratyaṅgabuddhyādirnaiṣāṃ bhūtamayaḥ smṛtaḥ |
ṣāḍguṇyamaya evaiṣāṃ divyo dehaḥ sanātanaḥ || 22 ||

22. buddhiratrāntaḥkaraṇam | naiṣāṃ bhūtamayaḥ iti vacanena na 
bhūtasaṅghasaṃsthāno deho'sya paramātmanaḥ iti śāntiparvavacanaṃ smāritam |

naivaiṣāṃ vāstavo bhedaścintanīyo divaspate [E, ī: vipaścitā] |
tattatkāryaprasiddhyarthaṃ kṛto'sau kalpanāvaśāt || 23 ||

jñānānnānyattathaiśvaryaṃ tasmānnānyā ca śaktikā |
mayaitāḥ kalpitāḥ śakra dhyānaviśrāmabhūmayaḥ [ā, B, c, 
ḍ: dhyeyāḥ-] || 24 ||

24. dhyāneti | upāsakānāṃ yathārucyālambanadānāya tatra 
bhedaparikalpanetyarthaḥ |

vastu pūrvaṃ tato bhāvaḥ paścādarthastataḥ kriyā |
cātūrūpyamidaṃ jñeyaṃ sarvabhāveṣu sarvadā || 25 ||

vāsudevādirūpeṇa caturdhātmānamātmanā |
saṃvibhajyāvatiṣṭhe'haṃ sarvamāvṛtya [ā, c, ḍ: sarvadā; B: 
sarvadā satya] saṃvidā || 26 ||

vāsudevādayo devāḥ pratyekaṃ tu tridhā tridhā |
keśavādisvarūpeṇa vibhajanti svakaṃ vapuḥ || 27 ||

27. evaṃ ca keśavādidāmodarāntā dvādaśāpi vyūhāntarabhūtā dhyeyāḥ |

etadvyūhāntaraṃ nāma pañcarātrābhiśabditam |
kāryasya [E: kālasya] nayane devā dvādaśaite vyavasthitāḥ || 28 ||

vibhorapyaniruddhasya hitāya jagatāṃ hareḥ |
prasaro vibhavo nāma padmanābhādayaḥ smṛtāḥ || 29 ||

āviśyāviśya kurute yatra devanarādikam |
jagaddhitaṃ jagannāthastajjñeyaṃ vibhavāntaram || 30 ||

30. devanarādikam ityanena dattātreyabādarāyaṇādīnāmapi vibhavāntaratvaṃ 
dhyeyam |

devarṣipitṛsiddhādyaiḥ svayaṃ vā jagatāṃ hite |
nirmitaṃ bhagavadrūpamarcā sā śuddhacinmayī || 31 ||

31. svayamityanena svayaṃvyaktakṣetrasthārcā gṛhyante |

ityeṣa leśato mārgaḥ śuddhaste saṃpradarśitaḥ |
traiguṇyamaparaṃ mārgaṃ gadantyā me niśāmaya || 32 ||

yatte jñānaṃ purā proktaṃ tatsattvena vivartate |
rajastayā tadaiśvaryaṃ śaktiścāpi tamastayā || 33 ||

prādhānyena rajastatra sṛṣṭau saṃparivartate |
abhitaḥ sattvatamasī guṇau dvau tasya tiṣṭhataḥ || 34 ||

yā sā pūrvaṃ mayā proktā koṭikoṭitamī kalā |
tasyāḥ koṭitamenāhamaṃśena visṛje jagat || 35 ||

sarvasyādyā mahālakṣmīstriguṇāhaṃ maheśvarī |
rajorūpamadhiṣṭhāya sṛṣṭimiṣṭāṃ karomyaham || 36 ||

36. anena triguṇajagatsṛṣṭikartṛtva daśāyāṃ mahālakṣmīsamākhyeti jñāyate 
|

agnīṣomamayau bhāvau divyau strīpuṃsalakṣaṇau |
bibhratī cārusarvāṅgī lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā || 37 ||

caturbhujā viśālākṣī taptakāñcanasaṃnibhā |
mātulaṅgaṃ gadāṃ kheṭaṃ sudhāpātraṃ ca bibhratī || 38 ||

p. 16)

mahālakṣmīḥ samākhyātā sāhaṃ sarvāṅgasundarī |
mahāśrīḥ sā mahālakṣmīścaṇḍā caṇḍī ca caṇḍikā || 39 ||

bhadrakāli tathā bhadrā kālī durgā maheśvarī |
triguṇā bhagavatpatnī tathā bhagavatī parā || 40 ||

etāḥ saṃjñāstathā cānyāstatra me bahudhā smṛtāḥ |
vikārayogādanyāśca tāstā vakṣyāmyaśeṣataḥ || 41 ||

lakṣayāmi jagatsarvaṃ puṇyāpuṇye kṛtākṛte |
mahanīyā [B: mahatī yā] ca sarvatra mahālakṣmīḥ prakīrtitā || 42 ||

mahadbhiḥ śrayaṇīyatvānmahāśrīriti gadyate [E: gīyate] |
caṇḍasya dayitā caṇḍī caṇḍatvāccaṇḍikā matā || 43 ||

kalyāṇarūpā bhadrāsmi kālī ca kalanātsatām |
dviṣatāṃ kālarūpatvādapi kālī prakīrtitā || 44 ||

suhṛdāṃ dviṣatāṃ caiva yugapatsadasadvidheḥ |
bhadrakālī samākhyātā māyāścaryaguṇātmikā || 45 ||

45. māyāścaryeti | anenāścaryāvahatvaṃ māyāśabdārtha ityuktaṃ bhavati | 
anena māyāśabdasya mithyārthakatvakalpanaṃ vāryate | 

mahattvācca mahāmāyā mohanānmohinī matā |
durgā ca durgamatvena bhaktarakṣāvidherapi || 46 ||

yojanāccaiva yogāhaṃ yogamāyā ca kīrtitā |
māyāyogeti vijñeyā jñānayojanato nṛṇām || 47 ||

47. jñānayojanata iti | anena māyā vayunaṃ jñānam iti yāskavacanāt 
māyāśabdasya jñānārthakatvamuktaṃ bhavati | saṃbhavāmyātmamāyayā ityatra 
ātmasaṃkalpenetyarthavarṇanamapyetanmūlakameva |

pūrṇaṣāḍguṇyarūpatvātsāhaṃ bhagavatī smṛtā |
bhagavadyajñasaṃyogātpatnī bhagavato'smyaham || 48 ||

48. yajñasaṃyogāditi | patyurno yajñasaṃyoge ityanuśāsanasiddhaṃ rūpam | 
bhagavadyajñaśca āśritajanarakṣaṇarūpo jñeyaḥ | tatrāsyāḥ saṃyogaḥ 
puruṣakāratvādinā |

viśālatvātsmṛtā vyoma pūraṇācca purī smṛtā [ā, B, c, ṅ: 
puraḥ sthitā] |
parāvarasvarūpatvāt smṛtā cāhaṃ parāvarā || 49 ||

śakanācchaktiruktāhaṃ rājñyahaṃ rañjanāt [B: rājanāt] sadā |
sadā śāntavikāratvācchāntāhaṃ parikīrtitā || 50 ||  

mattaḥ prakriyate viśvaṃ prakṛtiḥ sāsmi kīrtitā |
śrayantī śrayaṇīyāsmi śṛṇāmi duritaṃ satām || 51 ||

51. śri śrayaṇe śṝ hiṃsāyām iti ca dhātuḥ |

śṛṇomi karuṇāṃ vācaṃ śṛṇāmi ca guṇairjagat |
(śaye'ntaḥ) [āll ṃss: śrayate] sarvabhūtānāṃ rame'haṃ 
puṇyakarmaṇām || 52 ||

52. śru śravaṇe śṝ prīṇane śī svapne ram krīḍāyām iti dhātavo vivakṣitāḥ | 
śrīnāmni śakāravivaraṇam - śaye'ntariti | rephavivaraṇam - rame'hamiti |

īḍitā ca sadā devaiḥ śarīraṃ cāsmi vaiṣṇavam |
etānmayi guṇān dṛṣṭvā vedavedāntapāragāḥ || 53 ||

53. īkāravivaraṇam - īḍiteti | īḍa stutau iti dhātuḥ | viṣṇoḥ śarīraṃ 
rūpamityarthaḥ |

guṇayogavidhānajñāḥ śriyaṃ māṃ saṃpracakṣate |
sāhamevaṃvidhā nityā sarvākārā sanātanī || 54 ||

p. 17)

guṇatrayamadhiṣṭhātrī triguṇā parikīrtitā |
guṇavaiṣamyasargāya [ā, B, c, ḍ: guṇairvaiṣamya-] 
pravṛttāhaṃ sisṛkṣayā || 55 ||

taptakāñcanavarṇābhā taptakāñcanabhūṣaṇā |
nirālokamimaṃ lokaṃ pūrayāmi svatejasā || 56 ||

śūnyaṃ tadakhilaṃ lokaṃ svena pūrayituṃ purā |
bharāmi tvaparaṃ rūpaṃ tamasā kevalena tu || 57 ||

sā bhinnāñjanasaṃkāśā daṃṣṭrāñcitavarānanā [ā, B, c, ḍ: 
daṃṣṭānvita-] |
viśālalocanā nārī babhūva tanumadhyamā || 58 ||

khaḍgapātraśiraḥkheṭairalaṃkṛtamahābhujā |
kabandhahārā śirasi bibhrāṇāhiśiraḥsrajam || 59 ||

sā māṃ provāca saṃbhūtā tāmasī pramadottamā |
[ī: + śāntiḥ] nāma karma ca me mātardehi tubhyaṃ namo namaḥ || 60 ||

śrīḥ [ā, B, c, F: omit śrīḥ] -

tāmabravaṃ varārohāṃ tāmasīṃ pramadottamām |
dadāmi tava nāmāni yāni karmāṇi tāni te || 61 ||

mahākālī mahāmāyā mahāmārī kṣudhā tṛṣā [ā, B, c, F: 
kriyā] |
nidrā kṛṣṇā [ā, B, c, F: tṛṣṇā] caikavīrā 
kālarātrirduratyayā || 62 ||

etāni tava nāmāni pratipādyāni nāmabhiḥ |
ebhiḥ karmāṇi te jñātvā yo'dhīte so'śnute sukham || 63 ||

aparyāptamimaṃ sargaṃ manyamānāhamādimam |
sattvonmeṣamayaṃ rūpaṃ bharāmi smendusaṃnibham || 64 ||

akṣamālāṅkuśadharā vīṇāpustakadhāriṇī |
sā babhūva varā nārī nāma karma tadā hyadām || 65 ||

mahāvidyā mahāvāṇī bhāratī vāk sarasvatī |
āryā brāhmī mahādhenurvedagarbhā ca dhīśca gīḥ [ā, B, c: 
vedabhāgāścagaśva dhīḥ; ī: gīśca dhīḥ] || 66 ||

nāmānurūpaṃ karma syāt [E, ṅ: karmāsyā] sāttvikyāḥ [ā, 
B, c, ḍ: sātvatyāḥ] kāryamadbhutam |
vayaṃ tisro jagaddhātryo mātaraśca prakīrtitāḥ || 67 ||

iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre [ī: śrīpāñcarātre; ā: śrīpañcarātra] 
lakṣmītantre [ā, B, c, ḍ, F: omit the title; ṅ: 
sṛṣṭidvayaprakāśaḥ] mahālakṣmīsamudbhūtirnāma caturtho'dhyāyaḥ || 4 ||
p. 18)

pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ

śrī [B : śrīruvāca] -

yā māhaṃtā harerādyā sarvākārā sanātanī |
śuddhānandacidākārā sarvataḥ samatāṃ gatā || 1 ||

1. atra śāntāvasthā | dvitīyaśloke uditāvasthā | tṛtīyaśloke traiguṇyāvasthā 
mahālakṣmīsamākhyā | caturthe rajaḥpradhānāvasthā mahāśrīḥ 
mahālakṣmyaparaparyāyā | tamaḥpradhānatayā pūrvādhyāyoktā mahāmāyā | 
pañcame pūrvādhyāyoktasattvapradhānā mahāvidyā |

sāhaṃ sisṛkṣayā yuktā svalpālpenātmabindunā |
sṛṣṭiṃ kṛtavatī śuddhāṃ [ī: śuddhā] pūrṇaṣāḍguṇyavigrahām 
[ī: vigrahā] || 2 ||

anucitasyarūpāhaṃ madīyenālpabindunā [ā, B, ḍ: madīyanātma] 
|
mahālakṣmīḥ samākhyātā traiguṇyaparivartinī || 3 ||

rajaḥpradhānā tatrāhaṃ mahāśrīḥ parameśvarī |
madīyaṃ yattamorūpaṃ [ā, E: tattamo-] mahāmāyeti sā smṛtā 
|| 4 ||

madīyaṃ sattvarūpaṃ yanmahāvidyeti sā smṛtā |
ahaṃ ca taṃ ca kāminyau tā vayaṃ tisraṃ ūrjitāḥ || 5 ||

sṛṣṭavanyastu mithunānyarūpāṇi ca tridhā |
madīyaṃ mithunaṃ yattanmānasaṃ rucirākṛti || 6 ||

hiraṇyagrabhaṃ padmāśraṃ sundaraṃ kamalāsanam |
pradyūmrāṃprādidaṃ viddhi saṃbhūtaṃ mayi mānasam || 7 ||

dhātā vidhirviriñciśca brahmā ca puruṣaḥ smṛtaḥ |
śrīḥ padmā kamalā lakṣmīstatra nārī prakīrtitā || 8 ||

saṃkarṣaṇāṃśato dvandvaṃ mahāmāyāsamudbhavam |
trinetraṃ cārusarvāṅgaṃ mānasaṃ tatra yaḥ pumān || 9 ||

sa rudraḥ śaṃkaraḥ sthāṇuḥ kapardī ca trilocanaḥ |
tatra trayīśvarā bhāṣā vidyā caivākṣarā tathā || 10 ||

kāmadhenuśca vijñeyā sā strī gauśca [E, ī: gaurī] sarasvatī |
aniruddhāṃśasaṃbhūtaṃ mahāvidyāsamudbhavam || 11 ||

mithunaṃ mānasaṃ yattatpuruṣastatra keśavaḥ |
viṣṇuḥ kṛṣṇo hṛṣīkeśo vāsudevo janārdanaḥ || 12 ||

umā [ā, B, c, ḍ: mahā] gaurī satī caṇḍā tatra strī subhagā 
satī |
brahmaṇastu trayī patnī sā babhūva mamājñayā || 13 ||

rudrasya dayitā gaurī vāsudevasya cāmbujā |
rajasastamasaścaiva sattvasya ca vivartanam || 14 ||

ādyaṃ parva tadetatte kathitaṃ mithunatrayam |
madhyamaṃ parva vakṣyāmi guṇānāṃ tadidaṃ śṛṇu || 15 ||

15. ādyaṃ parveti | rājamyā lakṣmyā pradyumnāṃśāt mānasī sṛṣṭiḥ - 
viriñciḥ śrīśca | tāmasyā mahāmāyāyāḥ saṃkarṣaṇāṃśāt mānasī sṛṣṭiḥ 
- rudraḥ trayī ca | sāttvikyā mahāvidyāyā aniruddhāṃśāt mānasī sṛṣṭiḥ 
- viṣṇuḥ gaurī ca | tataḥ rājasasya viriñceḥ tāmasyā trayyā tāmasasya 
rudrasya sāttvikyā gauryā sāttvikasya viṣṇoḥ rājasyā śriyā ceti 
dāṃpatyaparikalpanarūpamityarthaḥ | madhyamaṃ parveti | dhātrā aṇḍasṛṣṭiḥ | 
rudreṇa tadbhedanam | tatra punaśca dhātrā aṇḍamadhye pradhānasṛṣṭiḥ | 
viṣṇunā tatparipālanarūpamityarthaḥ |

bhāṣayā saha saṃbhūya viriñco'ṇḍamajījanat |
madājñayā [B, c, ṅ: mamāśayā] bibhedaitatsa gauryā saha 
śaṃkaraḥ || 16 ||

p. 19)

aṇḍamadhye pradhānaṃ yatkāryamāsīttu vedhasaḥ |
tadetatpālayāmāsa padmayā saha keśavaḥ || 17 ||

tadetanmadhyamaṃ parva guṇānāṃ parikīrtitam |
tṛtīyaṃ parva vakṣyāmi tadihaikamanāḥ śṛṇu || 18 ||

18. tṛtīyaṃ parveti | viṣṇornābhisaroruhāt hiraṇyagarbhasya trayyā saha 
prādurbhāvaḥ | nābhipadmaṃ tadudbhūtaṃ dvandvaṃ ceti trayaṃ samuditaṃ 
mahaditi kathyate | tasmādahaṃkārādikrameṇa prapañcasṛṣṭirūpamityarthaḥ |

aṇḍamadhye pradhānaṃ hi yattatsadasadātmakam |
traiguṇyaṃ prakṛtirvyoma svabhāvo yonirakṣaram || 19 ||

tadetatsalilīkṛtya tattvamavyaktasaṃjñakam |
hṛṣīkeśaḥ sa bhagavān padmayā saha vidyayā || 20 ||

apsu [c: abdhi] saṃśayanaṃ cakre nidrāyogamupāgataḥ |
yā sā proktā mahākālī sā nidrā tāmasī hyabhūt || 21 ||

śayānasya tadā padmamabhūnnābhyāṃ puraṃdara |
tatkālamayamākhyātaṃ paṅkajaṃ yadapaṅkajam || 22 ||

[E, ī: kālā] jalādhikaraṇaṃ padmamādhāraḥ puṣkaraṃ tathā |
cakraṃ ca puṇḍarīkaṃ cetyevaṃ nāmāni tasya tu || 23 ||

śakraḥ -

cidacittattvamākhyātaṃ cetanaścitprakīrtitaḥ |
acit traiguṇyamityuktaṃ kīdṛk kālo'paraḥ smṛtaḥ || 24 ||

śrīḥ -

acidaṃśo'paraḥ kālastraiguṇyamaparaṃ smṛtam |
balādikaṃ tu yatpūrvaṃ ṣāḍguṇye trikamīritam || 25 ||

25. trikamiti | balaiśvaryavīryarūpamityarthaḥ |

tadetatkālarūpeṇa sṛṣṭau saṃparivartate |
svataścāpariṇāmīdaṃ [E, ī: apariṇāmo'yaṃ] traiguṇyaṃ pariṇāmi 
tat || 26 ||

kālakālyātmakaṃ [ā, B: kalyātmaka] dvandvamarcidetatprakīrtitam |
sṛjantyā vividhān bhāvānmama devyā mahāśriyaḥ || 27 ||

27. kālaḥ kṣaṇādiḥ | kālyaṃ triguṇaṃ tadvikārajātaṃ ca |

kālo'yaṃ karaṇatvena vartate manmayaḥ sadā |
tasmātkālamayātpadmādviṣṇunābhisamudbhavāt || 28 ||

brahmā vedamayo jajñe sa trayyā saha [c: trayyā saha sa] vīryavān |
hiraṇyagarbha ukto yaḥ [ā: ayaṃ] pūrvaṃ lakṣmīsamudbhavaḥ || 29 ||

mahākālīsamudbhūtā yā sā nārī trayī smṛtā |
tadetanmithunaṃ jajñe [E, ī: cakre] viṣṇornābhisaroruhāt[ā, 
B: nābhīsaroruhāt] || 30 ||

padmaṃ padmodbhavadvandvaṃ [F, ṅ: dharmādikaṃ caiva] tadetat 
tritayaṃ saha |
mahāṃstāmasa ākhyāto vikāraḥ pūrvakairbudhaiḥ [ī: pūrvajo-] || 31 
||

31. padmodbhavadvandvaṃ hiraṇyagarbhastrayī ca |

prāṇo hiraṇyagarbhaśca buddhiśceti tridhā bhidā |
padmapuṃstrīsamālambānmahattvaṃ tasya śabdyate || 32 ||

32. prāṇaḥ hiraṇyagarbhaḥ buddhiriti mahato bhedāḥ |

p. 20)

guṇaḥ prāṇasya tu spando buddheradhyavasāyatā |
dharmādikamadharmādyaṃ dvayaṃ puṃso guṇo mataḥ [ā, B, c: 
guṇottamaḥ] || 33 ||

33. puṃsa iti | hiraṇyagarbhasyetyarthaḥ |

dharmo jñānaṃ ca vairāgyamaiśvaryaṃ ceti varṇitaḥ [E, F: 
varṇitam] |
dharmādiko guṇo yasmādadharmādyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 34 ||

34. yasmāditi | dharmādīnāṃ viparyāsā adharmaḥ ajñānam avairāgyam 
anaiśvaryaṃ ceti jñeyā ityarthaḥ |

mahāntamāviśantyena prerayāmi svasṛṣṭaye [ṅ: sisṛkṣayā] |
preryamāṇāttatastasmādahaṃkāraśca jajñivān || 35 ||

pūrvaṃ yaḥ [E: yaḥ pūrvaṃ] śaṃkaraḥ prokto mahāmāyāsamudbhavaḥ 
|
yā patnī tasya gaurī sā jajñe'bhimatiratra tu || 36 ||

36. ahaṃkārasyaivābhimatiriti guṇaḥ ākhyāntaraṃ ca |

āviśyāmumahaṃkāraṃ sṛṣṭaye prerayāmyaham |
sa babhūva tridhā pūrvaṃ guṇavyatikarāttadā || 37 ||

tāmasastatra bhūtādistasya sarvamidaṃ śṛṇu |
bhūtādeḥ śabdatanmātraṃ tanmātrācchabdasaṃbhavaḥ || 38 ||

38. tāmasāhaṃkārasya bhūtādiriti sāttvikāhaṃkārasya vaikārika iti 
rājasāhaṃkārasya taijasa iti ca nāmāntaraṃ bodhyam |

matpreritācchabdamātrātsparśamātraṃ babhūva ha |
sparśastu sparśatanmātrāttanmātrātpreritānmayā || 39 ||

tadāsīdrūpatanmātraṃ tasmācca preritānmayā |
rūpamāvirbabhūvādyaṃ rasamātraṃ tataḥ param || 40 ||

rasamātrānmayā [E, ī: kṣubdhāt] kṣiptāttasmājjajñe rasastataḥ |
gandhatanmātramapyāsīttasmācca preritānmayā || 41 ||

śuddho gandhaḥ samudbhūta itīyaṃ bhautikī midā |
mātrāṇi sūkṣmabhūtāni sthūlabhūtāni cāpare || 42 ||

śabdādayaḥ samākhyātā guṇāḥ śabdādayastu ye |
sthūlabhūtavisargāste nānye śabdādayo guṇāḥ || 43 ||

śāntatvaṃ caiva ghoratvaṃ mūḍhatvaṃ ceti tat tridhā |
sattvādyunmeṣarūpāṇi tāni sūkṣmeṣu santi na || 44 ||

tena tanmātratā teṣāṃ sūkṣmāṇāṃ parikīrtitā |
sukhaduḥkhādidāyitvāt sthūlatvamitaratra tu || 45 ||

sthūlānāmeva bhūtānāṃ tridhāvasthā [F: avasthāḥ] prakīrtitā 
[ā, F: prakīrtitāḥ] |
sūkṣmāśca pitṛjāścaiva prabhūtā iti bhedataḥ || 46 ||

ghaṭādyā vividhā [E, ī: viṣayāḥ] bāhyāḥ prabhūtā iti śabdyate |
śuklaśoṇitasaṃbhūtā viśeṣāḥ pitṛjāḥ smṛtāḥ || 47 ||

sūkṣmāstu pañcabhūtāḥ syuḥ sūkṣmadehavyapāśrayāḥ |
sargo bhūtādijo hyevaṃ kramaśaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 48 ||

48. śabdatanmātrāt śabdaḥ sparśatanmātraṃ ca jātam | sparśatanmātrāt 
sparśaḥ rūpatanmātraṃ ca jātam | rūpatanmātrāt rūpaṃ rasatanmātraṃ ca jātam | 
rasatanmātrāt rasaḥ gandhatanmātraṃ ca jātam | gandhatanmātrāt gandho jāta 
ityatratyā prakriyā | sāṃkhyāstu - bhūtādereva sarveṣāṃ 
tanmātrāṇāmutpattiṃ vadanti | aupaniṣadaprakriyā tu - bhūtādeḥ 
śabdatanmātram tasmādākāśaḥ tasmāt sparśatanmātram tasmādvāyuḥ tasmāt 
rūpatanmātram tasmādagniḥ tasmāt rasatanmātram tasmāt jalam tasmāt 
gandhatanmātram tasmāt pṛthivī jāteti |

ahaṃkārasya yāvaṃśau rajaḥsattvasamāśrayau |
vaikārika iti proktaḥ sātvikoṃ'śastayoḥ paraḥ [E: aṃśastu yo'paraḥ] || 49 
||

taijasaḥ kathitaḥ sadbhistayoḥ sṛṣṭimimāṃ śṛṇu |
vaikārikādahaṃkārādāsīcchrotrādidhīndriyam || 50 ||

p. 21)

karmendriyaṃ ca vāgādi taijasātsaṃpravartate |
[ā, B, ḍ, F, ṅ: omit 9 lines from here] 
ubhayasmāttataścāsīd buddhikarmendriyaṃ manaḥ || 51 ||

51. vaikārikāt jñānendriyāṇāṃ taijasāt karmendriyāṇāṃ cotpattiriti 
bhedaparikalpanamatra | aupaniṣadāstu - sarveṣāmapīndriyāṇāṃ 
vaikārikādutpattimācakṣate | tathā manasa ubhayendriyatvaṃ sāṃkhyamatarītyā | 
aupaniṣadamate tu jñānendriyatvameva |

śrotraṃ tvak caiva cakṣuśca jihvā ghrāṇaṃ ca pañcamam |
buddhīndriyāṇi pañcāhuḥ śaktireṣā madātmikā || 52 ||

vāk ca hastau ca pādau ca tathopasthaṃ ca pāyu ca |
karmendriyāṇi pañcāhuḥ śaktireṣā madātmikā || 53 ||

yā sā vijñānaśaktirme pāraṃparyakramāgatā |
buddhindriyāṇyadhiṣṭhāya viṣayeṣu pravartate || 54 ||

kriyāśaktiśca yā sā me pāraṃparyakramāgatā |
karmendriyāṇyadhiṣṭhāya kartavyeṣu pravartate || 55 ||

śrotrasya viṣayaḥ śabdaḥ śravaṇaṃ ca kriyā matā |
tvacaśca viṣayaḥ sparśaḥ sparśanaṃ ca kriyā matā || 56 ||

cakṣuṣo viṣayo rūpaṃ darśanaṃ ca kriyā matā |
jihvāyā viṣayo rasyo rasanaṃ ca kriyā matā || 57 ||

ghrāṇasya viṣayo gandha āghrāṇaṃ ca kriyā matā |
vṛttayo viṣayeṣvasya śrotrādeḥ śravaṇādayaḥ || 58 ||

ālocanāni [ā, B, c, ṅ: ālocanādi] kathyante 
dharmimātragrahaśca saḥ |
dik ca vidyuttathā sūryaḥ somo vasumatī tathā || 59 ||

59. ālocanaṃ nāma śabdādidharmāṇāṃ sphuṭagrahaṇamantarā 
vastumātragrahaṇam | tadapyasphuṭameva | āhuśca - asti hyālocanaṃ jñānaṃ 
prathamaṃ nirvikalpakam | bālamūkādivijñānasadṛśaṃ mugdhavastukam | iti |

adhidaivamiti proktaṃ kramācchrotrādipañcake |
[c: omits this and the next line] adhibhūtamiti proktaḥ śabdādyo viṣayaḥ 
kramāt || 60 ||

śrotrādipañcakaṃ tvetadadhyātmaṃ parikīrtitam |
[ṅ: śrotrādisātvikī] śrotrādeḥ sāttvikātsṛṣṭirviyadādivyapekṣayā || 
61 ||

tena bhautikamityuktaṃ kramācchrotrādipañcakam |
vācastu viṣayaḥ śabdo vacanaṃ ca kriyā matā || 62 ||

[F, ṅ: omit this line] hastendriyasya cādeyamādānaṃ ca kriyā matā |
pādendriyasya gantavyaṃ gamanaṃ ca kriyā matā || 63 ||

upasthasya tadānandyamānandaśca kriyā matā |
visṛjyaṃ viṣayaḥ pāyorvisargaśca kriyā matā || 64 ||

hastādikaṃ catuṣkaṃ yattatpañcaviṣayātmakam |
agnirindraśca viṣṇuśca tathaivādyaḥ [ā, ṅ: tathaiva dyauḥ] 
prajāpatiḥ || 65 ||

mitraśceti kramājjñeyā adhidevā vicakṣaṇaiḥ |

so'dhibhūta iti prokto vāgādyadhyātmamucyate |
manastu sahakāryasminnubhayatrāpi pañcake || 67 ||

jñānendriyagaṇaiścaitadvikalpaṃ [ā, B: jñānendriyagaṇe caitat##-
vikalpo vividhā kḷptistacca proktaṃ viśeṣaṇam ||

dharmeṇa saha saṃbandho dharmiṇaśca sa ucyate |
vikalpaḥ pañcadhā jñeyo dravyakarmaguṇādibhiḥ || 69 ||

daṇḍīti dravyasaṃyogācchuklo guṇasamanvayāt |
gacchatīti kriyāyogātpumān sāmānyasaṃsthiteḥ [E: saṃgamāt] || 70 ||

p. 22)

[ā, B, ḍ, F, ṅ: omit four lines from here] ḍitthaḥ

audāsīnyacyutiryā sā saṃkalpodyoganāmikā |

saṃkalpapūrvarūpastu [E: rūpaṃ tu] saṃrambhaḥ [ī: 
saṃbhramaḥ] parikīrtitaḥ |
buddhiradhyavasāyena jñānendriyagaṇe sthitā || 75 ||

buddhiradhyavasāyārthāvadhāraṇamudīryate |
avadhāraṇamarthānāṃ niścayaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 76 ||

karmendriyagaṇe buddhiḥ prayatnena pravartate |
trayodaśavidhaṃ jñeyaṃ [E, ī: tatra] tadetatkaraṇaṃ budhaiḥ || 77 ||

bāhyaṃ daśavidhaṃ jñeyaṃ tridhāntaḥkaraṇaṃ smṛtam |
trayoviṃśatirete tu vikārāḥ [B: virājaḥ] parikīrtitāḥ || 78 ||

karaṇāni daśa trīṇi sūkṣmāṃśāḥ sthūlasaṃbhavāḥ |
etatsūkṣmaśarīraṃ tu virājaḥ parikīrtitam || 79 ||

vyaṣṭayaḥ [E, ī: sṛṣṭayaḥ] sūkṣmadehāśca [ā, B: 
bhedāśca] pratijīvaṃ vyavasthitāḥ |
apavarge nivartante jīvebhyaste svayonijāḥ || 80 ||

anyonyānugraheṇaite trayoviṃśatirutthitāḥ [ā, B, ḍ: ūrjitāḥ] |
mahadādyā viśeṣāntā hyaṇḍamutpādayanti te || 81 ||

tadaṇḍamabhavaddhaimaṃ sahasrāṃśusamaprabham [ṅ: sahasrabham] |
tasmin prajāpatirjajñe virāḍ devaścaturmukhaḥ || 82 ||

82. atra pūrvokta eva sṛṣṭikramo manvādisṛṣṭyā saha saṃkalayyocyate |

virājaśca manurjajñe manoste mānavāḥ smṛtāḥ |

prakāro'yaṃ mamodyatyā leśataste pradarśitaḥ |
svataḥ [F: svacchā] śuddhāpi cicchaktiḥ saṃviddhānādyavidyayā || 84 ||

duḥkhaṃ janmajarādyutthaṃ tatrasthā pratipadyate |
śuddhavijñānasaṃbandhācchuddhakarmasamanvayāt |
yadā dhunotyavidyāṃ tāṃ tadā [E: sānantya] sānandamaśnute || 85 ||

iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre [ā: śrīpañcarātra; ī: śrīpāñcarātre] 
lakṣmītantre prākṛtasṛṣṭiprakāśo [ā: omits the title; E, ṅ, ī: 
brahmādijagatsṛṣṭiḥ] nāma pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ ||
p. 23)

ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ

śrī [B: śrīruvāca] -

pūrṇastimitaṣāḍguṇyacidānandamahodadheḥ |
ahaṃtāhaṃ harerādyā nistaraṅgārṇavākṛteḥ || 1 ||

sāhamevaṃvidhā śuddhā kvaciducchūnatāṃ gatā |
sisṛkṣālakṣaṇā devī svatantrā saccidātmikā || 2 ||

ṣaṭkośatāṃ samāpadye sattāhaṃ vaiṣṇavī parā |
śaktirmāyā prasūtiśca prakṛtistriguṇātmikā || 3 ||

3. śaktimāyāprasūtiprakṛtibrahmāṇḍajīvadehākhyeṣu ṣaṭsu kośeṣu 
śaktikośaḥ śuddhamārgapravartakaḥ | tatraiva saṃkarṣaṇādīnāṃ 
trayāṇāmāvirbhāvaḥ |

brahmāṇḍaṃ jīvadehaścetyete [B: jīvadehāśca] ṣaṭkośasaṃjñitāḥ |
sisṛkṣā yā parā viṣṇorahaṃtāyāḥ samudgatā || 4 ||

śaktiḥ sā prathamaḥ kośaḥ śuddhamārgapravartanī |
kośaḥ kulāyaparyāyaḥ śarīrāparanāmavān || 5 ||

śuddhe'smin prathame kośe prathamonmeṣalakṣaṇe |
ahaṃmānī paro hyāsīddevaḥ saṃkarṣaṇaḥ prabhuḥ || 6 ||

tilakālakavattatra vikāro masṛṇaḥ sthitaḥ |
tasyāhaṃtā tu yā devī [E, ī: devī yā] sāhaṃ sāṃkarṣaṇī [B: 
saṃkarṣaṇī] parā || 7 ||

śrīrityeva [B: evaṃ] samākhyātā vijñānabalaśālinī |
yastasyā me samunmeṣaḥ pradyumnaḥ sa tu kīrtyate || 8 ||

8. saṃkarṣaṇasya mahiṣī śrīḥ | pradyumnasya sarasvatī | aniruddhasya ratiḥ | 
vāsudevasya śāntiḥ |

saṃkarṣaṇasya devasya śaktikośābhimāninaḥ |
buddhitve [ā, B: buddhivat] vartate devaḥ pradyumnaḥ puruṣottamaḥ || 
9 ||

bhoktṛbhogyasamaṣṭistu nilīnā [B: nilīnaṃ] tatra tiṣṭhati |
manobhūtasya devasya tasyāhaṃtā tu yā smṛtā || 10 ||

sāhaṃ sarasvatī nāma [ā, ī: vīryaśaurya] vīryaiśvaryavivartinī |
yo me tasyāḥ samunmeṣaḥ so'niruddhaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 11 ||

tasya saṃkarṣaṇasyāhamahaṃkāravidhau sthitā |
saṃkarṣaṇādayo devāstraya ete purātanāḥ || 12 ||

jīvo buddhirahaṃkāra iti nāmnā prakīrtitāḥ |
naivaite prākṛtā devāḥ kiṃtu śuddhacidātmakāḥ || 13 ||

ādivyūhasya [F: vyūḍhasya] devasya vāsudevasya dīvyataḥ |
tattatkāryakaratvena tattannāmnā nirūpitāḥ || 14 ||

sarve te ṣaḍguṇāḥ proktāḥ sarve te  puruṣottamāḥ |
pūrṇastimitaṣāḍguṇyasadānandamahodadheḥ || 15 ||

ṣaṇṇāṃ yugapadunmeṣo guṇānāṃ [B: unmeṣāt ṣāḍguṇyaṃ] 
kāryavattayā |
yo'bhūtsa vāsudevastu vyūhaḥ prathamakalpitaḥ || 16 ||

tasya śāntirahaṃtā tu sāhaṃ śaktiḥ prakīrtitā |
śaktikośasthitā devāḥ sūyante [E, ī: trayaste] yatra cintitāḥ || 17 ||

aniruddhasya yāhaṃtā ratirityeva saṃjñitā |
saiva devī mahālakṣmīrmāyākośaḥ sa ucyate || 18 ||

18. māyākośamārabhyāśuddhasṛṣṭiḥ | mahālakṣmyā rājasatvaṃ 
prapañcasṛṣṭinidānatvaṃ ca pūrvamuktam |

p. 24)

mahālakṣmyā ya unmeṣo māyāyā guṇasaṃjñitaḥ |
mahākālīmahāvidyādvayaṃ saṃparikīrtyate || 19 ||

mahālakṣmīmahāmāyāmahāvidyāmayo mahān |
prasūtirnāma kośo me tṛtīyaḥ paripaṭhyate || 20 ||

20. prasūtikośaḥ rājasyā mahālakṣmyāḥ tāmasyā mahāmāyāyāḥ sāttvikyā 
mahāvidyāyāśca samavāyarūpaḥ |

trīṇyatra mithunānyāsan yāni pūrvoditāni te |
pradhānaṃ salilīkṛtya yacchete puruṣottamaḥ || 21 ||

21. trīṇi mithunāni pañcamādhyāye saptamādibhiḥ saptabhiḥ ślokairvarṇitāni | 
pradhānameva prakṛtikośa ityucyate | etāsāṃ māyāprasūtiprakṛtīnāṃ 
tamo'vyaktaprakṛtināmabhirupaniṣatsu vyavahāraḥ |

sā proktā prakṛtiryonirguṇasāmyasvarūpiṇī |
viriñco'janayadyadvai pūrvamaṇḍaṃ svamātmani || 22 ||

tadeke prakṛtiṃ prāhustattvaśāstraviśāradāḥ |
mahadādyaiḥ pṛthivyantairaṇḍaṃ yannirmitaṃ saha || 23 ||

tadbrahmāṇḍamiti [E: brahmāṇḍamidaṃ] proktaṃ yatra brahmā 
virāḍabhūt |
aṅgapratyaṅgayuktaṃ yaccharīraṃ [E: jīvatāmiha] jīvināmiha || 24 ||

tasyaivobhayatorūpe śrīśca puṣṭiśca vāsava || 21 ||

caturdiśaṃ tu tasyaiva śrīḥ kīrtiśca jayā tathā |
māyeti kṛtvā rūpāṇi bhujye'haṃ [E: bhuñje'haṃ] tena viṣṇunā || 22 ||

tasyaiva koṇaṣaṭkasthā ṣoḍhāhaṃ śṛṇu nāma ca |
śuddhirnirañjanā nityā jñānaśaktiśca vāsava || 23 ||

tathāparājitā caiva ṣaṣṭhī tu prakṛtiḥ parā |
tasyaiva cāṣṭadhā dikṣu sāhaṃ rūpairvyavasthitā || 24 ||

lakṣmīḥ sarasvatī sarvakāmadā prītivardhanī |
yaśaskarī śāntidā ca tuṣṭidā puṣṭiraṣṭamī || 25 ||

koṇadviṣaṭke [B: koṇaṣaṭke tu] tasyaiva sthitā dvādaśadhāsmyaham 
[B: hyaham; F: koṇaṣaṭke tu tasyaiva ṣaḍbhujasyāhamaṣṭadhā] |
śrīśca kāmeśvarī kāntiḥ [ā: śāntiḥ; F: kīrtiḥ] kriyāśaktivibhūtayaḥ || 
26 ||

icchā prītī ratiścaiva māyā dhīrmahimeti ca |
evaṃ caturbhujasyāpi ṣoḍhāhaṃ kramaśaḥ sthitā [E: tathā] || 27 ||

tasyaiva ṣaḍbhujasyāhamaṣṭabāhośca [B, E: ṣaḍbhujasyaivaṃ] 
vāsava |
dviṣaḍbāhostathā sāhaṃ dvisaptakabhujasya ca || 28 ||

tathā ṣoḍaśahastasya bhujadvinavakasya ca |
vibhajya bahudhātmānamiyadbhedā vyavasthitā || 29 ||

avatāro hi yo viṣṇoḥ sindhuśāyīti saṃjñitaḥ |
sthitāhaṃ paritastasya caturdhā rūpameyuṣī || 30 ||

30. prakṛtikośāvatāramāha - sindhuśāyīti | atra 
pañcamādhyāyaikaviṃśaślokoktamanusaṃdheyam |

lakṣmīrnidrā tathā prītirvidyā ceti vibhedinī |
avatāro hi yo viṣṇoḥ śrīpatirnāma [ā: śaktīśo-] nāmataḥ || 31 ||

śrīrityevākhyayā tatra tasyāhaṃ vāmataḥ sthitā |
avatāro hi yo viṣṇornāmataḥ pārijātacit || 32 ||

tadaṃsasthakarā [ā, ṅ: tadānandakarā] tasya vāmotsaṅge hareḥ 
sthitā |
avatāro hi yo viṣṇornāmnā mīnadharaḥ śubhaḥ || 33 ||

33. brahmāṇḍakośāvatāramāha - nāmnā mīnadhara ityādi |

anubhramāmi [ā, B, ḍ: anugrahakramāttatra] taṃ tatra sāhaṃ 
naurūpadhāriṇī |
traivikramodayo viṣṇoravatāraḥ paraḥ smṛtaḥ || 34 ||

āhlādajananī gaṅgā tatpādātprabhavāmyaham |
anantaśayano nāma yo'vatāro hareraham || 35 ||

p. 31)

sthitā caturdiśaṃ tasya cāturātmyamupeyuṣī |
lakṣmīścintā tathā nidrā puṣṭiścetyākhyayā yutā || 36 ||

ityeṣu saha siddhāhamavatīrṇāṇḍamadhyataḥ |
[ā, B, c, ḍ, F: omit six lines from here] avatārāḥ 
pṛthagbhūtā yadā brahmāṇḍamadhyataḥ || 37 ||

anuvratā tathaivāhamavatīrṇā pṛthak pṛthak |
avatāro hi yo nāma varāho vedaviśrutaḥ || 38 ||

tadāhamapi bhūrnāma pṛthagbhūtā bhajāmyaham |
avatāro hi yo nāma dharmo viṣṇuḥ purātanaḥ || 39 ||

tadāhaṃ bhārgavī nāma khyātijā śrīḥ prakīrtitā |
avatāro hi yo nāma dattātreyo'trinandanaḥ || 40 ||

tadā hi tasya bhogāya saraso'haṃ [F: sarasāhaṃ] samutthitā [B: 
saraḥsthitā] |
avatāro hi yo nāma vāmano vaiṣṇavaḥ śubhaḥ || 41 ||

padmādahaṃ samutpannā tadā padmeti viśrutā |
avatāro yadā viṣṇorbhārgavo rāmasaṃjñitaḥ || 42 ||

tadāhaṃ dharaṇī nāma śaktirāsamayonijā |
avatāro hi yo nāma rāmo dāśarathiḥ śubhaḥ || 43 ||

jātā janakayajñe'haṃ kṣetrāddhalamukhakṣatāt |
nāmnā sīteti vikhyātā daśānanavināśanī || 44 ||

avatāro hi yo viṣṇoścaturdhā saṃbhaviṣyati |
madhurāyāmahaṃ vyaktiṃ caturdhaiṣyāmi vai tathā || 45 ||

revatī rukmiṇī caiva ratirnāmnā tathā hyuṣā |
avatārāntaraṃ yattu mohanaṃ buddhasaṃjñakam || 46 ||

tārāhaṃ tatra nāmnā vai dhārā [B, E: dharā; ṅ: dārā] caiva 
prakīrtitā |
dhruvādayo'vatārā ye kevalā vaiṣṇavāḥ smṛtāḥ || 47 ||

47. jīvadehakośāvatāramāha - dhruvādaya iti |

tattaccharīrabhūtāhaṃ teṣāṃ bhogyā vyavasthitā |
yattu me mohanaṃ [ḍ, E: tanmohanaṃ; F: tanmohinī] rūpaṃ 
śrūyate'mṛtadhārakam || 48 ||

bhavadbhāvau tadā tatra rūpe tulyopalakṣitau |
devaiḥ puruṣarūpeṇa strīrūpeṇa tathetaraiḥ || 49 ||

saha siddhaṃ pṛthaksiddhamityetajjanma me'dbhutam [ā, ṅ: 
mayītyetajanma me mahadbhutam] |
kīrtitaṃ tava deveśa kevalaṃ janma me śṛṇu || 50 ||

iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre [ā: śrīpañcarātra; E, ī: śrīpañcarātre] 
lakṣmītantre lakṣmyavatāraprakāśo [E, ī: omit the title] 
nāmāṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ || 8 ||
p. 32)

navamo'dhyāyaḥ 

śrīḥ [B: śrīruvāca]-

ahaṃ nārāyaṇī devī nārāyaṇamanuvratā |
jñānānandakriyātmānaṃ jñānānandakriyāmayī || 1 ||

tasyā [ā, B, ḍ: tasya] me na vinābhāvastena vā tasya vā mayā |
prakartuṃ [E, F: kartuṃ hi] śakyate kāle kasmiṃściddeśa eva vā || 2 
||

tattatkāryavaśāccaivānyadbhūtādbhutarūpakau |
ātmayogabalāttau svaḥ [ī: tu] sahaiva ca vinaiva ca || 3 ||

brahmādirdattavān yādṛktapoyogabalātkṛtaḥ |
daityādibhyo jagaddhvaṃsakarebhyo varamuttamam [ā, F: varānāśu 
jagaddhvaṃsijighāṃsayā] || 4 ||

tādṛśaṃ tādṛśaṃ rūpamāsthāyāvāṃ sanātanau |
tattatprīticikīrṣāyai carāvo devakāryataḥ || 5 ||

māyayā bhāvamācchādya paramārthaṃ svatejasā |
ahamevāvatīrṇā hi tattaddhvaṃsijighāṃsayā || 6 ||

ādau devī mahālakṣmīḥ smṛtāhaṃ parameśvarī |
abhūvaṃ ca punardvedhā kṛṣṇā brāhmīti rūpataḥ || 7 ||

guṇatrayavibhāgena rūpametatparaṃ mama |
mahālakṣmīrahaṃ śakra punaḥ svāyaṃbhuve'ntare || 8 ||

hitāya sarvalokānāṃ [ā, B, ḍ, F, ṅ: devānāṃ] jātā 
mahiṣamardanī |
madīyā śaktileśā ye tattaddevaśarīragāḥ || 9 ||

saṃbhūya te mamābhūvan rūpaṃ paramaśobhanam |
āyudhāni ca devānāṃ yāni yāni sureśvara || 10 ||

tacchaktayastadākārā āyudhāni mamābhavan |
abhiṣṭutā suraiḥ sāhaṃ mahiṣaṃ jaghnuṣī kṣaṇāt || 11 ||

mahiṣāntakarīsūktaṃ sarvasiddhipradaṃ tadā [ā, B, ī: tathā] |
devyā yayādikaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ sendrairdevaiḥ saharṣibhiḥ [B: maharṣibhiḥ] 
|| 12 ||

12. idaṃ ca sūktaṃ mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe devīmāhātmye caturthādhyāye paṭhitaṃ 
draṣṭavyam |

utpattiṃ [E, ī: utpattiḥ] yuddhavikrāntiṃ [E, ī: 
vikrāntiḥ] stotraṃ ceti sureśvara |
kathayanti suvistīrṇaṃ brāhmaṇā vedapāragāḥ || 13 ||

[ā, B, c, ḍ: omit this line] evaṃprabhāvāṃ devīṃ tāṃ 
stuvan dhyāyannamannapi |
labhate ca phalaṃ śaśvadādhipatyamanaśvaram || 14 ||

yoganidrā hareruktā yā sā devī duratyayā |
mahākālītanuṃ [B, c, E, ī: mahākālyāstanuṃ] viddhi tāṃ 
māṃ devīṃ sanātanīm || 15 ||

madhukaiṭabhanāśe hi mohitau tau [ā, B, ḍ: ca] tayā tadā |
jaghnāte varalābhena devadevena viṣṇunā || 16 ||

viśveśvaryādikaṃ sūktaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ tad brahmaṇā sadā [ṅ: purā] |
stutaye yoganidrāyā mama devyāḥ puraṃdaraḥ || 17 ||

17. idaṃ copākhyānaṃ mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe devīmāhātmye prathamādhyāye 
paṭhitam |

eṣā sā vaiṣṇavī māyā mahākālī duratyayā |
stutyā vaśīkṛtā kuryādvaśe stotuścarācaram || 18 ||

p. 33)

asyā devyāḥ samutpattiścaritaṃ stotramityapi |
hitāya sarvabhūtānāṃ dhāryante [ḍ, E: dhyāyante] brahmavādibhiḥ || 
19 ||

tāmase [ā, B, c: tāpase] tvantare śakra mahāvidyā hi yā 
[B, ī: bhidhā] parā |
gaurīdehātsamudbhūtā kauśikīti tadā hyaham [c: tadāpyaham] || 20 ||

vadhāya duṣṭadaityānāṃ tathā [ā, B, c: tadā] 
sumbhanisumbhayoḥ |
rakṣaṇāya ca lokānāṃ devānāmupakāriṇī || 21 ||

madīyāḥ śaktayo yāstā devaśreṣṭhaśarīragāḥ |
tāstāstadrūpadhāriṇyaḥ sāhāyyaṃ [ā, ḍ: sahāyaṃ] vidadhurhi me || 
22 ||

tābhirnihatya daityendrān hantavyā ye tathā tathā |
saṃhṛtyātmani tāḥ sarvā madīyā vipruṣo'khilāḥ || 23 ||

ahaṃ nijaghnuṣī paścāttayoḥ sumbhanisumbhayoḥ |
devi prapannātihare prasīdetyādikaṃ tathā || 24 ||

24. nārāyaṇīstutiḥ mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe devīmāhātmye ekādaśādhyāye paṭhitā |

nārāyaṇīstutirnāma sūktaṃ paramaśobhanam |
stutayo me tadā [E: sadā] dṛṣṭā devairvahnipurogamaiḥ || 25 ||

eṣā saṃpūjitā bhaktyā sarvajñatvaṃ prayacchati |
kauśikī sarvadeveśa [ā, B, ṅ: deveśā] bahukāmapradā hyaham 
|| 26 ||

utpattiryuddhavikrāntiḥ stutiśceti purātanaiḥ |
paṭhyate [F: pāṭhyate] tritayaṃ viprairvedavedāṅgapāragaiḥ [E: 
vedāntapāragaiḥ] || 27 ||

vaivasvate'ntare caitau [B: śakraḥ] punaḥ sumbhanisumbhakau |
utpatsyete varonmattau devopadravakāriṇau || 28 ||

nandagopakule jātā yaśodāgarbhasaṃbhavā |
tāvahaṃ nāśayiṣyāmi sunandā [E: sunandyā] vindhyavāsinī || 29 ||

punaścāpyatiraudreṇa rūpeṇa pṛthivītale |
avatīrya haniṣyāmi vaipracittān mahāsurān || 30 ||

[B: omits this line] bhakṣayantyāśca tānugrān [F: 
bhakṣayantyāgatānugrān] vaipracittān mahāsurān |
raktā dantā bhaviṣyanti dāḍimīkusumopamāḥ || 31 ||

tato māṃ devatāḥ sarve martyaloke ca mānavāḥ |
stuvanto vyāhariṣyanti satataṃ raktadantikām [ā, c: ratna-] || 32 ||

tasminnevāntare śakra catvāriṃśattame yuge |
sarvataḥ śatavārṣikyāmanāvṛṣṭyāmanambhasi || 33 ||

munibhiḥ saṃsmṛtā bhūmau saṃbhaviṣyāmyayonijā |
tataḥ śatena netrāṇāṃ nirīkṣiṣyāmyahaṃ munīn [E: nirīkṣiṣyāmi 
yanmunīn] || 34 ||

kīrtayiṣyanti māṃ śakra śatākṣīmiti mānavāḥ |
tadāhamakhilaṃ lokamātmadehasamudbhavaiḥ || 35 ||

bhariṣyāmi śubhaiḥ śākairāviṣṭaiḥ prāṇadhārakaiḥ [ḍ: dhāraṇaiḥ] |
śākaṃbharīti māṃ devāstadā stoṣyanti vāsava || 36 ||

tatraiva ca haniṣyāmi durgamākhyaṃ mahāsuram |
durgādevīti vikhyātiṃ tato yāsyāmyahaṃ bhuvi || 37 ||

śākaṃbharīṃ stuvan dhyāyan [B: tu saṃdhyāyāṃ] śakra 
saṃpūjayannaman |
akṣayyāmaśnute [ā, B, c: akṣayā] śīghramannapānodbhavāṃ 
ratim || 38 ||

caturyuge ca pañcāśattame munibhirarthitā |
sundaraṃ cātibhīmaṃ ca rūpaṃ kṛtvā himācale || 39 ||

rakṣāṃsi bhakṣayiṣyāmi munīnāṃ trāṇakāraṇāt |
tato māṃ munayaḥ sarve stoṣyantyānamramūrtayaḥ || 40 ||

p. 34)

bhīme devi prasīdeti bhīmāmabhayadāyinīm |
yuge ṣaṣṭitame kaścidaruṇo nāma dānavaḥ || 41 ||

manujānāṃ munīnāṃ ca mahābādhāṃ kariṣyati |
tatrāhaṃ bhrāmaraṃ [E, F: bhramaraṃ] rūpaṃ 
kṛtvāsaṃkhyeyaṣaṭpadā || 42 ||

trailokyasya hitārthāya vadhiṣyāmi mahāsuram |
bhrāmarīti ca māṃ lokāstadā stoṣyanti sarvadā [ā: saṃyatāḥ] || 43 ||

28-43. sunandā-raktadantikā-śatākṣī-śākambharī-durgā##-
ekādaśādhyāye draṣṭavyāni |

itthaṃ yadā yadā bādhā dānavotthā bhaviṣyati |
tadā tadāvatīryāhaṃ haniṣyāmi mahāsurān || 44 ||

amī te leśataḥ śakra darśitāḥ paramādbhutāḥ |
avatārā nirātaṅkā madīyāḥ kevalāhvayāḥ || 45 ||

eteṣāṃ paramā proktā kūṭasthā sā mahīyasī |
mahālakṣmīrmahābhāgā prakṛtiḥ parameśvarī || 46 ||

amuṣyāḥ stutaye dṛṣṭaṃ brahmādyaiḥ sakalaiḥ suraiḥ |

47. idaṃ ca mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe devīmāhātmye pañcamādhyāye paṭhitam |

imāṃ devīṃ stuvannityaṃ stotreṇānena māmiha |
[B, F: omit this line] kleśānatītya sakalānaiśvaryaṃ mahadaśnute ||

kathitāni purā śakra vasiṣṭhena mahātmanā |
svārociṣe'ntare rājñe surathāya mahātmane || 50 ||

50. idaṃ copākhyānaṃ mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe devīmāhātmye prathamādhyāye 
dṛśyate |

samādhaye ca vaiśyāya [B: vaśyāya] praṇatāyāvasīdate |

hṛdi sthitā sadā seyaṃ janmakarmāvalistutiḥ |
etāṃ dvijamukhācchrutvā hyadhīyāno naraḥ sadā || 52 ||

[c: omits this line] vidhūya nikhilāṃ māyāṃ samyagjñānaṃ samaśnute |
sarvāṃ [E, ī: sarva] saṃpadamāpnoti dhunoti sakalāpadaḥ || 53 ||

mama prabhāvāt saubhāgyaṃ kīrtiṃ caiva samaśnute |
kevalā api yadyete [ā, B, c: kevalāḥ kathayantyete] madīyā

p. 35)

daśamo'dhyāyaḥ

śakraḥ -

kṣīrodamathanāyāsaphalarūpe madhudviṣaḥ |
namaścandrasahodaryai namaste'mṛtayonaye || 1 ||

bhāvottarāḥ prakārāste śrutāstvadvaktrapaṅkajāt |
idānīṃ śrotumicchāmi prakārān bhavaduttarān || 2 ||

vaiṣṇavā avatārāste kiṃrūpāḥ kati vāmbuje [E: kathitā budhaiḥ] |
etatpṛṣṭā mayā brūhi namaste paṅkajāsane || 3 ||

śrīḥ -

hanta te [B, c: ahaṃ te] śakra vakṣyāmi prakārān bhavaduttarān |
vaiṣṇavā avatārāste yāvanto yadvidhāśca te || 4 ||

ṣāḍguṇyamamalaṃ brahma nirdoṣamajaraṃ dhruvam |
sarvaśakti nirātaṅkaṃ nirālambanabhāvanam || 5 ||

tadunmiṣati vai pūrvaṃ śaktimacchaktibhāvataḥ |
nārāyaṇaḥ paro devaḥ saṃsthitaḥ śaktimattayā || 6 ||

sthirā śaktirahaṃ tasya sarvakāryakarī vibhoḥ |
tāvāvāṃ jagato'rthāya bahudhā vikriyāvahe || 7 ||

yathāhamāsthitā bhedaistathā te kathitaṃ purā |
vikārānavikārasya viṣṇoḥ śṛṇu mayoditān || 8 ||

aprākṛtānanaupamyānacintyamahimojjvalān |
svāṃ śaktiṃ māmadhiṣṭhāya prakṛtiṃ paramādbhutām || 9 ||

trairūpyeṇa jagannāthaḥ samudeti jagaddhite |
ādyena pararūpeṇa vyūharūpeṇa cāpyatha || 10 ||

tathā vibhavarūpeṇa nānābhāvamupeyuṣā |
vyāpako bhagavān devo bhaktānugrahakāmyayā || 11 ||

anaupamyamanirdaśyaṃ vapuḥ [ā, B, ṅ, ī: punaḥ] sa bhajate 
param |
viśvāpyāyanakaṃ [E, ī: karaṃ] kāntyā pūrṇendvayutatulyayā || 12 ||

varadābhayahastaṃ ca dvibhujaṃ padmalocanam |
rekhāmayena cakreṇa śaṅkhena ca karadvaye || 13 ||

aṅkitaṃ nirvikārāṅghristhitaṃ paramaśobhanam |
anyūnānatiriktaiḥ svairguṇaiḥ ṣaḍbhiralaṃkṛtam || 14 ||

samaṃ samavibhaktāṅgaṃ sarvāvayavasundaram |
pūrṇamābharaṇaiḥ śubhraiḥ sudhākallolasaṃkulaiḥ || 15 ||

raśmibhūtairamūrtaiḥ svairacyutādyairavicyutam |
ekā mūrtiriyaṃ divyā parākhyā vaiṣṇavī parā || 16 ||

yogasiddhā bhajantyenāṃ hṛdi turyapadāśritām [E, F: śrayām] |
atha vyūhasvarūpaṃ te dvitīyaṃ varṇayāmyaham || 17 ||

vyūhyātmānaṃ caturdhā svaṃ devaḥ prāgādibhedataḥ [ā: bhedinaḥ] |
vāsudevādibhedena sauṣuptādhvani [B: suṣuptyadhvani; E: suṣuptātmani] 
tiṣṭhati || 18 ||

saṃsthānamādimūrtervai [ā, B, c: sṛṣṭyādisaṃsthā 
mūrtirvai] sarveṣāṃ tu samaṃ smṛtam |
ṣaḍguṇaṃ prathamaṃ rūpaṃ dvandvairjñānādisaṃbhavaiḥ || 19 ||

12. viśākhayūpo nāmāprākṛte divyaloke bhrājamāno jyotirmayaḥ stambhākāro

54. kleśakarmāśayā anantarādhyāye vakṣyante |

dvādaśo'dhyāyaḥ 

śakraḥ -

cicchaktireva te śuddhā yadi jīvaḥ sanātanaḥ |
kleśakarmāśayasparśaḥ kathamasya saroruhe || 1 ||

kleśāḥ ke kati te proktāḥ karma kīdṛk ca kiṃvidham |
āśayo [E, ī: āśayā] nāma ko [E, ī: ke] devi tadetat 
[F: tadevaṃ] kiṃ phalaṃ smṛtam || 2 ||

sindhukanye tadetanme brūhi tubhyaṃ namo namaḥ |
sarvajñe na tvadanyena vaktumetaddhi śakyate || 3 ||

śrīḥ -

ahaṃ nārāyaṇī devī svacchasvacchandacinmayī [ā, B, ṅ: svacchā##-
svatantrā niravadyāhaṃ viṣṇoḥ śrīranapāyinī || 4 ||

īśeśitavyabhedena dvidhā rūpaṃ mayā kṛtam |
īśitavyaṃ ca tadbhinnaṃ svācchandyādeva [ā, B, c: svacchandā##-

cicchaktirekā bhoktrākhyā parā bhogyādirūpiṇī |
kālakālyavibhedena sā dvidhā bheditā mayā || 6 ||

tatra kālyātmikā śaktirmohinī bandhanī tathā |
prakṛtiḥ savikāraiṣā cicchaktirbadhyate'nayā || 7 ||

kliśyate yena rūpeṇa cicchaktirbhoktṛtāṃ gatā |
sa kleśaḥ pañcadhā jñeyo nāmānyasya ca me śṛṇu || 8 ||

tamo moho mahāmohastāmisro hyandhasaṃjñitaḥ |
avidyā pañcaparvaiṣā tamaso gatiruttamā || 9 ||

asaṅginyapi cicchaktiḥ śuddhāpyapariṇāminī [ā, B, ṅ: 
śuddhātma-] |
āviddhamātmano rūpaṃ nairmalyena [c, ḍ: saṃkleśena] vibharti sā || 
10 ||

śakraḥ -

vyāhatāmiva paśyāmi cicchakteḥ [ḍ: cicchaktiṃ] kleśasaṅgitām |
muhyatīva mano me'dya taṃ mohaṃ chindhi padmaje || 11 ||

śrīḥ -

svatantrā sarvasiddhīnāṃ [ā, B, c, ḍ, ī: viśvasiddhānāṃ; 
ṅ: viśvasiddhīnāṃ] hetuścātra [E, ī: citrā] mahādbhutā |
śaktirnārāyaṇasyāhaṃ nityā devī sadoditā || 12 ||

tasyā me pañca karmāṇi nityāni tridaśeśvara |
tirobhāvastathā sṛṣṭiḥ sthitiḥ saṃhṛtireva ca || 13 ||

anugraha iti proktaṃ madīyaṃ karmapañcakam |
eteṣāṃ kramaśo vyākhyāṃ karmaṇāṃ śakra me śṛṇu || 14 ||

tatra nāma tirobhāvo'nyadbhāvaḥ parikīrtyate |
svacchāpi sā madīyā hi cicchaktirbhoktṛsaṃjñitā || 15 ||

madīyayā yayā śaktyā vartate prakṛtervaśe |
tirobhāvābhidhānā me sāvidyāśaktirucyate || 16 ||

madīyaṃ bheditaṃ rūpaṃ satyasaṃkalpayā mayā |
yo'varoho madīyaste varṇitaḥ prathamaḥ purā || 17 ||

cicchaktirjīva ityevaṃ vibudhaiḥ parikīrtyate |
matsvācchandyavaśādeva bhedaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 18 ||

madīyaṃ caityarūpaṃ yat satyasaṃkalpayā kṛtam |
mayā tadekīkaraṇaṃ cicchakteḥ kriyate hi yat || 19 ||

avidyā sā parā śaktistirobhāva iti smṛtaḥ |
pañca parvāṇi tasyāstu santi tāni [ā, B, c, F: saṃtatāni] 
nibodha me || 20 ||

p. 41)

tamastu prathamaṃ parva nāmāvidyeti tasya tu |
anātmanyasvabhūte ca caitye jīvasya yā matiḥ || 21 ||

svatayāhaṃtayā caiva tamo'vidyā ca sā smṛtā |
svīkṛte'haṃtayā caitye māno yastatra jāyate || 22 ||

asmitākhyo mahāmoho dvitīyaṃ kleśaparva tat |
caityacetanayorekabhāvāpattiravidyayā || 23 ||

moho'smitā [ā, E, ī: rāgo-] mahāmoha iti śabdairnigadyate |
sukhānusmṛtiheturyā vāsanāsmitayāhitā || 24 ||

sa rāgo [ā, B, c, ḍ, ī: ramya] rañjyaviṣayastṛtīyaṃ 
kleśaparva tat |
duḥkhānusmṛtiheturyā vāsanāsmitayāhitā || 25 ||

sa dveṣo dveṣyaviṣayaścaturthaṃ kleśaparva tat |
[F: omits 3 lines from here] duḥkhaṃ jihāsato yogaiḥ prepsataśca sukhaṃ 
tathā || 26 ||

tadantarāyairvitrāso madhye yo nāma jāyate |
andhākhyo'bhiniveśaḥ sa pañcamaṃ kleśaparva tat || 27 ||

dehamātmatayā [ā, B, c: dehaṃ cātma] buddhvā 
tatastādātmyamāgataḥ |
rañjanīyamabhiprepsurjihāsuśca tathetarat || 28 ||

tadantarāyavitrastastatpratīkāramācaran |
iṣṭasya prāptaye'niṣṭavidhātāya ca cetanaḥ [E: dehinaḥ] || 29 ||

yadayaṃ kurute karma trividhaṃ trividhātmakam |
tatkarma gaditaṃ sadbhiḥ sāṃkhyayogavicakṣaṇaiḥ || 30 ||

tatprasūtaṃ sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ tathā duḥkhasukhātmakam |
vipākastrividhaḥ proktastattvaśāstraviśāradaiḥ || 31 ||

vāsanā āśayāḥ proktāḥ kleśakarmavipākajāḥ |
antaḥkaraṇavartinyaḥ samantāccherate hi tāḥ || 32 ||

abhidhānayā] |

ṣaṭkośasaṃbhavāstvanyāstattatkālasamudbhavāḥ |

virājaśca tathānyeṣāṃ bhūtānāṃ liṅgajā tu sā |
mahadādyā viśeṣāntā viṃśatiśca trayaśca ye || 41 ||

padārthā liṅgadehasthā [E: dehāste] virājaḥ parikīrtitāḥ |
khānāṃ samaṣṭibhūtānāṃ tathāntaḥkaraṇasya ca || 42 ||

sthūlānāṃ caiva bhūtānāṃ ye sūkṣmāḥ kīrtitāḥ purā || 43 ||

vyaṣṭayo'ṣṭādaśemāśca kleśāḥ karmāṇi vāsanāḥ |

śuddhe hi bhagavajjñāne jāte satkarmajīvinām || 45 ||

jīvānāṃ vinivartante liṅgānyetāni nānyadā [B: nānyathā] |
virājaḥ sthūladeho yo brahmāṇḍāparanāmavān || 46 ||

p. 42)

caturvidhāni cānyāni śarīrāṇi śarīriṇām |

trailokyaviṣayā sā tu brahmaprasvāpahetukā |

trayodaśo'dhyāyaḥ

śrīḥ -

anugrahātmikā śakra śaktirme pañcamī smṛtā |
tāmimāṃ tattvato vatsa vadāmi tava sāṃpratam || 1 ||

avidyayā samāviddhā asmitādivaśīkṛtāḥ |
macchaktyaiva tirobhūtāstirodhānābhidhānayā || 2 ||

uccānnīce patantaste nīcādutpatayālavaḥ |
nibaddhāstrividhairbandhaiḥ sthānatrayavivartinaḥ || 3 ||

saṃsārāṅgāramadhyasthāḥ pacyamānāḥ svakarmaṇā |
sukhābhimānino duḥkhe nityamajñānadharṣitāḥ || 4 ||

[ā, B, c: tayorniranu] tā yonīranudhāvantaścarācaravibhedinīḥ 
[ā, B, c, F, ṅ: bhedinaḥ] |
apūrvāpūrvabhūtābhiścitritābhiḥ svahetubhiḥ || 5 ||

dehendriyamanobuddhivedanābhiraharniśam |
jananāni prabadhnanto maraṇāni tathā tathā || 6 ||

kliśyamānā iti kleśaistaistairyogaviyogajaiḥ |
udyatkāruṇyasaṃtānanirvāpitatadāgasā || 7 ||

mayā jīvāḥ samīkṣyante śriyā duḥkhavivarjitāḥ |
so'nugraha iti proktaḥ śaktipātāparāhvayaḥ [E, ī: pātaparāhvayaḥ] || 
8 ||

karmasāmyaṃ bhajantyete prekṣyamāṇā mayā tadā |
apaścimā tanuḥ sā syājjīvānāṃ prekṣitā mayā || 9 ||

ahameva hi jānāmi [E: jānāni] śaktipātakṣaṇaṃ ca tam [E: 
kṣaṇāñcitam] |
nāsau puruṣakāreṇa na cāpyanyena hetunā || 10 ||

kevalaṃ svecchayaivāhaṃ [E: ekā] prekṣe kaṃcit kadāpyaham |
tataḥ prabhṛti sa svacchasvacchāntaḥkaraṇaḥ pumān || 11|| 

karmasāmyaṃ samāsādya śuklakarmavyapāśrayaḥ |
vedāntajñānasaṃpannaḥ sāṃkhyayogaparāyaṇaḥ || 12 ||

samyaksāttvatavijñānādviṣṇau sadbhaktimudvahan |
kālena mahatā yogī nirdhūtakleśasaṃcayaḥ || 13 ||

vidhūya vividhaṃ bandhaṃ dyotamānastatastataḥ |
prāpnoti paramaṃ brahma lakṣmīnārāyaṇātmakam || 14 ||

eṣā tu pañcamī śaktirmadīyānugrahātmikā |
svācchandyameva me hetustirobhāvādikarmaṇi || 15 ||

itthaṃ śakra vijānīhi nānuyojyamataḥ [E: itaḥ; ī: yojyāsmyataḥ] param |

śakraḥ -

namaḥ saroruhāvāse namo nārāyaṇāśraye || 16 ||

namo nityānavadyāyai kalyāṇaguṇasindhave |
tvadvāgamṛtasaṃdohakṣālitaṃ me mahattamaḥ || 17 ||

[B: omits verses 18 and 19] bhūyo'haṃ śrotumicchāmi cicchakte 
rūpamuttamam |

śrīḥ - 

eko nārāyaṇo devaḥ paramātmā sanātanaḥ || 18 ||

anādiraparicchedyo deśakālasvarūpataḥ || 19 ||

p. 44)

tasyāhaṃ paramā devī ṣāḍguṇyamahimojjvalā |

nirbhayā] |
siddhayo viśvajīvānāmāyatante'khilā mayi || 21 ||

tayā [E: tathā] sphurati jīvo'sau svata evānurūpayā [E: rūpavān] |

yā vṛttirnīlapītādau [E, ī: bhittau nīlapītādi] sṛṣṭiḥ sā kathitā

gṛhītādviṣayādyo'sya virāmo'nyajighṛkṣayā |
sā saṃhṛtiḥ samākhyātā tattvaśāstraviśāradaiḥ || 28 ||

tadvāsanā tirobhāvo'nugrahastadvilāpanam |
grāhyagrasanaśīlo'yaṃ [ā, B, c: grāhyāśanīya] 
vahnivadgrasanaḥ [ā, B, c: chedanaḥ] sadā || 29 ||

puṣyatyeṣa sadā jīvo mātrayā me samindhanam |

cakṣuṣālokya vastūni vikalpya manasā tathā |
abhimatyāpyahaṃkārād buddhyaiva hyadhyavasyati || 34 ||

jāgarāyāmatha [ā, ṅ: jāgarāntaṃ yathā] svapne 
karaṇairāntaraiścaran |
vihāya tat suṣuptau tu svarūpeṇāvatiṣṭhate || 35 ||

avasthāstā imāstisraḥ prākṛtyo [ā, B, c, ḍ: prakṛtyā]

śrīḥ [B, E: śrīruvāca]

vyāpāraśaktireṣā me sisṛkṣālakṣaṇā bhavet [F, ṅ, ī: bhavat] |
ayutāyutakoṭyoghakoṭikoṭyayutāṃśataḥ [ā, B: ayutāyutakoṭyāśca##-

p. 46)

p. 47)

kāpyavasthā na me sāsti [ā, B, c: tu me nāsti] yasyāṃ saṃvinna
