vidyādharāḥ piśācāśca bhūtāśceti gaṇāṣṭakam |
manujā bahudhātmāno varṇakarmādibheditāḥ || 48 ||

paśavo'tha mṛgāścaiva pakṣiṇaśca sarīsṛpāḥ [E, ī: 
sasarīsṛpāḥ] |
sthāvarāśca tathaivānye kapūyacaraṇātmakāḥ || 49 ||

svargasthā narakasthāśca lokāścaiva caturdaśa |
sariddvīpasamudrāśca vividhā hyaṇḍapaddhatiḥ || 50 ||

uccāvacāni tattvāni vividhāḥ śabdarāśayaḥ |
bhogyaṃ bhogopakaraṇaṃ bhogasthānaṃ ca yat smṛtam || 51 ||

kośāḥ ṣaṭ kośajāścaiva [ā, B, c, F: ete] 
cetanācetanātmakāḥ |
śuddhāśuddhamayau bhāvau puruṣārthaścaturvidhaḥ || 52 ||

sarvaṃ prakṛtibhirnaddhaṃ kālena kalitaṃ tathā |
ityetatsakalaṃ vastu bhāvābhāvasvarūpakam || 53 ||

amanmayaṃ manmayaṃ ca mayi līnamavasthitam |
sarvātmanā sadaivāhaṃ svacchasvacchandacinmayī || 54 ||

lakṣyā sukhamayī śāntā bhāve bhāve vipaścitā |
evaṃ vyavasthitāyā me tirobhāvābhidhānayā || 55 ||

baddhā śaktyā tu cicchaktiḥ svato māṃ naiva vindati |
yadā nirvidyate sāsau [B: vāsau] madanugrahabindunā || 56 ||

upāyairmāṃ tadārādhya [B: māntarārādhya] jīvaścicchaktisaṃjñakaḥ |
saṃkṣiṇvan nikhilānkleśānvidhūnvanvāsanārajaḥ [E: sakalān kośān; 
ṅ, ī: nikhilān kośān] || 57 ||

saṃprāpya jñānasadbhāvaṃ yogakṣapitabandhanaḥ [E, ī: kalmaṣaḥ] 
|
māmeva paramānandamayīṃ lakṣmīṃ sa vindati || 58 ||

iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre [F: śrīpañcarātre; ī: śrīpāñcarātre sāre] 
lakṣmītantre lakṣmīsvarūpaprakāśo [ā, ṅ, ī: omit lakṣmī] nāma 
caturdaśo'dhyāyaḥ || 14 ||
p. 48)

pañcadaśo'dhyāyaḥ

śakraḥ -

namaste padmasaṃbhūte namaḥ kamalamālini |
namaḥ kamalavāsinyai [F: mālinyai] govindagṛhamedhini || 1 ||

namaste kaṃjakiñjalkakalpitālakavibhrame |
sarvajñe sarvabhūtānāmantaḥsthe sarvasākṣiṇi || 2 ||

tvadvaktrakamalodbhūtaṃ sarvaṃ tadavadhāritam |
tattvatsṛṣṭaṃ tvayā trātaṃ tvayyeva layameṣyati || 3 ||

mātā mānaṃ mitirmeyaṃ vidhā etāstvadātmikāḥ |
tvāmevārādhya jīvāste taranti bhavasāgaram || 4 ||

evamādi mayā devi tattvatastvavadhāritam |
kautūhalamidaṃ me'dya vartate padmasaṃbhave || 5 ||

toṣaṇīyāsi kena tvamupāyenāmbujāsane |
paramaḥ puruṣārtho yastvatprītistasya sādhanam || 6 ||

6. paramaḥ puruṣārtho mokṣaḥ | tasya tvatprītiḥ sādhanam | yathā cāhuḥ - 
śreyo na hyaravindalocanamanaḥkāntāprasādādṛte 
saṃsṛtyakṣaravaiṣṇavādhvasu nṛṇāṃ saṃbhāvyate karhicit iti | 

tvatprītau ka upāyaḥ syātkīdṛśaḥ kiṃvidhaḥ smṛtaḥ [B, ḍ, ī: 
tathā] |
etanme sakalaṃ brūhi namaste padmasaṃbhave || 7 ||

śrīḥ -

cāturātmyaṃ paraṃ brahma saccidānandalakṣaṇam [E, ī: saṃvidā##-
sarvaṃ sarvottaraṃ sarvabhūtāntaḥsthamanāmayam || 8 ||

8. satyaṃ jñānam ityādiśrutipratipādyaṃ svarūpalakṣaṇamucyate - 
sadityādinā | sarvopādānatvāt sarvātmatvācca sarvamityucyate | tathāpi 
svetarasamastavastuvilakṣaṇatvamāha - sarvottaramiti ||

vāsudevaḥ paraṃ brahma nārāyaṇamayaṃ [E, ī: narāṇāmayanaṃ] 
mahat |
tasyāhaṃ paramā śaktirahaṃtānandacinmayī || 9 ||

9. nārāyaṇamayamiti mayaṭ svārthe |

bhinnā'bhinnā ca varte'haṃ jyotsneva himadīdhiteḥ |
tāvāvāṃ tattvamekaṃ tu dvidhā bhūtau [ṅ: bhūtvā] vyavasthitau || 10 ||

10. prapañca iva brahmaṇa evāvasthābhedaḥ śrīriti 
keṣāṃcinmatamanabhimatyāha- bhinneti |

brahma nārāyaṇaṃ māṃ yajjñānenaivāpnuyādyatiḥ [E, ī: vā 
jñānena-] |
panthā nānyo'sti vijñānādayanāya vipaścitām || 11 ||

11. yatiḥ niyatātmavān | nātra caturthāśramī vivakṣitaḥ |

jñānaṃ tacca vivekotthaṃ sarvataḥ śuddhamavraṇam |
vāsudevaikaviṣayamapunarbhavakāraṇam || 12 ||

12. vivekaḥ brahmaṇaḥ sarvavilakṣaṇatvajñānam |

jñāne tasmin samutpanne viśate māmanantaram |
taistairupāyaiḥ prītāhaṃ jīvānāmamalātmanām || 13 ||

udbhāvayāmi tajjñānamātmajyotiḥpradarśakam |
upāyāste ca catvāro mama prītivivardhanāḥ || 14 ||

14. karmajñānabhaktinyāsākhyāḥ catvāro yogā atra vivakṣitāḥ |

p. 49)

[B: omits six lines from here] śakraḥ -

bhagavatyaravindasthe paṅkajekṣaṇakāmini |
upāyāḥ ke [E, ī: ke te'pyupāyāḥ] ca catvārastānme darśaya 
paṅkaje || 15 ||

śrīḥ -

upāyāṃścaturaḥ śakra śṛṇu matprītivardhanān |
yairahaṃ paramāṃ prītiṃ yāsyāmyanapagāminīm ||16 ||

svajātivihitaṃ karma sāṃkhyaṃ yogastathaiva ca |
sarvatyāgaśca vidvadbhirupāyāḥ kathitā ime || 17 ||

caturbhirlakṣaṇairyuktaṃ trividhaṃ karma vaidikam |
svavarṇāśramasaṃbandhī nityanaimittikātmakam || 18 ||

18. lakṣaṇacatuṣkaṃ viṃśe śloke vakṣyate | nityanaimittikakāmyabhedena 
traividhyam |

akāmahatasaṃsiddhaṃ karma tat pūrvasādhanam |
caturvidhastu saṃnyāsastatra kāryo vipaścitā || 19 ||

19. kāmyakarmasu viśeṣamāha - akāmeti | phalaviśeṣakāmanayā kṛtāni 
karmāṇi kāmyāni jyotiṣṭomādayaḥ | tāni ca kāmahatānītyucyante | kāmanāṃ 
vinā kevalaṃ bhagavatprītyarthaṃ kṛtāni tānyakāmahatāni | anena kāmyānāmapi 
karmaṇāṃ kāmanāṃ vinānuṣṭhānamanumanyate | paraṃ 
tvetadupāyāntaraniṣṭhaviṣayam | nyāsayoganiṣṭhānāṃ tu kāmyakarmaṇāṃ 
sarvathā svarūpato'pi tyāga eva na tu kāmanāṃ vināpyanuṣṭhānamiti 
siddhānto'vagantavyaḥ |

mantroktadevatāyāṃ vā prakṛtāvindriyeṣu vā |
parasmin devadeve vā vāsudeve janārdane || 20 ||

20. bhagavati vāsudeve karmaṇāṃ saṃnyasanameva mumukṣubhiḥ kartavyam | 
mantradevatādau vinyasanaṃ tu bubhukṣuviṣayamiti dhyeyam |

pūrvaṃ [E, ī: sarva] kartṛtvasaṃnyāsaḥ phalasaṃnyāsa eva ca |
karmaṇāmapi saṃnyāso devadeve janārdane || 21 ||

21. bhagavāneva svaśeṣataikarasena mayā svakīyaiścopakaraṇaiḥ 
svārādhanaikaprayojanāya svaśeṣabhūtamidaṃ karma svayameva kārayati 
ityanusaṃdhānaprakāro vivakṣitaḥ |

śāstrīyamācarannevaṃ nityanaimittikātmakam |
madārādhanakāmaḥ sañśaśvat prīṇāti māṃ naraḥ || 22 ||

iti te leśataḥ proktaṃ śrutismṛtinidarśitam [ā, B, c: 
nidarśanam] |
dvitīyaṃ sāṃkhyavijñānamupāyaṃ śṛṇu sāṃpratam || 23 ||

saṃkhyāstisro hi mantavyāḥ sāṃkhyaśāstranidarśitāḥ |
prathamā laukikī saṃkhyā dvitīyā carcanātmikā [E, ī: carcikā] || 24 ||

24. trayāṇāmapyeṣāṃ jñānānāṃ vivaraṇamatraiva kariṣyate |

samīcīnā tu yā dhīḥ sā tṛtīyā paripaṭhyate |
saṃkhyātrayasamūho yaḥ sāṃkhyaṃ tatparipaṭhyate || 25 ||

25. saṃkhyāḥ jñānāni pūrvoktāni | teṣāṃ samūhaḥ sāṃkhyamityucyate | etadeva 
jñānayoga ityucyate |

pṛthivyāpastathā tejo vāyurākāśameva ca |
ahaṃkāro mahāṃścaiva prakṛtiḥ paramā tathā || 26 ||

26. laukikī lokaviṣayā saṃkhyocyate'nena ślokena | uparitanaiḥ ślokairasyā 
vivaraṇaṃ kriyate |

etāḥ prakṛtayastvaṣṭau tāsāṃ vyākhyāmimāṃ śṛṇu |
prakṛtistrividhā proktā māyā sūtirguṇātmikā || 27 ||

27. māyā prasūtiḥ traiguṇyamiti prakṛtibhedāḥ | yadyapi prakṛtiḥ sūkṣmaiva 
tathāpi tatraiva sūkṣmasūkṣmatarasūkṣmatamarūpeṇa traividhyādevaṃ vyavahāraḥ 
|

p. 50)

niḥsaktāsaktamadvaitamataraṅgamanaśvaram |
acetanānāṃ paramaṃ saukṣmyaṃ māyeti gīyate || 28 ||

īṣaducchūnatā tasyāḥ prasūtiriti gīyate |
guṇatrayasamunmeṣaḥ sāmyena prakṛtiḥ parā || 29 ||

avyaktamakṣaraṃ yoniravidyā triguṇā sthitiḥ |
māyā svabhāva ityādyāḥ śabdāḥ paryāyavācakāḥ || 30 ||

30. avāntarabhedaiḥ saha saṃkalayya prakṛtiparyāyanāmāni avyaktādīni | 
upaniṣadi avyaktamakṣare līyate | akṣaraṃ tamasi līyate | tamaḥ 
paramātmanyekībhavati iti trayāṇāmeṣāṃ vyavahāraḥ kriyate |

sattvaṃ rajastamaśceti guṇā ete trayo matāḥ |
tatra sattvaṃ laghu jñeyaṃ sukharūpamacañcalam || 31 ||

prakāśo nāma tadvṛttiścaitanyodgrahaṇātmakaḥ |
rajo'pi ca laghu jñeyaṃ duḥkharūpaṃ ca cañcalam || 32 ||

32. caitanyasya jñānasya udgrahaṇam unmeṣa ityarthaḥ 
vastuyāthātmyagrahaṇaśīlateti yāvat |

pravṛttirnāma tadvṛttiḥ spandaheturanaśvaraḥ |
tamo nāma guru jñeyaṃ moharūpamacañcalam || 33 ||

niyamo nāma tadvṛttiḥ kvacit svāpanalakṣaṇam |
na tadasti pṛthivyāṃ vā divi vyomni ca vāsava || 34 ||

34. niyamo bandhaḥ |

bhūtaṃ prakṛtijairmuktaṃ yadebhiḥ syāt tribhirguṇaiḥ |
ete cittamadhiṣṭhāya guṇā indriyagāstathā || 35 ||

sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ tathā mohaṃ viṣayasthāśca kurvate |
[F: omits this line] śarīrendriyatāṃ yātā [E: indriyatāyāṃ ca] 
guṇāḥ karmāṇi kurvate |
iti yasya matirnityā sa guṇātyayamaśnute || 36 ||

iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre [ā: śrīpañcarātra] lakṣmītantre 
upāyaprakāraprakāśo [B, F, ṅ, ī: upāyaprakāśo] nāma 
pañcadaśo'dhyāyaḥ || 15 ||
p. 51)

ṣoḍaśo'dhyāyaḥ

śrīḥ -

vyākhyānaṃ mahataḥ śakra śṛṇuṣvāvahito mama |
vaiṣamyasya samunmeṣo guṇānāṃ prathamo hi yaḥ || 1 ||

1. anena guṇatrayasya sāmyāvasthāyāṃ prakṛtiriti vaiṣamyāvasthāyāṃ mahāniti 
ca vyavahāra ityuktaṃ bhavati |

sa mahānnāma tasyāpi vidhāstisraḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
sāttviko buddhirityukto rājasaḥ prāṇa eva hi [E: ucyate] || 2 ||

tāmasaḥ kāla ityuktasteṣāṃ vyākhyāmimāṃ śṛṇu |
buddhiradhyavasāyasya prāṇaḥ prayatanasya ca || 3 ||

3. kālasya tāmasatvamatrocyate | aupaniṣadaprakriyā kālasyāprākṛtatvameveti 
vadanti |

kālaḥ kalanarūpasya [ḍ: kālana] pariṇāmasya kāraṇam |
mahato'pi vikurvāṇādahaṃkāro vyajāyata [ā, B, c, ḍ: asya 
jāyate] || 4 ||

4. kalanaṃ vipariṇatiḥ | āmrādiśalāṭoḥ tatphalātmanā yuvadehasya 
valibhavṛddhadehātmanā ca yā pariṇatiḥ setyarthaḥ |

sa cāpi trividho jñeyo guṇavaiṣamyasaṃbhavāt |
tāmasādviyadādistu tanmātragaṇa ujjvalaḥ || 5 ||

jātaḥ sattvasamudriktādbuddhīndriyagaṇo mahān |
karmendriyagaṇaścāpi rājasādubhayātmakam [ā, B: 
rājasāttūbhayā] || 6 ||

6. karmendriyāṇāṃ rājasatvaṃ manasaḥ sāttvikarājasatvamiti 
vibhāgastāntrikaikadeśināṃ matena | vastutastu ekādaśāpīndriyāṇi 
sāttvikānītyeva bahūnāṃ pāñcarātrikāṇāṃ matam | devā vaikārikā daśa | 
ekādaśaṃ manaśca iti viṣṇupurāṇe coktam |

ubhayasmāt samudbhūtamitīyaṃ tattvapaddhatiḥ |
atra prakṛtirekaiva mūlabhūtā sanātanī || 7 ||

7. tattvapaddhatiḥ | acittattvapaddhatirityarthaḥ | itthamatra vibhāgaḥ - tattvaṃ 
dvividham acittattvaṃ cittattvaṃ ceti | tatrācittattvaṃ caturviṃśatidhā - 
prakṛtimahadahaṃkārāstrayaḥ tanmātrāṇi pañca mahābhūtāni pañca 
ekādaśendriyāṇīti | cittattvaṃ dvedhā - jīva īśvaraśceti | āhatya 
ṣaḍviṃśatistattvāni |

mahadādyāstu saptānye kāryakāraṇarūpiṇaḥ |
tanmātrebhyaḥ samudbhūtā viśeṣā viyadādayaḥ || 8 ||

buddhikarmendriyagaṇau pañcakau mana eva ca |
vikārā eva vijñeyā ete ṣoḍaśa cintakaiḥ || 9 ||

caturviṃśatiretāni tattvāni kathitāni te |
yāvānyaścātra vaktavyo viśeṣo yādṛśastviha || 10 ||

sa sarvaḥ kathitaḥ pūrvaṃ tava vṛtraniṣūdana |
viṃśatyā ca tribhiścaiva vikāraiḥ svaiḥ samanvitā || 11 ||

p. 52)

iyaṃ prakṛtiravyaktā kathitā te surādhipa |
vyaktāvyaktamayī saiṣā nityaṃ prasavadharmiṇī || 12 ||

12. prakṛtyavasthāyāmavyaktā pariṇatyavasthāyāṃ vyaktā | 
kāryakāraṇayorabhedādevamuktiḥ |

vilakṣaṇā sā vijñeyā cicchaktiravinaśvarā [B: avinaśvarī] |
sa jīvaḥ kathitaḥ sadbhistattvaśāstraviśāradaiḥ || 13 ||

ayaṃ svarasataḥ śuddhaḥ pariṇāmavivarjitaḥ |
kūṭasthaściddhano nityo hyananto'pratisaṃkramaḥ [ā, ḍ, F: 
nityamantaḥstho-] || 14 ||

14. svarasataḥ svabhāvataḥ | anantaḥ saṃkhyayā jñānādiguṇaiścāparicchinna 
ityarthaḥ |

imau svarasato'saktau saktātmānāviva sthitau [B: sattātmānau vyavasthitau] 
|
prakṛtiḥ puruṣaścaiva mahadbhyaśca mahattarau || 15 ||

liṅgagrāhyāvubhau nityāvaliṅgau cāpyubhāvapi |
sādharmyamevamādyevamanayorunnayedbudhaḥ || 16 ||

vaidharmyamanayoḥ śakra kathyamānaṃ nibodha me |
prakṛtistriguṇā nityaṃ satataṃ pariṇāminī || 17 ||

avivekāpyaśuddhā ca sarvajīvasamā sadā |
viṣayo'cetanā [ṅ: avedanā] caiva sukhaduḥkhavimohinī || 18 ||

madhyasthaḥ puruṣo nityaḥ kriyāvānapyavihvalaḥ |
sākṣī dṛśistathā draṣṭā śuddho'nanto guṇātmakaḥ || 19 ||

vaidharmyamanayoretat prakṛtiṃ cānayoḥ śṛṇu |
yā sā sadasadākhyādivikalpavikalā [c, ḍ: vipulā] dhruvā || 20 ||

20. vikalpavikaleti | vividhapariṇatyabhāginītyarthaḥ |

nityoditā sadānandā pūrṇaṣāḍguṇyavigrahā |
ahaṃ nārāyaṇī śaktirviṣṇoḥ śrīranapāyinī || 21 ||

mattaḥ prabhavato hyetau mayyeva layameṣyataḥ |
sāhametāvatī bhāvairvividhairvistṛtiṃ [ā, B, c, ḍ: 
bhāvairviṣayaiḥ] gatā || 22 ||

nārāyaṇe pratiṣṭhāya punastasmādudemyaham |
eko nārāyaṇo viṣṇurvāsudevaḥ sanātanaḥ || 23 ||

apṛthagbhūtaśaktitvādadvaitaṃ brahma niṣkalam |
jñānaśaktibalaiśvaryavīryatejomahodadhiḥ || 24 ||

24. śaktiśaktimatorapṛthaksiddhasaṃbandhāt ekatvam | ato nādvitīyatvavirodha iti 
bhāvaḥ |

nistaraṅgaḥ sadaivāsau jagadetaccarācaram |
iti te sāṃkhyavijñānaṃ leśataḥ śakra darśitam || 25 ||

25. asau nārāyaṇa eva carācarātmakajagadrūpatayāvatiṣṭhata iti yāvat |

yā tattvagaṇanā saṃkhyā tāṃ purā śīlayedbudhaḥ |
tataḥ sādharmyavaidharmyasvarūpaprabhavādikām || 26 ||

kuryāccarcātmikāṃ saṃkhyāṃ śāstratattvopadeśajām |
carcāyāmiha saṃkhyāyāṃ [F: omits all portions from here up to evaṃ hi 
parisaṃkhyāya in the 29th verse] siddhāyāmamalātmani || 27 ||

27. carcā nāma punaḥ punaḥ pariśīlanam |

udeti yā samīcīnā saṃkhyā sattattvagocarā |
eṣā sā paramā saṃkhyā matprasādasamudbhavā || 28 ||

sāṃkhyadarśanametatte parisaṃkhyānamīritam |
evaṃ hi parisaṃkhyāya sāṃkhyā madbhāvamāgatāḥ [ā, B, c: 
sadbhāva] || 29 ||

p. 53)

upāyo yastṛtīyaste [ā, B: ayaṃ] vakṣyate yogasaṃjñakaḥ |
yogastu dvividho jñeyaḥ samādhiḥ saṃyamastathā || 30 ||

yamādyaṅgasamudbhūtā samādhiḥ saṃsthitiḥ pare |
brahmaṇi śrīnivāsākhye hyutthānaparivarjitā [ā, B, c, ḍ: 
varjitaḥ] || 31 ||

sākṣātkāramayī sā hi sthitiḥ sadbrahmavedinām |
dhyātṛdhyeyāvibhāgasthā matprasādasamudbhavā || 32 ||

32. avibhāgastheti | ātmeti tūpagacchanti grāhayanti ca ityuktarītyā parasya 
brahmaṇo'vibhāgenopāsanamatrābhipretam |

saṃyamo nāma satkarma paramātmaikagocaram |
tatpunardvividhaṃ proktaṃ śārīraṃ mānasaṃ tathā || 33 ||

vistareṇābhidhāsyete samādhiḥ saṃyamastathā |
prathamo ya upāyaste karmātmā kathitaḥ purā || 34 ||

saṃjñānaṃ janayecchuddhamantaḥkaraṇaśodhanāt |
tena hi prīṇitā sāhaṃ sadācāraniṣevaṇāt || 35 ||

dadāmi buddhiyogaṃ tamantaḥkaraṇaśodhanam |
sāṃkhyaṃ nāma dvitīyo ya upāyaḥ kathitastava || 36 ||

parokṣaḥ śāstrajanyo'sau nirṇayo dṛḍhatāṃ gataḥ |
pratyakṣatāmivāpanno matprītiṃ janayetparām || 37 ||

ahaṃ saṃkhyāyamānā hi svarūpaguṇavaibhavaiḥ |
udbhāvayāmi tajjñānaṃ pratyakṣaṃ yadvivekajam || 38 ||

tṛtīyastu samādhyātmā pratyakṣo'viplavo [ā: pratyakṣāviplavo] 
dṛḍhaḥ |
prakṛṣṭasattvasaṃbhūtaḥ prasādātiśayo hi saḥ || 39 ||

tṛtīyasya vidhā yo'sau saṃyamo nāma varṇitaḥ |
bhogaiḥ śuddhaistridhodbhūtairatyantaprītaye mama || 40 ||

ahaṃ hi tatra viśvātmā viṣṇuśaktiḥ parāvarā |
sākṣādeva samārādhyā devo vā puruṣottamaḥ || 41 ||

iti te kathitāḥ samyagupāyāstraya ūrjitāḥ |
śṛṇūpāyaṃ caturthaṃ me sarvatyāgasamāhvayam || 42 ||

tatra [c: sarva] dharmān parityajya sarvānuccāvacāṅgakān |
saṃsārānalasaṃtapto māmekāṃ śaraṇaṃ vrajet || 43 ||

ahaṃ hi śaraṇaṃ prāptā nareṇānanyacetasā |
prāpayāmyātmanātmānaṃ nirdhūtākhilakalmaṣam || 44 ||

iti srīpāñcarātrasāre [ā: śrīpañcarātra; ī: śrīpāñcarātre sāre]

title] nāma ṣoḍaśo'dhyāyaḥ || 16 ||
p. 54)

saptadaśo'dhyāyaḥ 

śakraḥ -

namaste kamalāvāse jananyai sarvadehinām |
gṛhiṇyai padmanābhasya namaste sarasīruhe || 1 ||

upāyāste trayaḥ pūrve kathitā avadhāritāḥ |
vyācakṣyāmya caturthaṃ tamupāyaṃ [E. ī: tvam] paramambuje || 2 ||

śrīḥ -

eko nārāyaṇo devo vāsudevaḥ sanātanaḥ |
cāturātmyaṃ paraṃ brahma saccidānandamavraṇam || 3 ||

ekāhaṃ paramā śaktistasya devī sanātanī |
karomi sakalaṃ kṛtyaṃ sarvabhāvānugāminī || 4 ||

śāntānantacidānandaṃ [ā, B, ṅ: śāntānanda] yad brahma 
paramaṃ  dhruvam |
mahāvibhūtisaṃsthānaṃ sarvataḥ samatāṃ gatam || 5 ||

5. mahāvibhūtiḥ nityavibhūtiḥ | tripādasyāmṛtaṃ divi ityuktarītyā 
līlāvibhūtyapekṣayā tasyā mahattvāt mahāvibhūtitvam |

tasya śaktirahaṃ brāhmī śāntānandacidātmikā |
mahāvibhūtiranaghā sarvataḥ samatāṃ gatā || 6 ||

āśvāsanāya jīvānāṃ yattanmūrtīkṛtaṃ mahaḥ |
nārāyaṇaḥ paraṃ brahma divyaṃ nayananandanam || 7 ||

tadā mūrtimatī sāhaṃ śaktirnārāyaṇī parā |
samā samavibhaktāṅgā sarvāvayavasundarī || 8 ||

tayornau paramaṃ vyoma nirduḥkhaṃ padamuttamam [ī: sukhaṃ-] |
ṣāḍguṇyaprasaro divyaḥ svācchandyāddeśatāṃ gataḥ || 9 ||

9. madīyecchayā ṣāḍguṇyaprasara evāprākṛtadivyadeśatāṃ prāpta ityarthaḥ |

svakarmanirataiḥ siddhairvedavedāntapāragaiḥ |
anekajanmasaṃtānaniḥśeṣitakaṣāyakaiḥ || 10 ||

kleśena mahatā siddhairantarāyātigaiḥ kramāt |
saṃkhyāvidhividhānajñaiḥ sāṃkhyaiḥ saṃkhyānapāragaiḥ || 11 ||

pratyāhṛtendriyagrāmairdhāraṇādhyānaśālibhiḥ |
yaugaiḥ [ā, B, ī: yogaiḥ] samāhitaiḥ śaśvatkleśena 
yadavāpyate || 12 ||

acchidrāḥ pañcakālajñāḥ pañcayajñavicakṣaṇāḥ |

13. acchidrāḥ bhagavadananubhavarūpacchidrarahitāḥ | yathoktam -

kālāḥ |

yattatpurāṇamākāśaṃ sarvasmāt paramaṃ dhruvam |
yatpadaṃ prāpya tattvajñā mucyante sarvabandhanaiḥ || 14 ||

sūryakoṭipratīkāśāḥ pūrṇendvayutasaṃnibhāḥ |
yasmin pade virājante muktāḥ saṃsārabandhanaiḥ || 15 ||

sahitāmalāḥ] || 16 ||

p. 55)

ekāntino mahābhāgā yatra paśyanti nau sadā |

19. aṣṭākṣaraḥ nārāyaṇāṣṭākṣaramantraḥ | dviṣaṭkārṇaḥ

jitaṃtāsaktacittānāṃ tārikāniratātmanām || 20 ||

21. anutārā śrīṃmantraḥ |

senānyā sevitaḥ samyagviṣvaksenena dīpyatā [E, F: dīvyatā] || 25 ||

25. sārūpyaṃ śaṅkhacakrādinā bhagavatsārūpyam | tadevāha - śrīvatseti |

59. māmekam māmevetyarthaḥ | ekaśabdo'vadhāraṇārthaḥ san 
anyayogavyavacchede vartate | atredaṃ bodhyam - śaraṇavaraṇāt pūrvaṃ 
madhyamavṛttyāśrayaṇaṃ bhavatu vā mā vā | asti cet śaraṇavaraṇe 
tvarāmupajanayati | na cet vilambaḥ | śaraṇavaraṇānantaraṃ tu yāvajjīvaṃ 
madhyamavṛttyāśrayaṇamavaśyaṃ kartavyam | na cet vilambena phalaṃ syāt | 
pūrvakṛtāni tu karmāṇi nyāsabalādeva naśyanti | paścāt kṛtāni tu 
prāmādikāni na śliṣyanti | buddhipūrvakṛtānyapi prāyaścittāpanodyāni 
bhavanti | ataḥ cāndrāyaṇādiprāyaścittapariharaṇāya madhyamavṛttyā##-

rakṣiṣyatīti viśvāso goptṛtvavaraṇaṃ tathā || 60 ||

nirārambho nirāśīśca [ā, ḍ, E: nirāśī ca] nirmamo

īśeśitavyasaṃbandhādanidaṃprathamādapi |

tamaṃśaṃ naiva kurvīta manīṣī pūrvarāśivat || 89 ||

89. tṛtīye prathamāṃ vidhāmāha - prāyaścitteti |

kriyamāṇaṃ na kasmaicidyadarthāya prakalpate |
akriyāvadanarthāya tattu karma [E, ī: karma tattu] samācaret || 90 ||

90. tṛtīye dvitīyāṃ vidhāmāha - kriyamāṇetyādi | 
nityanaimittikarūpamityarthaḥ |

eṣā sā [ā, B, c, ṅ: hi] vaidikī niṣṭhā 
hyupāyāpāyamadhyamā |
asyāṃ sthito jagannāthaṃ prapadyeta janārdanam || 91 ||

sakṛdeva hi śāstrārthaḥ kṛto'yaṃ tārayennaram |
upāyāpāyasaṃyoge niṣṭhayā hīyate'nayā || 92 ||

92. sakṛtkṛtaḥ śāstrārthaḥ iti nyāyānusāreṇāha - sakṛdeva hīti | anayeti 
| prapattirūpayetyarthaḥ |

apāyasaṃplave sadyaḥ prāyaścittaṃ samācaret |
prāyaścittiriyaṃ sātra yatpunaḥ śaraṇaṃ śrayet [B, c, ṅ: 
vrajet] || 93 ||

93. apāyasaṃplava iti | buddhipūrvāparādhasaṃbhava ityarthaḥ |

upāyānāmupāyatvasvīkāre'pyetadeva hi |
aviplavāya dharmāṇāṃ pālanāya kulasya ca || 94 ||

94. jyotiṣṭomādīnāṃ mokṣopāyatvasvīkāre'pi niṣṭhāpracyutirbhavatyeva | tasmāt 
tāni na kurvīta | ayamatra nirgalitārthaḥ - bhaktiyoganiṣṭhānāṃ 
tadyogamahimneva prapattiyoganiṣṭhānāmapi prapattimahimnaiva pūrvatanāni 
sarvāṇyapi buddhipūrvakāṇyabuddhipūrvakāṇi ca mokṣavirodhikarmāṇi 
samūlanāśaṃ naśyanti | prapannānāṃ prāyaśaḥ prapatteranantaraṃ tādṛśāni 
karmāṇi na saṃbhavantyeva | kadācijjātānyapyabuddhipūrvakāṇi aśliṣṭāni 
bhavanti | buddhipūrvakāṇi tu punaḥ prapadanena naśyanti | 
akṛtapunaḥprapadanānāṃ tu svalpadaṇḍena tadbhogāttannāśaḥ | 
prārabdhakarmāṇyapi prapannasyārtitāratamyena sadyo vā taddehāvasāne vā 
naśyanti | bhaktiyogāt nyāsayogasyāyaṃ mahimātiśayaḥ - bhaktiyogaḥ 
prārabdhakarmāṇi nāpohayitumalam | nyāsayogastu 
prārabdhakarmāṇyapyapohayitumalam | yadyārtyatiśayāt prapattikāle 
sadyastannāśo'pi prārthitaḥ tadā sadya eva tāni naśyanti | yadi taddehāvasane 
prārthitaḥ tadā taddehāvasāna eva naśyanti | na tu bhaktiyoganiṣṭhānāmiva 
tadbhogārthadehāntarāpādakāni | tathā cāhuḥ - sādhyabhaktistu sā hantrī 
prārabdhasyāpi bhūyasi iti | kiṃca vaidikāni kāmyakarmāṇi niṣkāmanayā 
bhaktiyogāṅgatayānuṣṭhātumabhyanujñāyante | prapannānāṃ tu 
niṣkāmanayāpi teṣāmanuṣṭhānaṃ svarūpaviruddhamiti |

saṃgrahāya ca lokasya maryādāsthāpanāya ca |
priyāya mama viṣṇośca devadevasya śārṅgiṇaḥ || 95 ||

manīṣī vaidikācāraṃ manasāpi na laṅghayet |
yathā hi vallabho rājño nadīṃ rājñā pravartitām || 96 ||

lokopayoginīṃ ramyāṃ bahusasyavivardhinīm |
laṅghayañśūlamārohedanapekṣo'pi tāṃ prati || 97 ||

p. 60)

evaṃ vilaṅghayana martyo maryādāṃ vedanirmitām |
priyo'pi na priyo'sau me madājñāvyativartanāt || 98 ||

98. ayaṃ bhāvaḥ - nityanaimittikakarmāṇi na sarvathā phalarahitāni | kiṃtu 
pratyavāyotpattinirodhaphalāni | ānuṣaṅgikamapi hi prājāpatyādilokaprāpti##-
tānyādriyante | pratyavāyaśca bhagavannigraha eva | evaṃca 
bhagavannigrahotpatti-nirodhāya tāni kriyanta iti na sarvātmanā nirarthakānīti | 
ataḥ prayojanamanuddiśya na mando'pi pravartate iti codyasya nāvakāśaḥ |

upāyatvagrahaṃ tatra varjayana [ā, B, c, ṅ: varjayaṃta] 
manasā sudhīḥ |
caturdhamāśrayannevamupāyaṃ śaraṇāśrayam || 99 ||

atītya sakalaṃ kleśaṃ saṃviśatyamalaṃ padam |
apāyopāyanirmuktāṃ madhyamāṃ sthitimāsthitā [E: vṛttim-] || 100 
||

śaraṇāgatiragryaiṣā saṃsārārṇavatāriṇī |
idaṃ śaraṇamajñānāmidameva vijānatām || 101 ||

idaṃ titīrṣatāṃ pāramidamānantyamicchatām |
prāyaścittaprasaṅge [E: prāyaścitti-] tu sarvapāpasamudbhave || 102 
||

māmekāṃ devadevasya mahiṣīṃ śaraṇaṃ śrayet [B, E, ṅ: 
vrajet] |
upāyādvirataḥ [B, E: apāyāt] śaśvanmāṃ caiva [ā, c: 
cāpi] śaraṇaṃ vrajet || 103 ||

tanūkṛtyākhilaṃ pāpaṃ māṃ cāpnoti naraḥ śanaiḥ |
athopāyaprasaktaśca bhuktvā bhogānanāmayān [ā, B, c: 
amānavān] || 104 ||

ante viraktimāsādya viśate paramaṃ padam |
upāyaḥ sukaraḥ so'yaṃ duṣkaraśca mato mama || 105 ||

105. nanu cirakālasādhyasya bhaktiyogasya kṣaṇakālasādhyasya nyāsayogasya ca 
mokṣākhyatulyaphalatvaṃ na ghaṭate gurulaghūpāyayorvikalpāyogāt | tathā sati 
prekṣāvantaḥ sarve'pi laghūpāya eva pravarteran na gurūpāya iti 
tadvidherananuṣṭhānalakṣaṇamaprāmāṇyaṃ syādityatrāha - upāyaḥ sukara 
iti | yadyapyayaṃ nyāsayogaḥ kṣaṇakālakartavyatvāt karmādyanaṅgakatvācca 
sukara eva tathāpyatyantadurlabhamahāviśvāsādyaṅgāpekṣatvāt 
durūhamadhyamavṛttyāśrayatvācca duṣkara eveti bhāvaḥ | atra yadyapi 
jñānaśaktyādipauṣkalyatadrāhityābhyāmadhikāribhedāt vyavasthā suvacā 
tathāpi idameva vijānatām ityādinā jñānādimatāmapi nyāsayogavidhānāt sā 
rītirnādṛtā |

śiṣṭairniṣevyate so'yamakāmahatacetanaiḥ |
akāmaiśca sakāmaiśca tasmātsiddhyarthamātmanaḥ || 106 ||

arcanīyā naraiḥ śaśvanmama mantramayī tanuḥ |
praviśya vidhivaddīkṣāṃ gurorlabdhvārthasaṃpadaḥ [E, ī: 
saṃpadam] |
manmayairarcayenmantrairmāmikāṃ māntrikīṃ [E, ī: mama 
mantramayīṃ] tanum || 107 ||

iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre [ā: omits śrīpāñcarātrasāre] lakṣmītantre 
rahasyopāyaprakāśo [ā, B, ṅ: prasaṅgo] nāma 
saptadaśo'dhyāyaḥ || 17 ||
p. 61)

aṣṭādaśo'dhyāyaḥ 

śakraḥ - 

[F: omits this verse] namaste padmanilaye namaste padmasaṃbhave |
viditaṃ veditavyaṃ me vedānteṣvapi durlabham || 1 ||

brūhi mantramayaṃ mārgamidānīṃ viṣṇuvallabhe |
yaṃ vijñāyārcayeyaṃ te divyāṃ mantramayīṃ tanum || 2 ||

kuto mantrasamutpattiḥ kva ca mantraḥ pralīyate [B: vilīyate] |
mantrasya kiṃ phalaṃ padme kena madhye prapūryate || 3 ||

kiyatyaśca vidhā asya [B, ṅ, ī: vidhāstasya] parimāṇaṃ kiyat kila 
|
kṣetrakṣetrajñabhāvaśca kīdṛśaḥ paramo'mbuje || 4 ||

mantraśca kena saṃgrāhya upadeṣṭā ca kīdṛśaḥ |
upāsanaprakāraśca kathamasyābjasaṃbhave || 5 ||

upāsanopayogī ca yāvānartho'mbujāsane |
siddhisādhanayogaśca pratyayāśca tathā tathā || 6 ||

yogaḥ svādhyāyayogaśca rakṣāyogastathaiva ca |
prāyaścīttavidhiścaiva [ā, F, ṅ: prāyaścitti] 
śrāddhakalpastathaiva [ā: śraddhākalpaḥ] ca || 7 ||

dīkṣāpratiṣṭhayoḥ kalpo yantrakalpastathaiva ca |
etacca nikhilaṃ yaccāpyadṛṣṭamupayujyate [B: upapadyate] || 8 ||

prabrūhi tadaśeṣeṇa namaste padmasaṃbhave |
tavaiṣa śirasā pādau nato'smi kamalāruṇau || 9 ||

śaraṇaṃ ca prapanno'smi paṅkaje tvamadhīhi bho |

śrīḥ [ī: śrīruvāca] - 

praśnabhāro'yamatulastvayoddiṣṭaḥ puraṃdara || 10 ||

vācyaste prītisaṃyogācchṛṇu vakṣyāmyaśeṣataḥ |
ahamityeva yaḥ pūrṇaḥ [B, ṅ, ī: pūrṇa] puruṣaḥ puṣkarekṣaṇaḥ 
|| 11 ||

svabhāvaḥ sarvabhāvānāmabhāvānāṃ ca vāsava |
idaṃ tayāvalīḍhaṃ yat sadasajjagati sthitam || 12 ||

12. idaṃtayeti | idamiti pratītiviṣayatayetyarthaḥ | parāktveneti yāvat | yuṣmadasmat 
- pratyayagocaratvaṃ hi krameṇa parāktvapratyaktvayorlakṣaṇamāmananti |

tattallakṣaṇavanto ye tadahaṃtve vilīyate |
vilīnedaṃpadadvīpaḥ prāptaikadhyaścidambudhiḥ || 13 ||

13. vilīnetyādi idaṃpadārthabhūtāḥ sarve'pi acitpadārthāḥ ta eva dvīpāḥ 
cidambudhau vāsudeve vilīya tena sahaikatvaṃ prāptā ityarthaḥ |

nistaraṅgodayo'nanto vāsudevaḥ prakāśate |
pūrṇāhaṃtāsmi tasyaikā śaktirīśvaratāmayī || 14 ||

nityoditā sadānandā sarvataḥ samatāṃ gatā |
sarvabhāvasamudbhūtiḥ sarvapratyakṣasaṃmatā || 15 ||

15. nityoditeti | udayo dvividhaḥ - śāntodayaḥ nityodayaśceti | 
avatārāvasthāyāmādyaḥ | parāvasthāyāṃ dvitīyaḥ |

yā hyeṣā pratibhā tattatpadārthakramarūṣitā [ṅ: rūpiṇī] |
uddhṛteṣu padārtheṣu sāhamakramaśālinī || 16 ||

p. 62)

avabodhātmikāyā me yā pratyagavamarśitā [c: śravamarśinī] |
sā sphurattā mahānandā śabdabrahmeti gīyate || 17 ||

17. pratyagavamarśitā ahaṃpratyayagamyatā | 

prakāśānandasārāhaṃ sarvamantraprasūḥ parā |
śabdānāṃ jananī śaktirudayāstamayojjhitā || 18 ||

vyāpakaṃ yatparaṃ brahma nārāyaṇamanāmayam |
śāntatā nāma yāvasthā sāhaṃ śāntākhilaprasūḥ || 19 ||

tasyā me ya udeti sma sisṛkṣākhyo'lpa [B: ayam] udyamaḥ |
sa śabdārthavibhedena śānta unmeṣa ucyate || 20 ||

śabdodayapuraskāraḥ sarvatrārthodayaḥ smṛtaḥ |
arthaśabdapravṛttyātmā śabdasya sthūlatā hi sā || 21 ||

21. śabdasyodayaḥ prathamam anantaramarthasyeti tāntrikasiddhantaḥ | śrutāvapi 
nāmarūpe vyākaravāṇi iti nāmavyākaraṇaṃ pūrvaṃ tato rūpavyākaraṇamuktaṃ 
veditavyam |

bodhonmeṣaḥ smṛtaḥ śabdaḥ śabdonmeṣo'rtha ucyate |
udyacchabdodayaḥ śakteḥ prathamaḥ śāntatātmanaḥ || 22 ||

sa nāda iti vikhyāto vācyatāmasṛṇastadā |
nādena saha śaktiḥ sā sūkṣmeti parigīyate || 23 ||

23. evaṃ ca śabdabrahma parā vāk nāda iti paryāyāḥ |

nādāt paro ya unmeṣo dvitīyaḥ śaktisaṃbhavaḥ |
bindurityucyate so'tra vācyo'pi masṛṇaḥ sthitaḥ || 24 ||

24. nādānantaraṃ yaḥ śaktyunmeṣaḥ sa binduriti paśyantīti cocyate |

paśyantī nāma sāvasthā mama divyā mahodayā |
tataḥ paro ya unmeṣastṛtīyaḥ śaktisaṃbhavaḥ || 25 ||

25. bindoranantaraṃ śaktyunmeṣasya madhyameti nāma |

madhyamā sā daśā tatra saṃskārayati saṃgatim |
vācyavācakabhedastu tadā saṃskāratāmayaḥ || 26 ||

caturthastu ya unmeṣaḥ śaktermādhyamikāt paraḥ |
vaikharī nāma sāvasthā varṇavākyasphuṭodayā || 27 ||

asti śaktiḥ kriyātmā me bodharūpānuyāyinī [F: anapāyinī] |
sā prāṇayati nādādiṃ śaktyunmeṣaparaṃparām || 28 ||

śāntarūpātha paśyantī madhyamā vaikharī tathā |
catūrūpā catūrūpaṃ vacmi vācyaṃ svanirmitam || 29 ||

vāsudevādayaḥ sūkṣmā vācyāḥ śāntādayaḥ kramāt |
ahamekapadī jñeyā prakāśānandarūpiṇī || 30 ||

vācyavācakabhedena [B: bhāvena] punaḥ sā dvipadī smṛtā |
[B, F: omit nine lines from here] 
ūṣmāntaḥsthasvarasparśabhedāccāhaṃ catuṣpadī || 31 ||

31. śaṣasahā ūṣmāṇaḥ | yaralavā antaḥsthāḥ | acaḥ svarāḥ | kādayo 
bhāvasānāḥ sparśāḥ |

aṣṭavargavibhedācca sāhamaṣṭapadī smṛtā |
aghoṣarūpeṇānyena yuktā navapadī smṛtā || 32 ||

32. aṣṭa vargāḥ - svaravargaḥ kādipāntavargāḥ pañca ūṣmavargaḥ 
antaḥsthavargaśceti | aghoṣāśca - yamāḥ jihvāmūlīyopadhmānīyau 
visargaśceti |

ahamekapadī divyā śabdabrahmamayī parā |
ghoṣavarṇasvarūpeṇa varte'haṃ dvipadī punaḥ || 33 ||

33. prakārāntareṇaikadhyādikamucyate | śabdabrahmarūpeṇaikarūpā | 
dhvanivarṇātmanā dvirūpā |

takṣatī salilaṃ sarvaṃ dravyajātiguṇakriyāḥ |
caturdhābhidadhānāhaṃ catuṣpadyuditā budhaiḥ || 34 ||

34. takṣatī takṣantī tanūkurvatī | sarvaṃ salilaṃ saṃsaraṇahetuṃ prākṛtatuṣṭim | 
salilākhyastuṣṭiviśeṣaḥ sāṃkhyasamayaprasiddhaḥ | atra gaurīrmimāya salilāni 
takṣatyekapadī dvipadī sā catuṣpadī | aṣṭāpadī navapadī babhūvuṣī sahasrākṣarā 
parame vyoman | (ṛ 1-164-41) iti śrutiranukriyate | catuṣpadī 
dravyajātiguṇakriyāśabdātmanā catūrūpā |

p. 63)

nāmabhāvadvayopetā sāhamaṣṭapadī smṛtā |
avikalpavikalpasthā sāhaṃ navapadī smṛtā || 35 ||

35. teṣāmeva śabdārthātmanā pratyekaṃ dvaividhye saṃkalayyāṣṭarūpā |

vyomnyahaṃ parame divyā hyanantākṣaramālinī |
iyadvitativistīrṇā pūrṇāhaṃtāhamādimā || 36 ||

36. eṣā ca vyavasthā prākṛtaloka eva | aprākṛtaloke tu anantākṣararūpā |

mantrāṇāṃ jananī jñeyā bhuktimuktipradāyinī |
udyanti mantrakallolā matta eva cidambudheḥ || 37 ||

māmāśritya [F: māmevāśritya vartante] vivartante yānti cāstaṃ 
muhurmayi |
saṃvidānandasaṃdohasundarāḥ śabdadehakāḥ || 38 ||

38. vivartante pariṇamanti | nātrānyaparibhāṣito vivarto vivakṣitaḥ traiguṇyaṃ 
pariṇāmi tat ityuktatvena triguṇapariṇāmatvāt śabdasya |

sāmarthyapūrṇāḥ phaladā mantrātmāno hi manmayāḥ |
varṇāḥ padāni vākyāni sahaprakaraṇāhnikaiḥ || 39 ||

adhyāyāśca paricchedāḥ sargā ucchvāsakāstathā |
paṭalādyā avacchedāḥ [ḍ: vyavacchedāḥ] praśnavākānuvākakāḥ 
[c: prabhā] || 40 ||

maṇḍalāni ca kāṇḍāni saṃhitā vividhātmikāḥ |
ṛco yajūṃṣi sāmāni sūktāni ca khilaiḥ samam || 41 ||

śāstratantrātmakāḥ śabdā bāhyābāhyāgamāstathā |
bhāṣāśca vividhāstāstā vyaktāvyaktagiraḥ smṛtāḥ || 42 ||

mantrarūpamidaṃ śakra viddhi madrūpavedinām |
bhāvanātāratamyena mantramantrivyavasthitiḥ || 43 ||

māṃ trāyate'yamityevaṃ yogena svīkṛto dhvaniḥ |
guptāśayaḥ sadā yaśca mantrajñaṃ trāyate bhayāt || 44 ||

sa mantraḥ saṃsmṛto'haṃtāvikāsaḥ śabdajaiḥ kramaiḥ |
pūrṇāhaṃtāsamudbhūtaiḥ śuddhabodhānvayo yataḥ || 45 ||

sarve mantrā madīyāḥ syuḥ prabhavāpyayavedinām |
madīyāścānyadīyāśca bhāvanātāratamyataḥ || 46 ||

prakṛtyanvayino mantrā madīyāḥ syuḥ pradhānataḥ |
bhavadbhāvātmakaṃ brahma svārasyena viśanti ye || 47 ||

prakṛtyanvayino mantrāstārikottārikādayaḥ |
mantrāḥ svarasato yānti ye bhāvaṃ bhavaduttaram || 48 ||

te'pavargapradā jñeyāstāraprāsādakādayaḥ |
bhāvottarāṃ samāṃ vāpi ye bhajanti bhavatsthitim || 49 ||

bhogāpavargadā mantrā jñeyāste tārikādayaḥ |
viśanti bhāvamevaike yāntyeke bhavadeva ca || 50 ||

bhuktidā muktidāścaiva dvitaye te vyavasthayā |
prakṛtyanvayināmevaṃ svabhāvaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
abhisaṃdhibalāt sarvaṃ dvitaye te vitanvate || 51 ||

iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre [ā: śrīpañcarātra] lakṣmītantre 
mantrasvarūpakathanaṃ [ī: omits the title] nāma aṣṭādaśo'dhyāyaḥ || 18 ||
p. 64)

ekonaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

śrīḥ [B, ī: śrīruvāca] -

sisṛkṣālakṣaṇā pūrvā pūrṇāhaṃtā hareraham |
sṛṣṭirūpā parā śaktirūpetyevoditāsmyaham || 1 ||

1. sisṛkṣāvasthāyām ahaṃteti nāmnā prathitā | sṛṣṭyavasthāyāṃ parā 
śaktiriti nāmnā prathitā | 

daśa pañca ca tulyā me daśāstridaśanandana |
anuttaraṃ svasaṃvedyaṃ cidrūpaṃ mama śāśvatam || 2 ||

2. daśetyādi akārādibindvantāḥ pañcadaśa daśā jñeyāḥ | visargasya 
daśāprakṛtitvāt daśāsu na parigaṇanamiti jñeyam |

vāktattvaṃ tadakārātmā sarvavāṅmayasaṃbhavaḥ |
tadevānandarūpeṇa dvitīyaḥ svara iṣyate || 3 ||

icchātmanā tṛtīyaḥ syādīśānātmā turīyakaḥ |
unmeṣaḥ pañcamaḥ ṣaṣṭha ūrjatārūpa ucyate || 4 ||

catuṣkaṃ madhyamaṃ yattadichādereva vikriyā |
anuttarecchāsaṃyogādekāro nāma jāyate || 5 ||

5. catuṣkamiti | ṛ ṝ ḷ ḹkārarūpamityarthaḥ | tasmāt teṣāṃ na pṛthak 
grahaṇaṃ kṛtam |

tasyaivānandasaṃyogājjagadyonirudāhṛtā |
anuttaronmeṣayogādokāro nāma jāyate || 6 ||

6. jagadyoniḥ aikāraḥ |

tasyaivānuttaraśleṣāt sadyojātasamudbhavaḥ |
anuttarādbhavantyete vikāsā vedyasaṃśrayāḥ || 7 ||

7. sadyojātaḥ aukāraḥ |

tādṛktādṛksamunmeṣā ānandādyāstrayodaśa |
trayodaśatayonmeṣāḥ śritā vedanaśeṣitām || 8 ||

8. vedanaśeṣitām saṃvidrūpeṇa pariśeṣitām | asya 
sūkṣmadaśāmityuttareṇānvayaḥ |

anuttarīṃ sūkṣmadaśāṃ sa tu pañcadaśaḥ svaraḥ [B: pañcadaśa 
svarāḥ] |
etāḥ pañcadaśāvasthā visṛṣṭeḥ sphuraṇodyamāḥ || 9 ||

9. sūkṣmadaśā binduḥ | sa ca pañcadaśaḥ akāramārabhya gaṇane bhavati |

daśabhiḥ pañcabhiścaivamaṅgaiḥ pūrṇā [B, ḍ, F: pūrṇa] 
sisṛkṣayā |
daivī [B: devī] sṛṣṭimayī śaktiḥ kṛtye kṛtye kṛtodyamā || 10 ||

10. sisṛkṣayeti hetau tṛtīyā |

visṛjatyāstu tasyā me tattvānāṃ pañcaviṃśatim [ṅ: viṃśatiḥ] |
puruṣādyāḥ pṛthivyantāḥ kādimāntāḥ samudgatāḥ || 11 ||

11. me iti pañcamyarthe vibhaktipratirūpakamavyayam madityarthaḥ | samudgatā 
ityanenānveti | kādimāntā iti vyutkrameṇānvayaḥ |

tattadakṣarasaṃsphūrtestattattattvaṃ samudgatam |
catuṣkaṃ dhāraṇārūpaṃ yādivāntamudīryate || 12 ||

12. dhāraṇeti | paradaśāyā aparadaśāmavatarataḥ puruṣasya dhāraṇāt dhāraṇeti 
samākhyāḥ |

dhārayanti yato madhye puruṣaṃ dhāraṇāḥ smṛtāḥ |
kalā kiṃcitkriyārūpā yakāro vātasaṃjñitaḥ [B: saṃjñakaḥ] || 13 ||

kiṃcijjñānātmikā vidyā rephaḥ pāvakasajñitaḥ |
stambhamohātmikā māyā lakāraḥ pṛthivī mataḥ || 14 ||

p. 65)

rañjanātmā rāgaśaktirvakāro varuṇātmakaḥ |
parāparadaśāmadhye dhārayantyo naraṃ sadā || 15 ||

catasro dhāraṇā jñeyāstā etāstattvakovidaiḥ |
śādikṣāntaṃ tu vijñeyaṃ viśuddhaṃ brahmapañcakam || 16 ||

śaṣasaho'niruddhādyā vijñeyāstridaśeśvara |
sṛjantyāḥ kṣubhitaṃ rūpaṃ sṛṣṭyādau yanmamādbhutam || 17 ||

17. aniruddhādyā iti krameṇānvayaḥ | śakāraḥ aniruddhātmakaḥ |

kṣobhikā sā mahāśaktiḥ kṣātmā satyāparāhvayā |
pṛthivyādyā viyatprāntā yā divyāḥ pañca śaktayaḥ || 18 ||

18. kṣakārasya satyātmakatvamāha - kṣobhiketi |

balādipañcakātmāno divyā matsattvanāmikāḥ |
jñānātmāno mamodyatyāstā etāḥ śādiśaktayaḥ || 19 ||

19. balādīti | balaiśvaryavīryaśaktitejāṃsītyarthaḥ |

visargo nāma yaḥ proktaḥ purā pañcadaśāṅgavān |
sāhaṃ somamayī śaktiḥ kiraṇāyutasaṃkulā || 20 ||

[c: omits 4 lines from here] saṃkocaśca vikāsaśca tāveva parikīrtitau |
aṅgānāmantimo yastu proktaḥ pañcadaśo mayā || 21 ||

21. binduḥ saṃkocaḥ | visargaḥ vikāsaḥ | antima iti | bindurityarthaḥ |

ādānaśīlaṃ taṃ viddhi sūryaṃ bhoktāramañjasā |
sūryācandramasāvetau bindusargau puraṃdara || 22 ||

22. bhoktāram saṃhārakamityarthaḥ | binduḥ sūryaḥ | visargaścandramā ityarthaḥ |

[B: omits 3 lines from here] kiraṇāḥ sapta sapta syurdevayoranayordvayoḥ |
caturdaśa svarāḥ śiṣṭāḥ sapta yugmāni kalpayet || 23 ||

teṣu saptasu yugmeṣu pūrve sapta puraṃdara |
śoṣakāḥ sūryarūpāyā bhoktrākhyāyā mamāṃśavaḥ || 24 ||

24. pūrve sapteti | a i u ṛ ḷ e o iti saptetyarthaḥ |

uttare sapta yugmeṣu śītalāhlādakāriṇaḥ |
poṣakāḥ somarūpāyā bhogyākhyāyā mamāṃśavaḥ || 25 ||

25. uttare sapteti | ā ī ū ṝ ḹ ai au iti saptetyarthaḥ |

ālokastīkṣṇatā vyāptirgrahaṇaṃ kṣepaṇeraṇe |
pāka ityuditāḥ pūrve kiraṇāḥ sūryasaṃbhavāḥ || 26 ||

26. akārādyokārāntānāṃ saptānāṃ yugmasthapūrvavarṇānāṃ 
sūryakiraṇātmakatvaṃ guṇāṃścāha - āloka ityādi | tathā ca jayākhye - 
ālokastīkṣṇatā vyāptirgrahaṇaṃ kṣapaṇeraṇe | pākaḥ prāptiriti hyaṣṭau 
sūryabhāge vyavasthitāḥ | akārādiṣu hrasveṣu varṇeṣveteṣvanukramāt || (6##-

dravatā [F: bhāvanā] śītabhāvaśca śāntiḥ [ā, B, c: 
śaktiḥ] kāntiḥ prasannatā |
rasatānanda ityete sapta cāndramasāḥ karāḥ || 27 ||

27. evamuttaravarṇānāṃ somakiraṇātmakatvaṃ guṇāṃścāha - 
dravatetyādinā | jayākhye ca - dravatā śaityabhāvaśca tṛptiḥ kāntiḥ 
prasannatā | rasatāsvāda ānando hyaṣṭau cāndrā ime matāḥ || (6-15) 
iti | atra visarga saṃyojyāṣṭatvaṃ bhāvyam |

agnīṣomātmakairebhiḥ [ḍ: etaiḥ] kiraṇaiḥ kāntiśālinī |
pumāṃsaṃ bindurūpaṃ tamaṅgīkṛtya viśeṣaṇī || 28 ||

somarūpottarā śaktikoṭimaṇḍalamaṇḍitā |
mahāsṛṣṭirmahānandā pravarte'ntyasvarātmanā || 29 ||

tasyāḥ pravartamānāyā [B: tasyāṃ pravartamānāyāṃ] udgataṃ 
brahmapañcakam |
kṣādi śāntaṃ sureśāna śaktyunmeṣaviśeṣitam || 30 ||

p. 66)

kṣa ityeva mahākṣobha uditaḥ satyasaṃjñayā |
vāsudevākhyayā ho'bhūt sākhyaḥ saṃkarṣaṇodayaḥ || 31 ||

31. kṣādiśāntādhiṣṭhāyibrahmapañcakanāmānyāha - satyetyādinā |

pradyumnaḥ pākhyayā jñeyo hyaniruddhastu śākhyayā |
tā etāḥ śaktayaḥ pañca pañcabrahmātmikāḥ parāḥ || 32 ||

32. brahmātmikāḥ brahmamayya ityarthaḥ |

sphūrtayo madabhinnāstā jagadutpattihetavaḥ |
jvālā iva mahāvahnerbrahmaṇo mama śaktayaḥ || 33 ||

33. brahmaṇo mameti | brahmābhinnāyā mametyarthaḥ |

catasro dhāraṇā jātā vādyā yāntāḥ puraṃdaraḥ |
turyādyā jāgradantāstā avasthāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 34 ||

pumāṃsaṃ dhārayantyetā madhyato daśayordvayoḥ |
yaiṣā brahmadaśā proktā prākṛtī bhādikā [ā, B: bhautikī] ca yā || 
35 ||

35. madhyata iti | parāparadaśayormadhye ityarthaḥ | bhādiketi | 
bhakārādikakāraparyantādhiṣṭhātṛbhūtāḥ prakṛtyādipṛthivyantā jñeyāḥ 
|

madhye tayormakārākhyo dhāraṇānāṃ catuṣkataḥ |
dhriyate [ā: śrayate; F: kriyate] sa pumān prokto jāgradādivibhedavān || 36 
||

yadi na dhriyate tābhirdaśāmanyatarāṃ vrajet |
brāhmīṃ vā prākṛtīṃ vāpi naiva syāt saṃsṛtistataḥ || 37 ||

ityarthaṃ [ā, B: ya itthaṃ] dhāraṇā mattaḥ prādurbhūtā 
mamājñayā |
tato daśācatuṣkasthaḥ puruṣo bhoktṛsaṃjñakaḥ || 38 ||

38. dhāraṇāḥ antaḥsthāḥ | brahmaprākṛtadaśāmadhyasthatvāt antaḥsthā 
ityuktāḥ | ahirbudhnye tu - antaḥsthā iti ca proktā antaḥsthapuruṣeśayāḥ iti 
vyutpattyantaramuktam |

matto jajñe ma ityevaṃ yogyo bhogāpavargayoḥ |
bhogānāṃ prasavārthāya puruṣasyāsya vāsava || 39 ||

39. matto jajñe ma ityevamityanena maśabdasya jīvavācakasya vyutpattirabhipretā | 
manadhātorniṣpattirapyanyatroktā |

acaitanyaṃ paraṃ sūkṣmaṃ guṇasāmyamanulbaṇam |
yonisvabhāvasaṃjñātaṃ matto'bhūdbha iti svayam || 40 ||

40. acaitanyamiti bahuvrīhiḥ | acetanamityarthaḥ | acetanā parārthā ca nityā 
satatavikriyā ityanyatroktam | anulvaṇam avyaktamityarthaḥ | yoniḥ svabhāva iti ca 
tannāma | bha iti bhakāravācyatvenetyarthaḥ |

p. 67)

bhogyabhogādisiddhyarthaṃ [B: bhogyadattvādi-] bhuñjānasya 
vipaścitaḥ [F: avipaścitaḥ] |
bādyāt kakāraparyantādvarṇagrāmāt puraṃdara || 41 ||

41. tattvāvirbhāvakramamanurudhya bādyādityādinā varṇānāṃ vyutkrameṇa 
nirdeśaḥ kṛtaḥ |

vyaktāni jajñire matto viṃśatistrīṇi ca kramāt |
buddhyahaṃkāramanasāṃ sṛṣṭirbāditrayāttathā || 42 ||

vāgāderṇādiṭānteṣu pañcakasya samudbhavaḥ || 43 ||

śabdādyāḥ [ī: vyomādyā] pañcatanmātrā ñādicānteṣu jajñire |

viddhi bodhaṃ tu madrūpaṃ sarveyaṃ mattatistataḥ || 45 ||

varṇādhvanastviyaṃ rītirmadhyamā kathitā tava |
ādyāmantāṃ ca deveśa gadantyā me niśāmaya || 46 ||

iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre [ā, ḍ: śrīpañcarātra] lakṣmītantre [ā:

viṃśo'dhyāyaḥ 

śakraḥ -

namo nikhilanirmāṇatrāṇasaṃhāraśaktaye |
hareḥ svarūpabhūtāyai namaste jñānarūpiṇi || 1 ||

tvatprasādānmayā padme rahasyaṃ paramaṃ śrutam |
varṇādhvānaṃ yathāvanme [ā, B: padme] bhūyastvaṃ vaktumarhasi || 
2 ||

śrīḥ [c: śrīruvāca] -

śṛṇu varṇādhvano rītimādyāṃ tridaśapuṃgava |
prāpnoti yatparijñānātsādhako matsarūpatām [c: matsvarūpatām] || 3 ||

vedyavedakanirmuktamacyutaṃ brahma yat param |
anastamitabhārūpaṃ sarvābhinnamahaṃpadam || 4 ||

ahaṃtā nāma sā śaktistadabhinnā sadoditā |
anastamitabhārūpā vedyavedakavarjitā || 5 ||

prakāśānandasārāhaṃ sarvataḥ samatāṃ gatā |
koṭikoṭyayutaikāṃśakoṭyaṃśe kṣubhitā satī || 6 ||

śabdabrahmasvarūpeṇa svaśaktyā svayameva hi |
muktaye'khilajīvānāmudemi parameśvarāt || 7 ||

tadavyaktākṣaraṃ viddhi tantrīśabdo yathā kalaḥ |
pṛthagvarṇātmanā yāti sthitaye naikadhā [F: anekadhā] sa tu || 8 ||

sūkṣmavarṇasvarūpo'sau dhārāsaṃtānarūpadhṛt |
pañcādhvakośamuktasya manniṣṭhasya vivekinaḥ [B: vivekataḥ] || 9 ||

9. bhuvanādhvapadādhvamantrādhvatattvādhvakalādhvānaḥ pañcādhvānaḥ | 
māyāprasūtiprakṛtibrahmāṇḍajīvadehākhyāḥ pañca kośāḥ |

anubhūtipadaṃ [ā, B, c, ḍ: so'nubhūti] yāti prasādāt 
paramātmanaḥ |
maccāturātmyanicayo vijñeyo hi tadātmanā [c: tadātmanām] || 10 ||

10. cāturātmyaṃ vāsudevasaṃkarṣaṇādikam | tadātmanā varṇātmanā | 
taccānantarameva vakṣyate |

prabhavāpyayayogena bhārupadhvanilakṣaṇaḥ |
sakārāntastvakārācca hakārādānta eva ca || 11 ||

11. utpattikrame dhvanilakṣaṇaḥ | apyayakrame parārūpeṇa cillakṣaṇaḥ | 
akāramārabhya sakāraparyantaṃ gaṇane aṣṭacatvāriṃśat varṇā bhavanti | yathā 
- svarāḥ ṣoḍaśa sparśāḥ pañcaviṃśatiḥ antaḥsthāścatvāraḥ hakāraṃ 
vihāyoṣmāṇastraya iti | evaṃ hakāramārabhya dīrghākāraparyantagaṇane'pi |

prabhave dvādaśāntastu hakāraścaturātmanām |
akārastvapyaye caitau dvādaśāntāvubhau samau || 12 ||

12. caturātmanāṃ vāsudevādīnāṃ prabhave hakāro dvādaśānto bhavati | apyaye 
tu akārastathā | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ - pūrvoktāṣṭacatvāriṃśadvarṇānāṃ 
dvādaśadhā vibhāge prativibhāgaṃ catvāro varṇāḥ saṃpadyante | ete ca krameṇa 
vāsudevādibhiścaturbhiradhiṣṭhitā bhavanti | dvādaśabhāgānte prabhave hakāraḥ 
laye akāraśca bhavata iti |

vārṇe vyūhasamūhe'smin jñeyaṃ jñānasamādhinā |
viśrāma udayo vyāptirvyaktirā vāsudevataḥ || 13 ||

13. prativibhāgamāsthitā vāsudevādayaḥ krameṇa viśrāmodayavyāptivyaktirūpā 
jñeyāḥ |

p. 69)

atraikaikopari jñeyā mūrtirvai tvevameva hi |
[c: omits four lines from here] yuktā viśrāmapūrveṇa catuṣkeṇa 
samāsataḥ || 14 ||

viśrāmaṃ cintayeddevaṃ vāsudevaṃ sanātanam |
akāraṃ puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ pūrvadevaṃ sanātanam || 15 ||

15. tadeva viśadayati - viśrāmamityādinā |

saṃkarṣaṇāditattvāni viśrāmyanti laye'tra hi |
tataḥ saṃkarṣaṇaṃ devamākāramudayaṃ smaret || 16 ||

udito hi sa sarvātmā prathamaṃ sarvakṛt svayam |
vyāptaṃ pradyumnadevaṃ tamikāraṃ paricintayet || 17 ||

vividhaṃ prāpyate tena trayīkarmātmanā jagat |
aniruddhaṃ vyaktirūpamīkāraṃ tamanusmaret || 18 ||

vyajyante śaktayo hyatra jagatsṛṣṭyādayo'khilāḥ |
daṇḍavatsaṃniveśena saṃsthitā hyevameva hi || 19 ||

ā sakārāccatūrūpayuktā me caturātmatā |
smaret prabhavacintāyāṃ hakāraṃ dvādaśāntakam || 20 ||

20. ā sakārāditi | sakāraparyantamityarthaḥ |

hakāraṃ vāsudevaṃ tu viśrāmaṃ paricintayet |
saṃkarṣaṇaṃ sakāraṃ tamudayaṃ tvapyaye smaret || 21 ||

21. layakrame cāturātmyaniveśamāha - hakāramiti |

evamākārato divyāṃ cintayeccaturātmatām |
dviṣaṭkaṃ dhāraṇānāṃ ca dvādaśādhyātmalakṣaṇaṃ [B: cintanam] || 
22 ||

22. ākārata iti | ākāraparyantamityarthaḥ |

sopānabhūtaṃ yatkrāntvā dvādaśāntādviśet param [B, ḍ: puram] |
eṣā sā prathamā rītirvarṇamārgasya darśitā || 23 ||

sūkṣmā taccāturātmīyā bhārūpā manmayī parā |
madhyamā pūrvamevoktā viśeṣaṃ tatra me śṛṇu || 24 ||

dhāraṇāḥ pūrvamuktā yāścatasro matsvarūpikāḥ |
vakārākhyāniruddhasya śaktiḥ sā rāgasaṃjñitā [c: saṃjñikā] || 25 ||

māyā nāma mahālakṣmīrlakārāparanāmikā |
vidyā yā rephasaṃjñātā mahāvāṇī tu sā smṛtā || 26 ||

vātasaṃjñā mahākālī kriyāśaktiryakāriṇī |
brahmādyā mūrtayastisraḥ patnyastrayyādayaśca yāḥ || 27 ||

tajjñeyaṃ sakalaṃ sūkṣmamakārasyādimeṃ'śake |
madhyame bhoktṛkūṭasthaḥ puruṣoṃ'śe pratiṣṭhitaḥ || 28 ||

saṃsārī puruṣaḥ sarvaścaramāṃśe'vatiṣṭhate |
eṣā te madhyamā rītirvarṇamārgasya darśitā || 29 ||

caramāmatha vakṣyāmi rītiṃ balaniṣūdana |
vaikharī caramā rītiḥ prayatnasthānabhedinī || 30 ||

vyaktavācāṃ samuccāre sā sphuṭībhavati dhruvam |
jīvānāṃ dehabaddhānāṃ tattatsanmārgadarśikā || 31 ||

mātṛkā jāyate seyaṃ viṣṇuśaktyupabṛṃhitā |
viṣṇuvattatra pañcāśacchaktayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 32 ||

[c: omits 4 lines from here] adhitiṣṭhanti ye yāṃ ca mātṛkāṃ 
varṇamālinīm |
vāsudevādayo vyūhā daśa dvau keśavādayaḥ || 33 ||

svarādhiṣṭhāyino devāḥ śaktīsteṣāmimāḥ śṛṇu |
lakṣmīḥ kīrtirjayā māyā vyūhaśaktaya īritāḥ || 34 ||

śrīśca vāgīśvarī kāntikriyāśāntivibhūtayaḥ |
icchā prītī ratiścaiva māyā dhīrmahimeti ca || 35 ||

śaktayaḥ keśavādīnāṃ tā etāḥ svaraśaktayaḥ |
kādyadhiṣṭhāyino devān gadantyā me niśāmaya || 36 ||

padmanābhādināmāno viṣṇavaḥ kādidevatāḥ |
padmanābho dhruvo'nantaḥ śaktīśo madhusūdanaḥ || 37 ||

37. padmanābhādayo vibhavabhedāḥ pūrvamevaikādaśādhyāye paṭhitā 
jñātavyāḥ |

p. 70)

vidyādhidevaḥ kapilo viśvarūpo vihaṃgamaḥ |
kroḍātmā [ī: dharmātmā] baḍabāvaktro dharmo vāgīśvarastathā || 38 ||

ekārṇavaśayo devaḥ kūrmaḥ pātāladhārakaḥ |
varāho nārasiṃhaścāpyamṛtāharaṇastathā || 39 ||

śrīpatirdivyadeho'tha kāntātmāmṛtadhārakaḥ |
rāhujit kālanemighnaḥ pārijātaharo mahān || 40 ||

lokanāthastu śāntātmā dattātreyo mahāprabhuḥ |
nyagrodhaśāyī bhagavānekaśṛṅgatanustataḥ || 41 ||

devo vāmanadehastu sarvavyāpī trivikramaḥ |
naro nārāyaṇaścaiva hariḥ kṛṣṇastathaiva ca || 42 ||

jvalatparaśudhṛdrāmo rāmaścānyo dhanurdharaḥ |
vedavidbhagavān kalkī pātālaśayanaḥ prabhuḥ || 43 ||

eteṣvantyeṣu catvāro devā rāmādayo hi ye |
te raṅgayamayorjihvāmūlopadhmānayorapi || 44 ||

44. raṅgaḥ anunāsikaḥ | yamāḥ prātiśākhye prasiddhā varṇaviśeṣāḥ |

dhīstārā vāruṇī śaktiḥ padmā vidyā tathaiva ca |
saṃkhyā viśvā khagā bhūrgaurlakṣmīrvāgīśvarī tathā || 45 ||

amṛtā dharaṇī chāyā nārahiṃhī sudhā tathā [B, c: tathā sudhā] |
śrīḥ kīrtirviśvakāmā mā satyā kāntiḥ saroruhā || 46 ||

māyā padmāsanā kharvā vikrāntirnarasaṃbhavā |
nārāyaṇī hariprītirgāndhārī kāśyapī tathā || 47 ||

vaidehī vedavidyā [B: deva-] ca padminī nāgaśāyinī [c: 
padmavāsinī] |
madaṃśakā imā devyo vijñeyāḥ kādiśaktayaḥ || 48 ||

bhavopakaraṇaiśceyaṃ mātṛkādhiṣṭhitā suraiḥ |
śrīkaṇṭhānandasūkṣmādyairlambodaryādiśaktibhiḥ || 49 ||

vināyakaiśca durgābhiḥ kṣetreśairmātṛbhistathā |
samayasthaistathā boddhairārhatairapi cāparaiḥ || 50 ||

yathā hi kṣudhitā bālā mātaraṃ paryupāsate |
evaṃ sarve surā devīṃ mātṛkāṃ paryupāsate || 51 ||

iyaṃ yonirhi mantrāṇāṃ vidyānāṃ janmabhūriyam |
tattvānāṃ tātvikānāṃ ca jñānānāṃ prasavasthalī || 52 ||

iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre [ā: śrīpañcarātra; ī: śrīpañcarātre] lakṣmītantre 
mātṛkāprakāśo nāma viṃśo'dhyāyaḥ || 20 ||
p. 71)

ekaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

śakraḥ - 

śabdārthavyaktirūpāyai ṣaḍadhvaparivartini |
adhvātītāvabodhākhye namaste harivallabhe || 1 ||

1. ṣaḍadhveti | varṇakalātattvamantrapadabhuvanākhyāḥ ṣaḍadhvānaḥ |

varṇāḥ prakāśitā devi yathāvat sarvahetavaḥ |
mantramārgamidānīṃ me yathāvadvaktumarhasi || 2 ||

2. sarveti | śabdānāmarthānāṃ cetyarthaḥ |

śrīḥ -

eka eva paro devaḥ śrīmān puruṣasattamaḥ |
ṣāḍguṇyāmbhonidhirdivyaḥ sarvātmā sarvatomukhaḥ || 3 ||

tasyāhaṃ paramā śaktirahaṃtā śrīrabhedinī |
sarvādhārā sarvaśaktiḥ sarvajñā sarvatomukhī || 4 ||

mayi prakāśate viśvaṃ darpaṇodaraśailavat |
bodha eva svarūpaṃ me nirmalānandalakṣaṇaḥ || 5 ||

icchāparavatī sāhaṃ bodhakāṃśavivartinī |
śabdabrahmamayī bhūtvā vivarte'haṃ kalādhvanā || 6 ||

6. icchāparavatīti | svacchandetyarthaḥ | vivartaḥ pariṇāmaḥ | kalādhvaneti | 
jñānādiguṇātmanetyarthaḥ |

kalā jñānādayaḥ proktāḥ ṣaḍguṇāḥ pārameśvarāḥ |
tāsāṃ trikadviyogena vivarte tattvavartmanā || 7 ||

7. trikadviyogena yugalatrayayogenetyarthaḥ |

saṃkarṣaṇādayo devāstattvāni surasattama |
varṇavyatikarairbhūyo vivarte mantravartmanā || 8 ||

tasya mantrādhvano vyaktiṃ gadantyā me niśāmaya |
śabdabrahmavivarto'yaṃ kiraṇāyutasaṃkulaḥ || 9 ||

cillakṣaṇaḥ ṣaḍguṇātmā tasya bhedaścaturvidhaḥ |
kvacidbījaṃ kvacitpiṇḍaṃ kvacitsaṃjñā kvacitpadam || 10 ||

turyaṃ suṣuptiḥ svapnaśca jāgradbījādayaḥ kramāt |
ekasvaraṃ dvisvaraṃ vā svaravyañjanayordvayam || 11 ||

11. bījapiṇḍasaṃjñāpadamantrāḥ krameṇa 
turyādijāgradantapadacatuṣṭayasaṃgatā jñeyā ityarthaḥ |

bījaṃ bahusvaraṃ vāpi vijñeyaṃ vibudheśvara |
antarā harayaḥ [ā: antarāhurayaḥ; B, ṅ: antarāhalayaḥ; c: antarākulayaḥ; 
ḍ: anantarāvilaṃ] piṇḍaṃ kvacitsvarasamāyutam || 12 ||

12. antarā madhye sthitāḥ harayaḥ vyañjanāni piṇḍamantra ityarthaḥ | 
tasyāpavādamāha - kvaciditi |

tattadvācyābhidhā saṃjñā namaḥpraṇavasaṃyutā |
kriyākārakasaṃyogastutisaṃbodhalakṣaṇaḥ || 13 ||

13. padamantrasvarūpamāha - kriyetyādi | saṃbodhaḥ saṃbodhanam |

nānābhijñāsamāyuktaḥ padātmā mantra ucyate |
etaccatuṣṭayaṃ mantraṃ saṃpūrṇaṃ devatātmani || 14 ||

14. abhijñā saṃjñā |

p. 72)

sā catuṣṭayasaṃbaddhā siddhimiṣṭāṃ prayacchati |
kṣetrakṣetrajñabhāvaṃ ca mantrāṇāṃ tridaśeśvara || 15 ||

15. sā devatā |

vijñāya tattvato mantrān prayuñjīta vicakṣaṇaḥ |

śakraḥ - 

kṣetrakṣetrajñasadbhāvaṃ mantrāṇāṃ vada me'mbuje || 16 ||

[F: omits four lines from here] yadvijñāya na muhyanti siddhimeṣyanti 
cācirāt |

śrīḥ - 

bījaṃ bījavatāṃ jīvaḥ śiṣṭaṃ kṣetraṃ prakīrtitam || 17 ||

17. bījavatāṃ mantrāṇāṃ bījākṣaraṃ jīva ityarthaḥ |

nirbījānāmādi jīvaḥ kṣetraṃ tu pariśeṣitam |
bījānāṃ caiva piṇḍānāmastu kṣetrajña ucyate || 18 ||

18. ādīti | ādyakṣaramityarthaḥ | astu akārastvityarthaḥ |

śiṣṭaṃ tu kṣetramuddiṣṭamakārarahite punaḥ |
kṣetrajñaḥ svara uddiṣṭaḥ kevale ca svare punaḥ || 19 ||

19. akārarahite punariti uttaratrānveti | svaraḥ yaḥ kaścit svara ityarthaḥ | kevale ca 
svare ityuttaratrānveti |

jīvaḥ syāt prathamā mātrā dvitīyādi tanurbhavet [B: manurbhavet] |
ekamātre tu jīvaḥ syāt saṃskāro'dbhutalakṣaṇaḥ [ī: 
saṃskārodbodhalakṣaṇaḥ] || 20 ||

20. saṃskāra iti | madhyamā vāgityarthaḥ | atrāṣṭādaśādhyāyasthaḥ ṣaḍviṃśaḥ 
śloko'vadheyaḥ |

uccāryamāṇaṃ kṣetraṃ syānniḥsvare piṇḍake punaḥ |
prathamo jīva uddiṣṭaḥ śiṣṭaṃ kṣetraṃ pracakṣate [ī: kṣetrajña ucyate] || 
21 ||

21. uccāryamāṇamiti | vaikharītyarthaḥ |

kṣetrakṣetrajñasadbhāva eṣa te saṃpradarśitaḥ |
ādau madhye tathānte ca triṣu vānyataratra vā || 22 ||

eṣāṃ piṇḍo'thavā bījaṃ te mantrāḥ sārvakālikāḥ |
bījābhāve tu mantrāṇāṃ bījaṃ kṛtvādimākṣaram || 23 ||

23. yatra mantre bījaṃ nāsti tatra prathamākṣaramanusvārayutaṃ bījamityarthaḥ | 
yathā gaṇapataye namaḥ ityatra gaṃ iti bījamantraḥ |

anusvārayutaṃ paścāt sakalaṃ mantra ucyate |
prakrīḍayanti puruṣaṃ mantrā rāgeṇa rañjitam || 24 ||

caturdaśavibhāgasthe prākṛte bhuvanādhvani |
turyavarjaṃ suṣuptyādye prākṛte ca padādhvani || 25 ||

ācāryadṛṣṭipātasthaṃ puruṣaṃ saṃyatendriyam |
prasādasumukhā mantrā uttārya bhuvanādhvanaḥ || 26 ||

[ī: omits three lines from here] padādhvanaśca vairāgyaṃ janayantaḥ pade 
pade |
kramāttattvakalāvarṇapadavīṣu nayanti tam || 27 ||

māntraṃ prāsādamāsādya nirdhūtāśeṣabandhanaḥ |
lakṣmīnārāyaṇākhyaṃ tadviśati brahma śāśvatam || 28 ||

śakraḥ -

ācāryaḥ kīdṛśo devi śiṣyastasya ca kīdṛśaḥ |
mantreṣu katamo mantraḥ prabhavet paramāptaye || 29 ||

kathaṃ sa copadeṣṭavya etad brūhi namo'stu te |

śrīḥ -

sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyukto [c: saṃpanno] brāhmaṇo vedapāragaḥ || 30 ||

p. 73)

ṣaṭkarmanirataḥ śāntaḥ pañcakālarataḥ śuciḥ |
pañcarātrārthavinmaunī mantrākṣarakṛtaśramaḥ || 31 ||

na sthūlo na kṛśo hrasvo na kāṇo naiva rogavān |
nāndho na badhiro mūḍho na khalvāṭo na paṅgukaḥ || 32 ||

na hīnāṅgo'tiriktāṅgo na śvitrī na ca ḍāmbhikaḥ |
na krodhano na duścarmā na lobhahatacetanaḥ || 33 ||

akulīnaṃ durācāraṃ śaṭhaṃ jihmaṃ ca varjayet |
dayādāntiśamopetaṃ [B: śāntidamopetaṃ; ḍ, ī: śamadamopetaṃ] 
dṛḍhabhaktiṃ kriyāparam || 34 ||

satyavākchīlasaṃpannaṃ rekhākarmasu kauśalam |
jitendriyaṃ susaṃtuṣṭaṃ karuṇāpūrṇamānasam || 35 ||

ācāryalakṣaṇasaṃpannamārjavaṃ [F: āryaṃ-; ī: kuryāt-] 
cāruhāsinam |
evaṃ guṇagaṇākīrṇaṃ [c, B: evaṃguruguṇā-] guruṃ vidyāttu 
vaiṣṇavam [F: kevalam] || 36 ||

śiṣyaśca tādṛśo jñeyaḥ sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitaḥ |
kṣāntiśīlaṃ suddhīmantaṃ [B: suśīlaṃ taṃ] krodhalobhavivarjitam || 37 ||

snānārcanarataṃ [c: paraṃ] nityaṃ guruśuśrūṣaṇodyatam |
viprāgnidevapitṛṣu bhaktaṃ tarpaṇaśīlinam || 38 ||

kulīnaṃ ca tathā prājñaṃ śāsthrārthanirataṃ sadā |
brāhmaṇaṃ kṣatriyaṃ vaiśyaṃ śūdraṃ vā bhagavatparam || 39 ||

īdṛglakṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ śiṣyamārjavasaṃyutam |
varṇadharmakriyopetāṃ nārīṃ vā sadvivekinīm || 40 ||

vidyādanumate [ī: dadyāt-] palyurananyāṃ patimāninīm |
evaṃlakṣaṇakaṃ śiṣyamācāryo bhagavanmayaḥ |
jñāpayedvidhivanmantrān [c: śrāvayedvividhān-] gurudṛṣṭyā 
samīkṣya tu || 41 ||

41. anena strīśūdrayorapi tāntrikamantragrahaṇe adhikāro vidhīyate | paraṃ tu 
tāraviṣaye viśeṣo granthāntareṣvavagantavyaḥ |

iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre [ā: śrīpañcarātra; B, ḍ: śrīpāñcarātre] 
lakṣmītantre guruśiṣyalakṣaṇaṃ nāma ekaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ || 21 ||
p. 74)

dvāviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

śakraḥ -

anādinidhane devi sarvajñe harivallabhe |
kathaṃ vai jñāpayenmantrāṃsteṣāṃ rūpaṃ ca kīdṛśam || 1 ||

samapradhānatā vaiṣāmuta jyeṣṭhakaniṣṭhatā |
etat pṛṣṭā mayā brūhi namaste sarasīruhe || 2 ||

śrīḥ - 

śṛṇu sarvamaśeṣeṇa mattastvaṃ pākaśāsana |
yathāsmi mantrarūpāhaṃ yathā ca jñāpayāmi tān || 3 ||

3. jñāpayāmīti | ācāryarūpeṇa bodhayāmītyarthaḥ | 

paraṃ brahma paraṃ dhāma ṣāḍguṇyamamalojjvalam |
deśakālānavacchinnamanākāramanūpamam || 4 ||

4. anūpamamiti | anupamamityarthaḥ |

ahamityeva tadbrahma svātmasaṃbodhi nirguṇam |
anādinidhanaṃ divyaṃ lakṣmīnārāyaṇaṃ mahat || 5 ||

5. nirguṇam sattvādiprākṛtaguṇahīnam |

cidānandarasaṃ divyamakhaṇḍamajarāmaram |
anunmiṣadbhavadbhāvaṃ grāhyagrāhakavarjitam [ī: grāhakatojjhitam] || 6 ||

6. anunmiṣadityādi | śāntaparāvasthāyāṃ bhavadbhāvarūponmeṣarahitamityarthaḥ 
| tathā grāhyetyādikamapi bhāvyam |

stimitaṃ tat paraṃ brahma tasya stimitatāsmyaham |
tat kadācitparaṃ brahma bhavadbhāvavyavasthayā || 7 ||

unmiṣatyajahadrūpaṃ svecchayaiva kadācana |
sāhaṃ [ḍ: ahaṃ] bhāvātmikāhaṃtā saṃpūjyā [B: saṃpūrṇā; F: 
pūjitā] paramātmanaḥ || 8 ||

8. unmiṣati prakāśata ityarthaḥ | ajahadityādi | svābhāvikaṃ 
nirvikāratvamajahadityarthaḥ |

udemi bhavato devādicchayaiva vivasvataḥ [c: vyavasthitā] |
saṃbhṛtyākhilasaṃbhāramicchayaiva svanirmitam || 9 ||

9. vivasvataḥ tejomayādbhagavata ityarthaḥ |

svabhittau likhitaṃ nītvā [ā: nītaṃ; ī: vilikhantīdaṃ] prabhavāmi 
ṣaḍadhvanā |
vārṇaḥ kalāmayaścaiva tāttviko māntrikastathā || 10 ||

pādiko bhauvanaścaiva ṣaḍadhvānaḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
paramaṃ yadahaṃtākhyaṃ saṃvidrūpamanāmayam [B: saccidrūpam] || 11 
||

unmeṣaḥ prathamastasya varṇādhvā parikīrtitaḥ |
vyākṛtirdaśitā tasya pūrvaṃ te pākaśāsana || 12 ||

tadeva paramaṃ rūpaṃ mama saṃvinmayaṃ mahat |
vivartate'dhvabhāvena jñānādyena kalātmanā || 13 ||

jñānādīnāṃ kalānāṃ tu guṇānāṃ paramātmanaḥ |
pūrvaṃ te kathitā sūktiryāvanto yādṛśāśca te || 14 ||

adhvadvayamupādāya tadrūpaṃ mama cinmayam |
vāsudevādirūpeṇa vartate tattvavartmanā || 15 || 

15. adhvadvayam varṇādhvānaṃ kalādhvānaṃ cetyarthaḥ |

p. 75)

vyūhāśca vibhavāścaiva yaccānyadbhagavanmayam |
tattvādhvano vivṛttiḥ sā kīrtitā paramātmanaḥ || 16 ||

16. anyat vyūhāntarāṇi vibhavāntarāṇi cetyarthaḥ |

pūrvādhvadvayamādāya tadeva mama cinmayam |
rūpaṃ vivartate śaśvanmāntreṇa paramādhvanā || 17 ||

uttāraṇāya jīvānāṃ magnānāṃ bhavasāgare |
bhogāya bhavasaṃsthānāṃ vairāgyajananāya ca || 18 ||

18. mantrādhvaprayojanamāha - uttāraṇāyetyādinā |

ārādhanasya [ī: ārādhanaprasiddhyartham] siddhyarthaṃ 
mānasālambanāya ca |
mantrādhvā paramodāro mama cidrūpalakṣaṇaḥ || 19 ||

19. mānasālambanāyeti | śubhāśrayarūpapradarśanena yogināṃ mānasasya 
dhyānālambanapradānāyetyarthaḥ | etacca śrīviṣṇupurāṇe prapañcitam |

vāsudevādidevānāṃ mūrtibhāvaṃ vrajatyasau |
mantrāḥ sarve cidātmānaḥ sarvagāḥ sarvasādhakāḥ || 20 ||

20. atha prakṛtaṃ mantramahimānamāha - mantrā ityādinā |

trāyamāṇāśca mantāraṃ guptarūpāśca śāstrataḥ |
bhogāpavargadā hyete devadevasya śarṅgiṇaḥ || 21 ||

21. mahimavarṇanamukhena mantraśabdayaugikārthamāha - trāyamāṇāśceti | 
mandhātoḥ traidhātośca vyutpanna iti bhāvaḥ | vyutpattyantaramāha - gupteti | 
matri guptabhāṣaṇe iti dhātuḥ |

rūpaṃ me bhagavanmantrā [B: maghavan] vijñeyā mūrtayo'malāḥ |
jāgratsvapnau [ḍ: svapnaḥ] suṣuptiśca turyaṃ ceti catuṣṭayam || 22 ||

jñeyaṃ padādhvano rūpaṃ jāgradbāhyendriyakramaḥ [ḍ: kramāt] |
bāhyendriyāṇāṃ tamasābhibhūte vibhave sati || 23 ||

23. svapnāvasthāṃ nirūpayati - bāhyetyādinā |

antaḥkaraṇavṛttiryā saṃskārapariśeṣiṇī |
sā svapna iti vijñeyā tadabhāve suṣuptikā || 24 ||

24. tadabhāve saṃskārābhāve |

tamasānabhibhūtasya sattvasthasya vipaścitaḥ |
bāhyāntaḥkaraṇasthāyā vṛtteruparame sati || 25 ||

śuddhasattvaprasādasya saṃtatisturyasaṃjñitā |
evaṃ caturvidhe mārge nirdiṣṭe'smin padābhidhe || 26 ||

turyavarjaṃ suṣuptyādiraśuddhāṃ bhajate gatim |
māyādikṣitiparyantā yoktā bhuvanapaddhatiḥ || 27 ||

27. bhuvanādhvānaṃ nirūpayati - māyādīti |

bhuvanādhvā sa vijñeyo hyaśuddho malapaṅkilaḥ |
prakrīḍayanti mantrāste śaśvadrāgaparaṃ naram || 28 ||

tattadbhogendrajālāni darśayanto vimohitam |
guruṇā sadayaṃ samyagvīkṣitaṃ karuṇādṛśā || 29 ||

uttārayanti vairāgyaṃ janayantaḥ pade pade |
tataḥ śuddhamayān mārgān prāpayantaḥ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ || 30 ||

30. pade pade jāgradādika ityarthaḥ |

śabdabrahmaṇi niṣṇātaṃ prāpayeyuḥ parāṃ śriyam |
evaṃvidhā mahātmāno mantrāḥ paramapāvanāḥ || 31 ||

31. śabdabrahmaṇi mantreṣvityarthaḥ |

trividhāste tu vijñeyā adhamā madhyamāḥ pare |
bhavopakaraṇānāṃ ye devānāṃ mūrtitāṃ gatāḥ || 32 ||

32. pare iti | uttamā ityarthaḥ | bhavopakaraṇāni sargopakaraṇabhūtāni |

antavatphaladā mantrāste jñeyā adhamā budhaiḥ |
mantrā vibhavadevānāṃ saśaktīnāṃ tu mūrtayaḥ || 33 ||

p. 76)

te jñeyā madhyamā mantrā uttamā vyūhamūrtayaḥ |
ye tu brahmāvagāhante lakṣmīnārāyaṇātmakam || 34 ||

bhavadbhāvavyavasthānāste mantrā uttamottamāḥ |
evaṃ ca jyaiṣṭhyakāniṣṭhyaṃ vijñeyaṃ mantrakovidaiḥ || 35 ||

uttamāḥ pañcarātrasthā madhyamāstu trayīmayāḥ |
tantrāntarasthā vijñeyā adhamāḥ śāstracakṣuṣā || 36 ||

36. pañcarātrasthā iti | ananyaparasāttvikatamabhāgavatoddeśapravṛttatvāt 
teṣāmuttamatvam | trayīmayā iti | traiguṇyaviṣayatvātteṣāṃ madhyamatvam | 

kalāṅgā uttamā jñeyā anyāṅgā madhyamāḥ smṛtāḥ |
anaṅgā adhamā mantrā bhūyaḥ śṛṇu sureśvara || 37 ||

37. kalāḥ jñānādayaḥ |

bījapiṇḍādisaṃyuktā uttamāḥ parikīrtitāḥ |
bījādyanyatamāntaḥsthā mantrā madhyamasaṃjñitāḥ || 38 ||

abījādiyutā jñeyā mantrā adhamasaṃjñitāḥ |
evaṃ mantravidhā jñātvā hyācāryaḥ śāstralocanaḥ |
yathāsvarūpataḥ [ḍ, F: yathānurūpataḥ] śiṣyān jñāpayedarthito 
manūn || 39 ||

iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre [ā: śrīpañcarātre; B: śrīpāñcarātre] 
lakṣmītantre ṣaḍadhvamantrasvarūpakathanaṃ nāma [ī: omits the title] 
dvāviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ || 22 ||
p. 77)

trayoviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

śrīḥ - 

vyāpakaṃ yat paraṃ brahma lakṣmīnārāyaṇaṃ mahat |
[B: omits this line] ahaṃtā paramā tasya śaktirnārāyaṇī hyaham || 1 ||

anugrahāya lokānāmahamācāryatāṃ gatā |
saṃkarṣaṇasvarūpeṇa śāstraṃ pradyotayāmyaham || 2 ||

punaśca gurumūrtisthā samyagvijñānaśālinī |
śaktimayyā svayā dṛṣṭyā karuṇāmantrapūrṇayā [c: pūrayā] || 3 ||

pālayāmi [B: pāṣayāmi] gururbhūtvā śiṣyānātmopasarpiṇaḥ |
tasmājjñeyaḥ sadā śiṣyairācāryo'sau [ḍ: ācāryaḥ saḥ] madātmakaḥ || 
4 ||

śiṣyaṃ saṃjñāpayenmantrān [B, ḍ, F, ṅ: yathāvat] yathā 
tadavadhāraya |
ādau śuddhe same snigdhe deśe bhūdoṣavarjite || 5 ||

5. bhūdoṣāḥ keśanakhāsthisaṃsargajāḥ ūṣaratvādayaśca | 

varṇānurūpavarṇāḍhye gomayenopalepite |
dhūpite śalyanirmukte gandhapuṣpādyalaṃkṛte || 6 ||

6. varṇānurūpetyādi | brāhmaṇādivarṇānuguṇasitādivarṇayukte ityarthaḥ | tathā 
coktaṃ paramasaṃhitāyām - brāhmaṇasya sitā bhūmiḥ kṣatriyasyāruṇā 
bhavet | pītavarṇā ca vaiśyasya kṛṣṇā śūdrasya kīrtitā || (7-5) iti | 
śalyāni kaṇṭakādīni |

pañcagavyena saṃsikte candanādyanulepite [B, c, F, ī: 
candanādyupalepite] |
sumṛnmayīṃ ca ghaṭikāṃ kevalaiḥ kusumaiḥ śubhaiḥ || 7 ||

7. kevalaiḥ visrastaiḥ agrathitairityarthaḥ |

praṇavādinamo'ntena svena mantreṇa pūjayet |

66. viparyasta iti | so'hamiti mantra ityarthaḥ | sakāreṇa śaktimādāya hakāre

ṣaḍviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

śakraḥ -

paraṃ brahma paraṃ dhāma padmasthe padmamālini |
namaste padmaje padme govindagṛhameghini || 1 ||

dve ete kathite devi tava tanvau sanātane [B, c: sanātani] |
viśeṣastvasti [ḍ, ī: viśeṣo'nvasti] vā kaścidanayoḥ sūkṣmarūpataḥ 
|| 2 ||

śrīḥ -

ekamādau paraṃ tattvaṃ lakṣmīnārāyaṇātmakam |
pūrṇastimitaṣāḍguṇyaṃ svacchasvacchandaciddhanam || 3 ||

tasyāhaṃ paramā śaktiḥ sarvāvasthānusāriṇī |
devī sā [ī: ahaṃtā] paramā divyā sthūlasūkṣmaparāhvayā || 4 ||

mama tanvāvime śaktī tārikā cānutārikā |
duhāte sakalān kāmānubhe ete [ī: kāmānugāvete] puraṃdara || 5 ||

ubhe ete mate divye ubhe niṣṭhe pare smṛte |
ubhe saṃsthā mate [ā, B, c: ume] sarvā ubhe te viṣṇuvallabhe || 6 
||

ubhe ete vicintyātha gacchanti paramāṃ gatim |
tattvaṃ tu paramaṃ sūkṣmaṃ gadantyā me niśāmaya || 7 ||

paraṃ brahma tataḥ śāntaṃ tato nāda iti kramaḥ |
sarvatrāvasthitā sāhaṃ [ā: bhāntī] nimeṣonmeṣarūpiṇī || 8 ||

ādyaṃ yat paramaṃ brahma sūkṣmaṃ stimitaśaktikam |
tārastatra pratiṣṭhāya tanoti vitatāṃ gatim || 9 ||

bhavato brahmaṇo yo'yamunmeṣaḥ paramātmanaḥ |
bhavadbhāvātmake tasmiṃstārikā pratitiṣṭhati || 10 ||

brahmaṇastvavaroho yaḥ śāntarūpaḥ sisṛkṣayā |
śāntākhye bhāvabhūyiṣṭhe tasminnāste'nutārikā || 11 ||

dvitīyastvavaroho yaḥ śaktyākhyo bhāva ūrjitaḥ |
vāgbhavādīni bījāni tatra tiṣṭhanti vāsava || 12 ||

etāvānanayorbhedaḥ proktaste sūkṣmadhīmayaḥ |

30-34. sārasvatabījamāha - ṣaṣṭhīmityādinā | prajñādhāra ūkāraḥ |

ādhārabhūtā prajñāyā vyomeśe saṃsthitā punaḥ || 33 ||

p. 90)

punarvisṛṣṭiyogāya parameśvaramāgatā |
ṣaṣṭhī samuddhṛtā vidyā śabdataścārthataśca te || 34 ||

iyaṃ bījatrayī vidyā kathitā tripurāhvayā |
vyutkramānukramābhyāṃ sā hyātmasāmyapradāpi ca [ā: ātmasādyasato'pi 
ca; B, c: ātmasāmyasame'pi ca; ḍ, F: samāsavyāsato'pi ca; ī: hyanyā sā 
vyāsato'pi ca] || 35 ||

vidyeyaṃ kāmadhuk proktā japahomādisādhitā |
vyañjanasvarasaṃyogāt [ā, ḍ: halaḥ svarādi] tasyā bhedān [c: 
asyā bhāgān] bahūnviduḥ || 36 ||

caturṇāṃ puruṣārthānāṃ hetūn vyasya samasya ca |
saptamī tu mahālakṣmīrvidyā sā sarvasādhanī || 37 ||

parāṃ prakṛtimādāya bhāskaraṃ tatra yojayet |
mardanena samāyojya yojayet kālavahninā || 38 ||

38-39. mahālakṣmībījoddhāramāha - parāmityādinā | prakṛtiṃ 
kakāramādāya tena bhāskaraṃ ṣakāraṃ mardanaṃ makāraṃ kālavahniṃ rephaṃ 
māyāmīkāraṃ vyāpinaṃ binduṃ ca yojayet | kṣmrīṃ iti mantraḥ | tathā ca 
tantrarāje - grāso nabho dāhavahnisvairyuktaḥ kaulinīmanuḥ iti | tatra grāsaḥ 
kṣakāraḥ nabho makāraḥ dāhavahniḥ rephaḥ svamīkāro binduśca |

bhūṣayenmāyayā piṇḍamante vyāpinamānayet |
kathitaṃ te mahālakṣmībījametat puraṃdara || 39 ||

karṣaṇtī vyākṛtāvasthāmahaṃ hi svena tejasā |
pradhānabhūmikāṃ gatvā mūrtitrayavibhāvinī || 40 ||

nirmāya sakalaṃ bhāvaṃ vyomeśe saṃpratiṣṭhitā |
iti bhāvyamidaṃ bījaṃ japatā sādhakena tu || 41 ||

ityete raśmayo jñeyā vidyāyāstārikākṛteḥ |
anutārādayo vidyā itīdaṃ tārikāmayam || 42 ||

tāmimāṃ tārikāṃ vidyāṃ bhajamāno yathāvidhi |
aihikāmuṣmikān bhogānakṣayān [F: lokān-] pratipadyate || 43 ||

43. sapta vidyāḥ - tāraḥ tārikā anutārikā vāgbhavaḥ kāmabījaṃ 
sārasvatabījaṃ mahālakṣmībījaṃ ceti |

iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre [ā: śrīpañcarātra; B, F: śrīpāñcarātre] 
lakṣmītantre saptavidyāprakāśo nāma ṣaḍviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ || 26 ||
p. 91)

saptaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ 

śakraḥ - 

namastubhyaṃ jagannāthe puṇḍarīkākṣavallabhe |
aśeṣajagadīśāne sarvajñe sarvabhāvinī || 1 ||

śrutametanmayā samyagvidyānāṃ [B, F: vijñānaṃ] tattvamuttamam 
|
bhūyaśca tārikāyā me vidhiṃ vyākhyātumarhasi || 2 ||

śrīḥ [ī: śrīruvāca] -

ādyamekaṃ paraṃ brahma sarvajñaṃ saccidātmakam |
svaśakticaritaṃ [ī: svaśaktyā churitaṃ] divyaṃ lakṣmīnārāyaṇaṃ 
mahaḥ || 3 ||

ahaṃ sā [ī: sāhaṃtā] paramā śaktirahaṃtākhyā sanātanī |
taddharmadharmiṇī nityā prabhā bhānorivāmalā || 4 ||

tadīyāni vidhīyante pañca kṛtyāni sarvadā |
tadunmeṣasvarūpiṇyā mayaivāditinandana || 5 ||

mama divyā parā śaktirnityaṃ [B, F, ī: nityā] 
maddharmadharmiṇī |
hṛllekhā paramā vidyā matsvarūpā [ā, ḍ, ī: matsvarūpam] 
puraṃdara || 6 ||

6. hṛllekhā pañcaviṃśādhyāye nirdiṣṭatārikāparanāmā hrīṃmantraḥ | 
atrāpyanupadameva | mantrasvarūpaṃ nirdekṣyate |

asyā vyākhyāmimāṃ śaśvat sāvadhānena cetasā |
śraddadhānaḥ prapannastvamupasanno gṛhāṇa me || 7 ||

āmananti yamātmānaṃ jagatastasthuṣaḥ param |
prasavasthitisaṃhārakāraṇaṃ [F: prabhava-] sūryasaṃjñitam 
[B, c, F: saṃjñakam] || 8 ||

nityaṃ [B, ḍ: nitya] prerayitāraṃ ca prāṇasaṃjñaṃ sanātanam |
taṃ viddhi prathamaṃ varṇaṃ hakāraṃ puruṣottamam || 9 ||

yastasya prathamonmeṣaḥ kroḍīkṛtajagattrayaḥ [ṅ: jagatkriyaḥ] |
aśeṣabhuvanādhāro jvaladrūpo'mito'nyataḥ || 10 ||

rephaṃ tatparamaṃ viddhi tejorūpaṃ sanātanam |
unmeṣa eva samyak sa [B, ḍ: saṃ] vyāpnuvan sakalāṃ gatim || 11 ||

vyāpārān pañca bibhracca bindūn sṛṣṭyādilakṣaṇān |
āścaryajñānarūpaśca nimeṣonmeṣasaṃtateḥ || 12 ||

icchājñānakriyārūpaṃ bibhracca vitatikramam [B: viditakramaḥ; ī: vitatiṃ 
kramāt] |
i-īrūpasya yugmasya sthitireṣā sanātanī || 13 ||

tadevaṃ [c: tadeva] paramonmeṣarūpāhaṃ vitatodayā |
icchājñānakriyārūpā pañcakṛtyakarī vibhoḥ || 14 ||

nānāvidhāścaryamayī ciddhanā sukharūpiṇī |
vijñeyā paramātmasthā vyāpinī viṣṇuvallabhā || 15 ||

vidhāya kṛtyamakhilaṃ trailokyaiśvaryadāyinī |
tasminneva punardeve vyomeśe paramātmani || 16 ||

p. 92)

ādāya sarvasaṃbhāraṃ pratitiṣṭhāmi niṣkalā |
asyā rūpāṇi pañceha tattvajñāḥ saṃpracakṣate || 17 ||

tāni rūpāṇi deveśa gadantyā me niśāmaya |
vyomeśāntamidaṃ rūpamekaṃ yattatprakīrtitam || 18 ||

18. vyomeśāntamiti | bindvantamityarthaḥ |

vyomeśāt parataḥ kecidvāñchanti parameśvaram |
vyomeśamapahāyānye pradhānaṃ viniyojya tu || 19 ||

19. parameśvaraḥ visargaḥ | pradhāno makāraḥ |

bindunādau ca vāñchanti tadante praṇavopamam |
ante pradhānamevaikaṃ keciddhīrāḥ pracakṣate || 20 ||

sṛṣṭikartāramante'nye vaidikāḥ samadhīyate |
evaṃ pañca svarūpāṇi tārikāyā vidurbudhāḥ || 21 ||

21. sṛṣṭikartā visargaḥ |

śāntasthāyāḥ sureśāna rūpāṇyevaṃvidhānyapi |
pradhānānte visṛṣṭyante vyomeśānte tathaiva ca || 22 ||

22. mantrāṇāmeṣāṃ phalatāratamyamāha - pradhānānte ityādinā |

vyomeśordhvavisṛṣṭyanta iti rūpacatuṣṭaye |
aihikī paramā siddhistattaccāmutrikī [ḍ: tatra cāmuṣmikī; ī: tatra cāmutrikī] 
parā || 23 ||

pradhānabindunādānte mokṣaśrīreva kevalā |
ityevamanusaṃdhāya tārikāyāḥ parāṃ sthitim || 24 ||

śiṣyāya sādhuśīlāya gurubrahmahitaiṣiṇe |
ācārya ādiśedvidyāṃ parabrahmasvarūpiṇīm || 25 ||

hastadehāṅgavinyāsaṃ vidhāyātmani vai purā |
vinyasya śiṣyadehe ca tataścopadiśenmanum || 26 ||

sthāpayeddhṛdi śiṣyasya bhāvapūrvaṃ manuṃ param |
punaśca sthāpayet svasya [B, F, ī: svasmin] hṛdaye 
mantramuttamam || 27 ||

dīkṣābhiṣekapūrvaṃ ca sarvametat samācaret |
ācāryādatha saṃprāpya vidyāṃ śiṣyo vicakṣaṇaḥ || 28 ||

ātmānamātmanaścaiva vittaṃ dattvā tu dakṣiṇām |
sakalaṃ tvardhamaṃśaṃ vā yena vā toṣyate guruḥ || 29 ||

vaidike ca samācāre [B, F, ī: samayācāre] laukike ca vyavasthite |
apramādyan sadācārye [ḍ: sadācāre] guruṣu brāhmaṇeṣu ca || 30 ||

adrohaṃ śīlayan śaśvadbhūtagrāme caturvidhe |
nityamātmaguṇopeto dharmalakṣaṇasevakaḥ || 31 ||

saṃskāraiḥ saṃskṛtaḥ śubhrairdevapitratithikriyaḥ |
divyaśāstrāṇyadhīyāno nigamāṃścaiva vaidikān || 32 ||

alolupena cittena siddhāntānanusaṃcaran |
yāvadarthaṃ tu vijñānamādadānastatastataḥ || 33 ||

adūṣayaṃśca śāstrāṇi pramāṇairanusaṃcaran |
āhnikaṃ vidhivat kurvan śāstrārthaṃ karmaṇāṃ kramaiḥ || 34 ||

aharādi tvahorātraṃ punā rātryavasānakam |
abandhyaṃ [B, F: avaśyaṃ] satataṃ kurvaṃścoditaiḥ karmaṇāṃ 
kramaiḥ || 35 ||

pañcakālarato [ḍ, E: paro] nityaṃ pañcayajñaparāyaṇaḥ |
bhūṣito damadānābhyāṃ satyenāhiṃsanena ca || 36 ||

coditena krameṇemāṃ vidyāṃ saṃsādhya yatnataḥ |
prasannayā [B: prapannayā] dhiyā yukto nityaṃ svārthaparārthayoḥ || 37 ||

bhūtimeva parāmicchan vibhūtiṃ [ī: abhūtiṃ] parivarjayet |
pāvanaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ manasā cakṣuṣā girā || 38 ||

p. 93)

cittaprasādanīrdevīścatasraḥ pariśīlayan |
maitryādyāḥ śāntimanvicchan japayajñaparāyaṇaḥ || 39 ||

apramādyan svakarmasthaḥ pramāde sati daivataḥ |
prāyaścittaṃ caran samyagyasminyasmiṃstu [B: yasmiṃśca] yādṛśam 
|| 40 ||

karmaṇā manasā vācā devadevaṃ janārdanam |
prapannaḥ śaraṇaṃ śaśvanmāṃ ca taddharmadharmiṇīm || 41 ||

uttamaṃ puruṣaṃ strīṃ ca saṃdṛṣṭvā māmanusmaran |
daṃpatī pūjayannityaṃ [ā, B, c, ī: pūjayet-] 
dāṃpatyaṃ cāpyalopayan || 42 ||

śabdasthamarthagaṃ vāpi puṃbhāvaṃ vividhātmakam |
strībhāvaṃ viddhi tadrūpaṃ lakṣmīnārāyaṇaṃ smaran [B: smaret] || 43 ||

uttamāṃ guṇasaṃpannāṃ rūpayauvanaśālinīm |
alolupena cittena dṛṣṭvā māmeva cintayan || 44 ||

44. alolupeneti | atyāsaktirahitenetyarthaḥ |

strīṣu kṣāntamanā nityamavadannapriyaṃ sadā |
atikramaṃ [ā, B, ḍ: atikāmaṃ] pariharan saṃjātaṃ 
cāpyatarkayan [ā, B: akīrtayan] || 45 ||

45. atikramaḥ pramādaḥ |

kṣālayan pāvanaiḥ strīṇāṃ jāyamānaṃ vyatikramam |
kubjāṃ vā vikalāṃ vāpi sarvāvasthāṃ gatāṃ striyam || 46 ||

46. pāvanairiti | śuddhikarmabhirityarthaḥ | vyatikramaḥ pramādaḥ |

avinindaṃścaraṃstrīṇāṃ priyaṃ śāstrānukūlataḥ |
evaṃvṛttaḥ sadācāro naro vigatakalmaṣaḥ || 47 ||

[F: omits three lines from here] madbhakto matpriyakaro madyājī 
matparāyaṇaḥ |
prāpnoti paramaṃ dhāma tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam || 48 ||

iti te kathitā śakra tārikāyāḥ parā sthitiḥ |
vidyānāmapi cānyāsāṃ kiṃ bhūyaḥ śrotumicchasi || 49 ||

49. tārikāyā iti | hṛllekhāparanāmnyā ityarthaḥ |

iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre [ā: śrīpañcarātra; B, ḍ: śrīpāñcarātre] 
lakṣmītantre sadācāraprakāśo nāma saptaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ || 27 ||
p. 94)

aṣṭāviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ 

śakraḥ -

namaste kamalāvāse namastrayyantavāsini |
tvatprasādena vidhivacchruto mantraḥ samādhinā || 1 ||

pratipattiśca sakalā svarūpaṃ ca yathāsthitam |
āhorātrikamācāramidānīṃ vaktumarhasi || 2 ||

śrīḥ -

eko nārāyaṇaḥ śrīmānanādiḥ puṣkarekṣaṇaḥ |
jñānaiśvaryamahāśaktivīryatejomahodadhiḥ || 3 ||

ātmā sa sarvabhūtānāṃ haṃso nārāyaṇo vaśī [c: hariḥ] |
tasya sāmarthyarūpāhamekā taddharmadharmiṇī || 4 ||

sāhaṃ sṛṣṭyādikān bhāvān vidadhānā punaḥ punaḥ |
ārādhitā satī sarvāṃstārayāmi bhavārṇavāt || 5 ||

dadāmi vividhān bhogān dharmeṇa paritoṣitā |
saddharmaparasaṃsthānā [ḍ: paramastasyāḥ; ī: paramasthānā] mama 
sattvādikā tanuḥ || 6 ||

6. vividhāniti | apavargapradāpyānuṣaṅgikānaihikānāmutrikāṃśca bhogān 
dadāmītyarthaḥ | saddharmapareṣu saṃsthānaṃ yasyā iti bahuvrīhiḥ |

ācārarūpo dharmo'sāvācārastasya lakṣaṇam [c: rakṣaṇam] |
tamācāraṃ pravakṣyāmi yaḥ sadbhiranupālyate || 7 ||

hitvā yogamayīṃ nidrāmutthāyāpararātrataḥ |
prapadyeta hṛṣīkeśaṃ śaraṇyaṃ śrīpatiṃ harim || 8 ||

8. yogamayīmiti | abhigamanādiṣu dharmeṣu yogākhyaḥ pañcamo dharmaḥ 
pūrvadinarātrimārabhya paradinabrāhmamuhūrtāvadhikakālānuṣṭheyaḥ |

prapatteśca svarūpaṃ te pūrvamuktaṃ sureśvara |
bhūyaśca śṛṇu vakṣyāmi sā yathā syāt sthirā tvayi [B: bhuvi] || 9 ||

ācamya prayato bhūtvā smṛtvāstraṃ jvalanākṛti |
tat praviśya viniṣkrāntaḥ pūto bhūtvāstratejasā || 10 ||

prapattiṃ tāṃ prayuñjīta svāṅgaiḥ pañcabhiranvitām |
prātikūlyaṃ parityaktamānukūlyaṃ ca saṃśritam || 11 ||

mayā sarveṣu bhūteṣu yathāśakti yathāmati |
alasasyālpaśakteśca yathāvaccāvijānataḥ || 12 ||

upāyāḥ kriyamāṇāste naiva syustārakā mama |
ato'haṃ kṛpaṇo dīno nirlepaścāpyakiṃcanaḥ || 13 ||

lakṣmyā saha hṛṣīkeśo devyā kāruṇyarūpayā |
rakṣakaḥ sarvasiddhānte [B, ṅ: sarvabhūtānāṃ] vedānte'pi ca gīyate || 
14 ||

14. mahāviśvāso'tra vivakṣitaḥ | sarvasiddhānte sarveṣu divyaśāstreṣu | vedānte 
upaniṣatsu |

yanme'sti dustyajaṃ kiṃcit putradārakriyādikam |
samastamātmanā [ā, B, c, ṅ: samantāt-] nyastaṃ 
śrīpate tava pādayoḥ || 15 ||

15. ātmātmīyanikṣepamāha - yanme'stītyādinā | ātmanā ātmanā sahetyarthaḥ |

p. 95)

śaraṇaṃ bhava deveśa nātha lakṣmīpate mama |
sakṛdevaṃ prapannasya kṛtyaṃ naivānyadiṣyate || 16 ||

16. goptṛtvavaraṇamāha - śaraṇamityādinā |

upāyāpāyamuktasya vartamānasya madhyataḥ |
narasya buddhidaurbalyādupāyāntaramiṣyate || 17 ||

17. madhyato vartamānasya naivānyat kṛtyamiti pūrveṇānvayaḥ | 
buddhidaurbalyāditi | svādhikāramanālocya duṣkare karmaṇi pravṛttirhi 
buddhidaurbalyaprayukteti bhāvaḥ | upāyāntaramatra bhaktiyogaḥ | anena 
bhaktiyogasya nindā kriyata iti vā mumukṣorbhaktiyogaḥ svarūpaviruddha iti vā na 
mantavyam | narasya buddhidaurbalyādindubimbagrahe spṛhā ityukte 
vacanamidamindubimbaṃ nindatīti na kaścidapi prekṣāvān brūyāt | kiṃca bhaktyā 
paramayā vāpi prapattyā vā mahāmate iti bhaktervaikalpikasādhanatvoktirvirudhyeta 
| aśaktasya mumukṣoḥ svarupaviruddha iti cet nātra kasyāpi vipratipattiḥ |

ataḥ paraṃ sadācāraṃ procyamānaṃ nibodha me |
āśaṃsānaḥ samuttiṣṭhet sarvabhūtasukhodayam || 18 ||

bhavantu sarvabhūtāni sāttvike vimale pathi |
bhajantāṃ śrīpatiṃ śaśvadviśantu paramaṃ padam || 19 ||

ityāśāsya priyaṃ samyagbhūtebhyo manasā girā |
śarīraśodhanaṃ kṛtvā dharmaśāstravidhānataḥ [F: viśāradaḥ] || 20 ||

śaucaṃ ca vidhivat kṛtvā bhakṣayeddantadhāvanam |
athācamya vidhānena pavitraiḥ śāstracoditaiḥ [B, ḍ: bodhitaiḥ] || 21 ||

plāvayitvābhyupāsīta saṃdhyāṃ trailokyapāvanīm |
manmayī trividhā śaktiḥ sūryasomāgnirūpiṇī || 22 ||

śuddhaye sarvabhūtānāṃ saṃdhyā devī pravartate |
upasthāya vivasvantamantaḥsthaṃ puruṣottamam [B, ī: antaḥstha] || 23 
||

23. vivasvantaṃ viśiṣṭadīptimantamiti puruṣottamaviśeṣaṇam | antaḥstham 
sūryamaṇḍalāntarvartinam | arkamaṇḍalamadhyasthaṃ sūryakoṭisamaprabham 
ityādidhyānamatrābhipretam |

kuryādagnividhiṃ samyagupādānamathācaret [B, ḍ: dānaṃ 
samācaret] |
sati vitte na kurvītopādānaṃ tu vicakṣaṇaḥ || 24 ||

24. upādānaṃ nāma bhagavadārādhanopayuktadravyārjanam | tacca niḥsvasya 
pañcayajñapañcakālaparāyaṇabhāgavatottamādayācitopanatataṇḍulādisvī##-

sapta vittāgamā dharmyā dāyo lābhaḥ krayo jayaḥ |
prayogaḥ karmayogaśca satpratigraha eva ca || 25 ||

snānaṃ kṛtvā vidhānena trividhaṃ śāstracoditam |
bhūtaśuddhiṃ vidhāyātha yāgamāntaramācaret || 26 ||

svayamutpāditaiḥ sphītairlabdhaiḥ [ā: krītaiḥ; B, c: anyaiḥ] 
śiṣyāditastathā [B, F: śiṣyādinā tathā] |
bhogairyajet māṃ viṣṇumubhau vā śāstrapūrvakam || 27 ||

aṣṭāṅgena vidhānena hyanuyāgāvasānakaiḥ |
svādhyāyamācaret samyagaparāhne vicakṣaṇaḥ || 28 ||

28. aṣṭāṅgayajanaṃ yathā jayākhye - antaryāgaḥ bhogayāgaḥ 
madhvādiyāgaḥ annayāgaḥ saṃpradānam vahnisaṃtarpaṇam pitṛyāgaḥ 
anuyāgaśceti (22 a0 75-80) |

divyaśāstrāṇyadhīyīta nigamāṃścaiva vaidikān |
sarvānanucaret samyak siddhāntānātmasiddhaye || 29 || 

29. siddhāntāniti | mantrāgamādi siddhāntabhedānityarthaḥ |

p. 96)

alolupena cittena rāgadveṣavivarjitaḥ |
na nindenmanasā vācā śāstrāṇyuccāvacānyapi || 30 ||

tāvanmātrārthamādadyād yāvatā hyartha ātmanaḥ |
bhūtānāṃ śreyase sarve sarvaśāstrāṇi tanvate || 31 ||

tāṃ tāmavasthāṃ saṃprāpya tāni śreyo vitanvate |
ādau madhye ca sarveṣāṃ śāstrāṇāmantime tathā || 32 ||

śrīmānnārāyaṇaḥ [ā, B, c, ṅ: śrīmannārāyaṇaḥ] prokto 
vidhayaiva tayā tayā |
ahaṃ nārāyaṇasthāpi sarvajñā sarvadarśinī || 33 ||

33. sarvaśāstrāṇāṃ bhūtahitatvaṃ paratatvaprakāśanena 
tadārādhanapratipādanena cetyabhisaṃdhāyāha - śrīmāniti |

nidānajñā miṣakkalpā tattadgurvādirūpiṇī |
pravartayāmi śāstrāṇi tāni tāni tathā tathā || 34 ||

adhikārānurūpeṇa pramāṇāni tathā tathā |
atyantaheyaṃ na kvāpi śāstraṃ kiṃcana vidyate || 35 ||

sarvatra sulabhaṃ śreyaḥ svalpaṃ vā yadi vā bahu |
tataḥ kāryo na vidveṣo yāvadarthamupāśrayet || 36 ||

samayaṃ na viśettatra naiva dīkṣāṃ kadācana |
tataḥ saṃdhyāmupāsīta paścimāṃ sārdhabhāskarām || 37 ||

vidhāyāgnyarthakāryaṃ tu yogaṃ yuñjīta vai tataḥ |
suvivikte śucau deśe niḥśalāke manorame || 38 ||

mṛdvāstaraṇasaṃkīrṇe celājinakuśottare |
antarbahiśca saṃśuddhe yamādipariśodhitaḥ || 39 ||

āsanaṃ cakramāsthāya padmaṃ svastikameva vā |
yatra vā ramate buddhirnāḍīmārgān nipīḍayan [ī: apīḍayan] || 40 ||

vijitya pavanagrāmaṃ pratyāhārajitendriyaḥ |
dhāraṇāsu śramaṃ kṛtvā māṃ dhyāyet susamāhitaḥ || 41 ||

anaupamyāmanirdeśyāmavikalpāṃ nirañjanām |
sarvatra sulabhāṃ lakṣmīṃ sarvapratyayatāṃ [B, ḍ: pratyakṣatāṃ] 
gatām || 42 ||

sākārāmathavā yogī varābhayakarāṃ parām |
padmagarbhopamāṃ padmāṃ padmahastāṃ sulakṣaṇām || 43 ||

yadvā nārāyaṇāṅkasthāṃ sāmarasyamupāgatām |
cidānandamayīṃ devīṃ tādṛśaṃ ca śriyaḥ patim || 44 ||

bahudhā yogamārgāste veditavyāḥ sureśvara |
teṣvekaṃ dharmamāsthāya bhaktiḥ śraddhā ca yatra te || 45 ||

samyaṅ nidhyānamutpādya samādhiṃ samupāśrayet |
dhyātā dhyānaṃ tathā dhyeyaṃ trayaṃ yatra vilīyate || 46 ||

46. yogasya parā kāṣṭhocyate - dhyātetyādinā | yatreti | yamyāṃ 
samādhidaśāyāmityarthaḥ |

ekaivāhaṃ tadā bhāse pūrṇāhaṃtā sanātanī |
aikadhyamanusaṃprāpte [ā, B, c: aikārthyaṃ-] mayi 
saṃvinmahodadhau || 47 ||

47. aikadhyamekabhāvaṃ yogini saṃprāpte sati nānyat kiṃcit prakāśate | kiṃtu 
ahameva bhāsa ityuttareṇānvayaḥ |

nānyat prakāśate kiṃcidahameva tadā parā |
yogācchrānto japaṃ kuryāttacchrānto yogamācaret || 48 ||

tasya kṣipraṃ prasīdāmi japayogābhiyoginaḥ |
nītvaivaṃ prathamaṃ yāmaṃ japayogādinā sudhīḥ || 49 ||

49. yoganidrāṃ nirvakti - nītvaivamiti |

yogastha eva taddhīrastato yāmadvayaṃ svapet |
utthāyāpararātre tu pūrvoktamanusaṃcaret || 50 ||

50. yogo nāma paramātmanyātmanikṣepapuraḥsaraṃ 
paramātmaguṇagaṇānusaṃdhānam | tatkurvāṇa eva svāpamanubhavedityarthaḥ |

iti vyāmiśrakṛtyaṃ tat proktaṃ te balasūdana |
acchidrān pañcakālāṃstu bhagavatkarmaṇā nayet || 51 ||

51. vyāmiśreti | bahuvidhakarmakalāpasahitaṃ bhagavadārādhanamityarthaḥ | 
acchidrāniti | yanmuhūrtaṃ kṣaṇaṃ vāpi vāsudevo na cintyate | sā 
hānistanmahacchidraṃ sā bhrāntiḥ sā ca vikriyā || (ga0 pu0 pū0 222##-

p. 97)

dīkṣitaḥ pañcakālajño lakṣmīmantraparāyaṇaḥ |
antaraṃ nānayoḥ kiṃcinniṣṭhāyāṃ balasūdanaḥ || 52 ||

ubhāvetau matau bhaktau viśato māṃ tanukṣaye |
cakrapadmadharo nityaṃ bhavellakṣmīparāyaṇaḥ || 53 ||

svadāranirataśca syād brahmacārī sadā bhavet |
manmantramabhyasennityaṃ maccitto matparāyaṇaḥ || 54 ||

sarvānuccāvacāñchabdāṃstadbhāvena vibhāvayet |
agnīṣomavibhāgajñaḥ kriyābhūtivibhāgavit || 55 ||

sthūlasūkṣmaparatvānāṃ veditā ca yathārthataḥ |
aṅgopāṅgāditantrajño mudrābhedavidhānavit || 56 ||

antaryāgabahiryāgajapahomavicakṣaṇaḥ |
puraścaraṇabhedajñaḥ siddhisādhanatattvavit [B, c, ḍ: siddha##-

[ā: yajñamūrti; B: omits this line] 
saṃjñāmūrtividhānajñastatsādhanavidhānavit |

evaṃ prakāraḥ śāstrarthavedī dhīro vicakṣaṇaḥ |
ahiṃsro damadānastho māṃ bhajet śriyaṃ naraḥ || 59 ||

iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre [ā: śrīpañcarātra; B: śrīpāñcarātre]

ekonatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

śakraḥ -

śuddhāśuddhaṣaḍadhvākhyacitranirmāṇabhittaye |
namaḥ śrīvatsabhāminyai bhavasaṃtāpaśāntaye || 1 ||

agnīṣomavibhāgaṃ me kriyābhūtividhāmapi |
brūhi me tattvataḥ padme vidyāyāstārikākṛteḥ || 2 ||

śrīḥ -

vyāpakaṃ yat paraṃ brahma śaktirnārāyaṇī hi yā |
sā hyahaṃ pariṇāmena bhavāmi praṇavākṛtiḥ || 3 ||

agniṣomavibhāgaṃ me tārikāyā niśāmaya |
kriyābhūtividhānaṃ ca yathāvat suranandana || 4 ||

unmeṣaḥ paramo viṣṇorādyaḥ ṣāḍguṇyacinmayaḥ |
sāhaṃ saṃbhṛtasaṃbhārā śaktiste kathitā purā || 5 ||

sṛṣṭavatyā jagat kṛtsnaṃ tasyā me rakṣaṇodyame |
dvidhā pravartate rūpaṃ [ā, ṅ: kiṃcittejomukhaṃ matam; B: omits four 
quarters from here] mukhenaiśvaryatejasoḥ || 6 ||

6. mukheneti prādhānyenetyarthaḥ |

[ā: omits this line] ṣāḍguṇyameva me rūpaṃ kiṃcittejomukhaṃ matam |
ṣāḍguṇyameva me rūpaṃ paramaiśvaryasaṃmukham || 7 ||

tejomukhaṃ tu yadrūpaṃ sā kriyāśaktirucyate |
saivāgnirucyate śaktiḥ sarvopaplavadāhanāt [c: dāhataḥ] || 8 ||

aiśvaryasaṃmukhaṃ rūpaṃ bhūtirlakṣmīritīritā |
śaktiraiśvaryabhūyiṣṭhā sā me somamayī tanuḥ || 9 ||

śoṣaṇāt [c, ī: ploṣaṇāt] sarvadoṣāṇāmagniśaktiḥ kriyāmayī |
jagadāpyāyayantyanyā bhūtiḥ soma ihocyate || 10 ||

icchājñānakriyāmayyāste ime vyūhaje mama |
ṣāḍguṇyavigrahā sāhaṃ vyūhinī parameśvarī || 11 ||

yā sā śaktiḥ kriyākhyā [ā, c, ṅ: kriyānyā; ī: kriyāgnyākhyā] 
me ṣāḍguṇyaṃ tejasojjvalam |
tasyā āsaṃstrayo vyūhāḥ sūryasomāgniśaktayaḥ || 12 ||

tāsāmādyā parā [B, c, F: tāsāṃ me paramā] divyā sūryākhyā 
śaktirujjvalā |
nirvahantī jagatkṛtyamaniśaṃ [ā, B, F: īdṛśaṃ] parivartate || 
13 ||

adhyātmamadhidaivaṃ ca tathā caivādhibhautikam |
tasyā rūpatrayaṃ viddhi sūryākhyāyāḥ sureśvara || 14 ||

adhyātmasthā tu sūryākhyā piṅgalāmārgagāminī |
ālokenādhibhūtasthā sūryaśaktiḥ pravartate || 15 ||

sūryamaṇḍalasaṃsthānā śaktiḥ sānyādhidaivikī |
sūryamaṇḍalasaṃsthānā arciṣo yāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 16 ||

16. sūryamaṇḍalāntarvartino devasya trayīrūpatvaṃ vaktuṃ prathamatastasya

ṛcastā viddhi deveśa tapantīstapanātmikāḥ |
dīptayo yāstadantaḥsthāstāni sāmāni viddhi me || 17 ||

17. tasya yajuḥsvarūpatvamāha - antaḥstheti | anena ādityo vā eṣa

p. 99)

antaḥsthā yā parā śaktiḥ pauruṣīṃ tanumāsthitā |

20. antaḥsthasyāsya puruṣasya daśahotā mūrdhā bhavati |

21. agnirhotā ityārabhya tṛtīyānuvākapaṭhitaḥ pañcahotā | sūryaṃ te cakṣuḥ

śobhāstu dakṣiṇāstasya saṃbhārāḥ saṃdhayaḥ smṛtāḥ || 22 ||

22. mahāhavirhotā ityārabhya pañcamānuvākapaṭhitaḥ saptahotā | devasya tvā

23. senendrasya ityārabhya navamānuvākapaṭhitā devapatnyaḥ | suvarṇaṃ gharmam

24. namaste rudra manyave ityārabhya paṭhitā rudriyāḥ | evaṃ śukriyā api

25. yajurmayeti sāvitrī | anuvyāhṛti vyāhṛtibhyaḥ paścāt | asya abhyasyan iti

26. trayīti | vedairaśūnyastribhireti sūryaḥ iti śrutiranusaṃdheyā |

28. tadāditi | tat ityādayo varṇā ityarthaḥ | śiraḥ gāyatrīśiromantraḥ | kṣityādīti |

p. 100)

anulomavilomābhyāṃ saumyāgneyī nigadyate |

31. ādāyeti | atra yābhirādityastapati tābhiḥ parjanyo varṣati iti śrutiranusaṃdheyā |

punarmuñcati megheṣu [ā, B: navamāsahṛtaṃ; c: omits eight quarters

33. abindhanā vidyut divyā | kāṣṭhādindhanā bhaumī | jaṭharasthā kauṣṭhīti

36. somamayyapi tridhā - ākāśe bimbātmanā bhūmāvoṣadhyātmanā

p. 101)

56, 57. somaḥ sakāraḥ | prāṇo hakāraḥ | amṛtaṃ sakāraḥ | kālapāvakaḥ

58. saptavarṇātmakamiti | somaprāṇāmṛtakālapāvakānalamāyāvyāpi##-
sahasrāra iti piṇḍamantraḥ |

60. smarye smaraṇīye ityarthaḥ |

yo'sau piṇḍordhvabhāgastho varṇaḥ kālānalābhidhaḥ |

varmāstrāntaṃ dhruvādyaṃ ca saṃjñāmantratvamṛcchati |
vyāpakau yojayedante piṇḍādyoḥ somasūryayoḥ || 64 ||

64. pūrvoktameva viśadayati - vyāpakāviti |

ante somāgnikūṭasya hyekaṃ vyāpakamānayet |
ādyantayostathāntyasya varṇasya vyāpakau smṛtau || 65 ||

66. varma kavacam | prāṇorjayorvyoma huṃmantraḥ |

sa phaṭkārastadante cāpyāhlādaḥ śāntikārakaḥ |
[ā, c, ḍ, F: add here lines 2 to 14 (tasyā vyāptiṃ to

67. vikarṣaḥ anusyūtiḥ |

p. 102)

svavarṇairaṅgavāneṣa valī saudarśano manuḥ |
divyāntarikṣabhaumānāṃ bhogānāmāptisādhanam || 68 ||
